# Harinama-cintamani

## Introduction

This book is not an ordinary text. Only those in whom firm faith in Śrī Caitanya has appeared, who have taken shelter of the Holy Name and have faith in bhakti are qualified to understand this book. Amongst various types of sādhana-bhakti, all perfection is attained by taking shelter of the Name and those who believe this are the best of sādhakas. These instructions of Śrīman Mahāprabhu are found in His Śikṣāṣṭaka. Śrī Mahāprabhu appointed Hari Dāsa Ṭhākura as the ācārya of these teachings.

Authoritative books affirm that Śrīla Hari Dāsa Ṭhākura took birth in a yavana household, and we know this for certain. Hari Dāsa accepted his birth in a rural village named Buḍhana. After some time, due to his previous saṁskāra (deeds performed in a previous life), he eventually became attracted to hari-bhajana. Leaving home, he built a small kuṭīra (cottage) in the forest of Benāpula, and lived there, constantly engaged in nāma-saṅkīrtana and smaraṇa. Because some inimical people were opposed to him, he left that place and came to live on the banks of the Gaṅgā.

Some evil men sent a prostitute in order to corrupt him, yet by hearing hari-nāma from the mouth of Hari Dāsa, that prostitute eventually attained some sukṛti and became a devotee. Offering his kuṭīra in Benāpula to this new devotee, Hari Dāsa left that province. Continuously singing hari-nāma, he crossed the Gaṅgā and came to Saptagrāma where he stayed in the house of Śrīla Yadunandana Ācārya. In that village, and in the association of the Ācārya, he attended meetings hosted by Śrī Hiraṇya and Govardhana who were landowners with the title Majumdar. He had many debates on the glories of Śrī Nāma with a brahma-bandhu (so-called brāhmaṇa) named Gopāla Cakravartī. Hiraṇya and Govardhana dismissed that brāhmaṇa from their employment and later, due to vaiṣṇava-aparādha, he developed leprosy. At that time, Govardhana’s son, Raghunātha Dāsa, who was very young, became attracted to the path of Vaiṣṇavism by the mercy of Hari Dāsa. Hearing of Gopāla Cakravartī’s suffering, Hari Dāsa felt sad and left that place, took shelter of Śrīmad Advaita Prabhu in Phuliyāgrāma, and performed nirjana-hari-bhajana (solitary worship of Hari) in a cave on the bank of the Gaṅgā. Although a devotee may dislike fame and give up the association of worldly people, the glories of a devotee can never be hidden. As the light of his bhakti spread, the jealousy of the Muslims arose and he was seized by them and especially tortured by the muluk-pati (leader of the Muslims). Hari Dāsa was filled with mercy for all living beings. Without considering their faults, he gave blessings to everyone, left that place and returned to his cave. A few days later Mahāprabhu came to that holy place. In the company of Śrī Advaita, Hari Dāsa met Śrīman Mahāprabhu and sought refuge at His feet. From that time on, he was appointed by Mahāprabhu as the ācārya of nāma-pracāra (propagating the Holy Name). Later on, Mahāprabhu relocated to Śrī Puruṣottama (Jagannātha Purī) and at that time Hari Dāsa stayed at Siddha Bakula. When Hari Dāsa left this world, the Lord Himself placed him in samādhi at the seashore and during the ceremony performed saṅkīrtana and ended it with a viraha-mahotsava (observance of separation).

This is Śrīman Mahāprabhu’s līlā, and those devotees who became elevated in bhakti preached His teachings to the world. After asking Hari Dāsa some questions, the Lord revealed nāma-tattva (the intrinsic truth about the Holy Name) from his mouth. All these things are described in many places in Śrī Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Śrī Caitanya-bhāgavata, and in many other books describing bhakti. Being encouraged by devotees at various times, I have compiled an anthology from all the books describing Śrī Hari Dāsa’s preaching about nāma-tattva. I also received some books about our Hari Dāsa from devotees living far away. Seeing amongst them writings of the sahajiyās, bāulas, and other incoherent statements, I carefully discarded these. One or two books agreed with pure Vaiṣṇava philosophy. In one of the texts, a rasika meaning of the sixteen Holy Names of thirty-two syllables was found. I was very happy to find this. It seems that Śrī Hari Dāsa instructed a pure devotee about the Holy Name, and he wrote this book in the name of his Gurudeva. After seeing these books, I would like to profusely thank the devotee community of the Śrīhaṭṭa province. In all these books I found Hari Dāsa’s instructions in relation to hari-nāma and they are all compiled in this book, Hari-nāma Cintāmaṇi. To increase the happiness of the niṣkiñcana-bhaktas (those devotees who have renounced everything for Kṛṣṇa), we have published this book. We think that those who are not niṣkiñcanas who are exclusively devoted to the Holy Name should not read this book, and we do not wish to hear their arguments about this topic.

There are many methods of sādhana-bhajana, but the method of bhajana where exclusive shelter of the Name is taken is of one type only. From the time of Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu, the great devotees have followed this bhajana-praṇālī (method of worship), as spoken by Śrī Hari Dāsa. Since ancient times, the Vaiṣṇavas residing in the forests of Vraja have all followed this process of bhajana, and sometime later, we saw with our own eyes how all the bhajanānandī Vaiṣṇavas of Śrī Puruṣottama-kṣetra also followed it. At the end of Śrī Hari-bhakti-vilāsa, Śrī Sanātana and Śrī Gopāla Bhaṭṭa Gosvāmīs have clearly written about this exclusive bhajana-praṇālī, consisting of living in a solitary place and engaging in constant śravaṇa, kīrtana and smaraṇa of śrī-hari-nāma without offence.

This Śrī Hari-nāma Cintāmaṇi is a book written in the payāra metre, and women, children and those inexperienced in Sanskrit can all grasp Mahāprabhu’s teachings from reading it. It is for this reason that I have not included any Sanskrit quotes, which might trouble them. There is another anthology called Pramāṇa-mālā which contains śāstrika evidence for every sentence of this Hari-nāma Cintāmaṇi. If Śrī Kṛṣṇa desires, this book will also be published for the devotees very soon.

A servant of the akiñcanas –

Śrī Bhaktivinoda

## Chapter 1. Śrī Nāma Māhātmya Sūcanā (An Introduction to the Glories of the Holy Name)

śrī-godruma-candrāya namaḥ  
(Obeisance unto the Moon of Godruma)

### Text 1

*gadāi-gaurāṅga jaya jāhnavā-jīvana*  
*sītādvaita jaya śrīvāsādi bhakta-gaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**Glories to Gaura-Gadādhara, and the life of Jāhnavā (Nityānanda Prabhu). Glories to the husband of Sītā (Advaita Ācārya), to Śrīvāsa and all the devotees.**

### Text 2

*lavaṇa-jaladhi-tīre, nīlācale śrī mandire*  
*dāru-brahma puruṣa-pradhāna*  
*jīve nistārite hari, arcā-rūpe avatāri*  
*bhoga mokṣa karena pradāna*

**Перевод:**

**On the shore of the salt-water ocean, in a beautiful temple in Nīlācala, the Supreme Person descended in the form of a wooden Deity (dāru-brahma) in order to deliver the jīvas. He bestows material enjoyment and liberation.**

### Text 3

*sei dhāme śrī-caitanya, mānave karite dhanya*  
*sannyāsī rūpete bhagavān*  
*kalite ye yuga-dharma, bujhāite tā’ra marma*  
*kāśī-miśra-ghare adhiṣṭhāna*

**Перевод:**

**In this dhāma, the Supreme Lord Śrī Caitanya appeared in the form of a sannyāsī in order to bless humanity and to teach the yuga-dharma of the Kali-age while residing at the home of Kāśī Miśra.**

### Text 4

*nija bhakta-vṛnda laye, nije kalpa-taru haye*  
*kṛṣṇa-prema dena sarva-jane*  
*nānā-mate bhakta-mukhe, bhakti-kathā śuni’ sukhe*  
*jīva śikṣā dena suyatane*

**Перевод:**

**Along with His intimate associates, He became like a wish-fulfilling tree and gave everyone kṛṣṇa-prema. He happily listened to topics about bhakti from the mouths of various devotees and taught this to the jīvas with great care. (1)**

**Комментарий:**

(1) Through the mouth of Śrī Rāmānanda Rāya rasa-kathā (discussions on rasa) emanated; from the mouth of Śrī Sārvabhauma, the subject of liberation (mukti-tattva) manifested; from the mouth of Śrī Rūpa came rasa-vicāra (deliberations on rasa), and from the mouth of Śrī Hari Dāsa, śrī-nāma-māhātmya (the glories of the Holy Name) appeared.

### Text 5

*eka-dina bhagavān, samudre kariyā snāna*  
*śrī-siddha-bakule hari-dāse*  
*mili’ ānandita mane, jijñāsilā sa-yatane*  
*kise jīva tare anāyāse*

**Перевод:**

**One day, after bathing in the ocean, the Lord met Hari Dāsa at Śrī Siddha Bakula. Feeling great bliss, He asked sincerely, “How can the jīvas be easily delivered?”**

### Text 6

*prabhura caraṇa dhari’ aneka vinaya kari’*  
*galad-aśru pulaka śarīra*  
*hari-dāsa mahāśaya, kāṅdite kāṅdite kaya*  
*prabhu tava līlā su-gabhīra*

**Перевод:**

**Grasping the Lord’s feet with great humility, with moist eyes and the hairs of his body standing on end, the broad-hearted Hari Dāsa continuously wept, saying, “O Prabhu, Your pastimes are extremely deep.**

### Text 7

*āmi ati akiñcana, nāhi mora vidyā dhana*  
*tava pada āmāra sambala*  
*e hena ayogya jane, praśna kari’ akāraṇe*  
*bala prabhu habe kibā phala*

**Перевод:**

**“I am most destitute! I do not possess a wealth of knowledge. Your feet are my only treasure. Tell me, O Prabhu, if You ask this question to such an unfit person, what will be the result?**

### Text 8

*tumi kṛṣṇa svayaṁ prabho, jīva uddhārite vibho*  
*navadvīpa-dhāme avatāra*  
*kṛpā kari’ rāṅgā pāya, rākha more gaura-rāya*  
*tabe citta praphulla āmāra*

**Перевод:**

**“O Prabhu, You are directly Kṛṣṇa Himself. O Great One, in order to deliver the jīvas You have descended to Navadvīpa. Please be merciful. Please be kind. O Gaura Rāya, kindly protect me! Make my heart blossom with bliss.**

### Text 9

*tomāra ananta nāma, tavānanta guṇa-grāma*  
*tava rūpa sukhera sāgara*  
*ananta tomāra līlā, kṛpā kari’ prakāśilā*  
*tāi āsvādaye e pāmara*

**Перевод:**

**“Your Names are unlimited. Your many qualities are boundless. Your form is an ocean of bliss. Your pastimes are endless. Please show Your mercy and allow this wretch to relish them. (2)**

**Комментарий:**

(2) By Your mercy, Your spiritual name, form, qualities and pastimes have arisen within the world of matter. Please allow jīvas like myself to relish them. The material body and senses of the jīva cannot perceive Kṛṣṇa’s name, form, qualities and pastimes, which are completely śuddha-sattva (pure goodness). By Kṛṣṇa’s mercy, these transcendental realities appear in this material world to award auspiciousness to the jīvas. Because they are spiritual by nature, they are self-manifest.

### Text 10

*cinmaya bhāskara tumi, kiraṇera kaṇa āmi*  
*tumi prabhu, āmi nitya-dāsa*  
*caraṇa pīyūṣa tava, mama sukha su-vaibhava*  
*tava nāmāmṛte mora āśa*

**Перевод:**

**“You are the transcendental sun, and I am but a particle of light. You are the Master and I am an eternal servant. The nectar of Your feet is my blissful wealth. I only desire the nectar of Your Names.**

### Text 11

*e mata adhama āmi, ki balite jāni svāmī*  
*tabu ājñā kariba pālana*  
*yā’ balā’be mora mukhe, tomāre baliba sukhe*  
*doṣa-guṇa nā kari gaṇana*

**Перевод:**

**“Thus, I am very fallen. How will I know what to say, O Master? Still, I must accept Your order. Whatever words You speak through my mouth I will happily tell You. I will not consider their faults or virtues.**

**Kṛṣṇa-Tattva (The Principle Concerning Kṛṣṇa)**

### Text 12

*eka-mātra icchāmaya kṛṣṇa sarveśvara*  
*nitya śakti-yoge kṛṣṇa vibhu parātpara*

**Перевод:**

**“Kṛṣṇa is the sole Controller of everything, possessing independent desires. With His eternal potencies, Kṛṣṇa is supremely infinite. (3)**

**Комментарий:**

(3) Kṛṣṇa is the Supreme independent Enjoyer who possesses inconceivable potencies. He is that conscious subject who possesses independent desires and He naturally possesses potencies. Therefore, nothing is independent from Him. The potencies of the Supreme Consciousness are His glories. Kṛṣṇa is the one non-dual Absolute Truth and His glories are limitless. On the path of jñāna, icchā (will) and śakti (potency) are considered to be separate from Kṛṣṇa, and the non-dual, qualityless Brahman is perceived as the goal. The reality is that Parabrahman is the effulgence emanating from His personal form. In aṣṭāṅga-yoga, the subtle in-dwelling Monitor (Antaryāmī), who is the all-pervading consciousness within the universe and within the hearts of all beings as Paramātmā, is considered to be the goal. In reality, He is but one manifestation of Kṛṣṇa. Therefore, Brahman and Paramātmā are both portions of Kṛṣṇa’s personal form. Kṛṣṇa is completely conscious and replete with desire and potencies. He is the Supreme Enjoyer whose desires are always fulfilled.

**Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa-śakti (Kṛṣṇa and Kṛṣṇa’s Potencies)**

### Text 13

*kṛṣṇa-śakti kṛṣṇa haite nā haya svatantra*  
*yei śakti, sei kṛṣṇa – kahe veda-mantra*

**Перевод:**

**“Kṛṣṇa’s potencies are never independent from Kṛṣṇa. Those potencies and Kṛṣṇa are identical. The mantras of the Vedas explain this.**

### Text 14

*kṛṣṇa vibhu, śakti tāṅ’ra vaibhava-svarūpa*  
*ananta vaibhave kṛṣṇa haya eka-rūpa*

**Перевод:**

**“Kṛṣṇa is infinite and His potencies are His manifestations. His manifestations are unlimited and Kṛṣṇa is one.**

**Trividha-vaibhava (The Three Kinds of Manifestations)**

### Text 15

*śaktira prakāśa yei sei ta vaibhava*  
*vibhura vaibhava-mātra haya anubhava*

**Перевод:**

**“These potencies display His manifestations, and the manifestations of the Absolute enable one to perceive Him.**

### Text 16

*vaibhava trividha tava gaurāṅga sundara*  
*cid-acit jīva tina śāstrera gocara*

**Перевод:**

**“O Gaurāṅga-sundara, You have three kinds of manifestations. The śāstra says that these three are cit, acit and jīva. (4)**

**Комментарий:**

(4) Kṛṣṇa’s manifestations are threefold, namely cid-vaibhava (spiritual manifestation), acit – or māyā-vaibhava (material manifestation), and jīva-vaibhava (the manifestation of the jīva).

**Cid-vaibhava (The Spiritual Manifestations)**

### Text 17

*ananta vaikuṇṭha ādi yata kṛṣṇa-dhāma*  
*govinda śrī-kṛṣṇa hari ādi yata nāma*

**Перевод:**

**“Kṛṣṇa’s abodes start with the unlimited Vaikuṇṭha planets. His Names begin with Govinda, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, and Hari.**

### Text 18

*dvi-bhuja muralīdhara ādi yata rūpa*  
*bhaktānanda-prada ādi guṇa aparūpa*

**Перевод:**

**“His forms begin with two-armed Muralīdhara (playing the flute). His wonderful qualities begin with His bestowing bliss to His devotees.**

### Text 19

*vraje rāsa-līlā navadvīpe saṅkīrtana*  
*ei rūpa kṛṣṇa-līlā vicitra gaṇana*

**Перевод:**

**“In Vraja there is the rāsa-līlā and in Navadvīpa there is saṅkīrtana. Such activities are considered to be Kṛṣṇa’s amazing līlā. (5)**

**Комментарий:**

(5) Kṛṣṇa’s spiritual manifestations (cid-vaibhava) are considered to be amongst His spiritual potencies (cit-śakti). The spiritual potency of Kṛṣṇa is His superior potency. His complete spiritual potency produces His spiritual manifestation. From transcendental Kṛṣṇa’s spiritual potencies, all of His spiritual abodes, spiritual names, spiritual forms, and various spiritual pastimes are manifest. From the spiritual sandhinī potency, existence manifests; from the samvit potency all knowledge manifests; and from the hlādinī potency, bliss in relation to bhāva and rasa appear.s Yogamāyā is also counted amongst these spiritual potencies and is beyond material place, time, and attributes. She is always pure and full of bliss.

### Text 20

*e samasta cid-vaibhava aprākṛta haya*  
*āsiyāo e prapañce prāpañcika naya*

**Перевод:**

**“All of these spiritual manifestations are transcendental. Even though they may manifest within this material world, they are not material.**

### Text 21

*cid-vyāpāra samudaya viṣṇu-tattva-sāra*  
*viṣṇu-pada bali’ vede gāya bāra bāra*

**Перевод:**

**“All these are essentially viṣṇu-tattva. The Vedas refer to them as viṣṇu-pada again and again.**

**Kṛṣṇera cid-vibhutā-i viṣṇu-tattva śuddha-tattva (Kṛṣṇa’s Spiritual Manifestation is Viṣṇu-tattva and Pure Reality)**

### Text 22

*nāhi tāhe jaḍa-dharma māyāra vikāra*  
*jaḍātīta viṣṇu-tattva śuddha-sattva-sāra*

**Перевод:**

**“These spiritual manifestations are not of the nature of matter which is transformed by māyā. Viṣṇu-tattva is beyond matter and essentially situated in śuddha-sattva (pure goodness).**

### Text 23

*śuddha-sattva rajas-tamo-gandha-virahita*  
*rajas-tamo-miśra miśra-sattva suvidita*

**Перевод:**

**“Śuddha-sattva is devoid of even a scent of the modes of passion or ignorance. When goodness is mixed with passion or ignorance, it is miśra-sattva (mixed goodness). (6)**

**Комментарий:**

(6) Goodness is of two kinds, namely pure goodness (śuddha-sattva) and mixed goodness (miśra-sattva). All goodness found within spiritual manifestations is pure goodness. All goodness found within the material world is mixed goodness. Passion and ignorance are not present within pure goodness. From passion comes birth. Beginningless, transcendental existence does not have the passion that naturally produces birth, and it also does not have the ignorance that produces death – it only has eternal present (nitya-vartamāna). The Supreme Controller’s separated expansions (vibhinnāṁśa) are all situated in pure goodness whose connection with Māyā makes it combine with passion and ignorance. Although Girīśa (Śiva) and the Devas possess many great qualities as far as jīvas are concerned, the illusory nature of bound jīvas in the form of pride means that the Devas have mixed goodness combined with passion and ignorance. The Supreme Controller, who is in pure goodness, employs His inconceivable potency (acintya-śakti), and descends as Viṣṇu into the material world, always remaining in pure goodness and as the Master of Māyā. Māyā is His maidservant.

### Text 24

*govinda, vaikuṇṭha-nātha, kāraṇoda-śāyī*  
*garbhoda-śāyī āra kṣīra-sindhu-śāyī*

**Перевод:**

**“Govinda is Vaikuṇṭha-nātha, Kāraṇodakaśāyī, Garbhodakaśāyī and Kṣīrodakaśāyī Viṣṇu.**

### Text 25

*āra yata svāṁśa paricita avatāra*  
*sei saba śuddha-sattva viṣṇu-tattva-sāra*

**Перевод:**

**“He also descends as His personal expansions (svaṁśa). They are all situated in śuddha-sattva and are in essence viṣṇu-tattva.**

### Text 26

*goloke, vaikuṇṭhe, āra kāraṇa-sāgare*  
*athavā e jaḍe thāke, viṣṇu-nāma dhare*

**Перевод:**

**“In Goloka, Vaikuṇṭha, the Kāraṇa-sāgara (Causal Ocean), as well as within the material world, He is known as Viṣṇu.”**

### Text 27

*praveśi’ e jaḍa viśva māyāra adhīśa*  
*viṣṇu-nāma prāpta vibhu sarva-deva īśa*  
*māyāra īśvara māyī śuddha-sattva-maya*

**Перевод:**

**“When He enters the material world, He commands Māyā. He is known by the name ‘Viṣṇu.’ He is infinite and is the Controller of all the Devas. He is the Controller of Māyā. Māyā belongs to Him. He is situated in śuddha-sattva. (7)**

**Комментарий:**

(7) When the spiritual potency descends to this material world, it does not become material; it remains as a spiritual potency. Manifesting through the acintya-śakti (inconceivable potency), it is of a transcendental nature and is situated in pure goodness.

**Miśra-sattva (Mixed goodness)**

### Text 28

*miśra-sattva brahmā śiva ādi saba haya*

**Перевод:**

**“Brahmā, Śiva and the Devas are situated in mixed goodness.**

**Cid-vaibhavera vistṛti (The Expansion of Spiritual Potencies)**

### Text 29

*e samagra viṣṇu-tattva āra viṣṇu-dhāma*  
*tava cid-vaibhava-nātha tava līlā-grāma*

**Перевод:**

**“All viṣṇu-tattva forms and the abodes of Viṣṇu (viṣṇu-dhāma) are Your spiritual potencies, O Lord, and these are all Your pastimes.**

**Acid-vaibhava māya-tattva (The Material Potency, Māyā-tattva)**

### Text 30

*virajāra ei pāre yata vastu haya*  
*acit vaibhava tava caudda-loka-maya*

**Перевод:**

**“After these, on the other side of the Virajā, is Your material manifestation, which consists of the fourteen worlds.**

### Text 31

*māyāra vaibhava bali’ bale devī-dhāma*  
*pañca-bhūta mano-buddhi ahaṅkāra nāma*

**Перевод:**

**“Māyā’s manifestation is known as Devī-dhāma, consisting of the five gross elements as well as mind, intelligence, and false ego. (8)**

**Комментарий:**

(8) This planet is made up of the five material elements and the gross bodies of the bound jīvas are also made of these five elements. The subtle body that stores the impressions and desires of the jīvas is made up of mind, intelligence and false ego, and all these are also material. The jīva, who is a spiritual particle, is naturally pure and situated in śuddha-sattva. He is spiritual and different from the subtle body consisting of mind, intelligence and false ego.

### Text 32

*e bhūrloka, bhuvarloka āra svargaloka*  
*maharloka, jana-tapa-satya-brahmaloka*

**Перевод:**

**“This refers to Bhūrloka, Bhuvarloka, Svargaloka, Maharloka, Janaloka, Tapoloka, Satyaloka, and Brahmaloka.**

### Text 33

*atala, sutala-ādi nimna loka sāta*  
*māyika vaibhava tava śuna jagannātha*

**Перевод:**

**“Below those are Atala, Sutala etc. Please hear about Your material manifestation, O Lord of the universe.**

### Text 34

*cid-vaibhava pūrṇa-tattva māyā chāyā tāra*  
*cid-aṇu-svarūpa jīva vaibhava prakāra*

**Перевод:**

**“Your spiritual manifestation is complete reality. Māyā is a shadow of that. The finite spiritual jīvas are products of the spiritual manifestation.**

**Jīva-vaibhava (The Manifestation of the Jīva)**

### Text 35

*cid-dharma-vaśataḥ jīva svatantra gaṭhana*  
*saṅkhyāya ananta sukha tā’ra prayojana*

**Перевод:**

**“Because they are inherently spiritual by nature, the jīvas possess independence. They are innumerable and their goal is unlimited pleasure.**

**Mukta jīva (Liberated Jīvas)**

### Text 36

*sei sukha hetu yā’rā kṛṣṇere barila*  
*kṛṣṇa-pāriṣada mukta-rūpete rahila*

**Перевод:**

**“Those jīvas who turn towards Kṛṣṇa to attain such pleasure remain liberated and become Kṛṣṇa’s companions.**

**Baddha vā bahirmukha jīva (Bound, or Averse Jīvas)**

### Text 37

*yā’rā punaḥ nija-sukha kariyā bhāvanā*  
*pārśva-sthitā māyā-prati karila kāmanā*

**Перевод:**

**“Those who continue to think of their own selfish happiness will wish to remain at the side of Māyā.**

### Text 38

*sei saba nitya-kṛṣṇa-bahirmukha haila*  
*devī-dhāme māyā-kṛta śarīra pāila*

**Перевод:**

**“All of them eternally turn their faces away from Kṛṣṇa, and enter into Devī-dhāma, where they attain bodies created by Māyā.**

### Text 39

*puṇya-pāpa karma-cakre paḍiyā ekhana*  
*sthūla liṅga-dehe sadā karena bhramaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“Then they fall upon the wheel of puṇya and pāpa. They constantly roam while residing in gross and subtle bodies.**

### Text 40

*kabhu svarge uṭhe, kabhu niraye paḍiyā*  
*caurāśi lakṣa yoni bhoge bhramiyā bhramiyā*

**Перевод:**

**“Sometimes they rise to Svarga, and sometimes they fall into hellish conditions. They constantly roam through eight million, four hundred thousand wombs.**

**Tathāpi kṛṣṇa-dayā (Even Then, Kṛṣṇa is Merciful)**

### Text 41

*tumi vibhu, tomāra vaibhava jīva haya*  
*dāsera maṅgala cintā tomāra niścaya*

**Перевод:**

**“You are Infinite, and the jīvas are Your manifestations. Indeed, you always consider Your servant’s welfare. (9)**

**Комментарий:**

(9) In whatever condition the jīva is in, Kṛṣṇa is his friend and always gives him the results of his desires. The eternal relationship between the jīva and Kṛṣṇa is that Kṛṣṇa is the controller, and the jīva is the controlled. Kṛṣṇa is the lawgiver, and the jīva follows the laws. Kṛṣṇa is independent, and the jīva is dependent upon Kṛṣṇa. Kṛṣṇa is the Master, and the jīva is the servant. Kṛṣṇa gives the results, and the jīva enjoys the results.

### Text 42

*dāsa yāhā sukha māni’ kare anveṣaṇa*  
*tumi tāhā kṛpā kari’ kara vitaraṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“Whatever happiness Your servants seek, by Your mercy, You bestow that.**

**Prākṛta śubha-karma karma-kāṇḍa (Material Auspicious Works and Karma-kāṇḍa)**

### Text 43

*māyāra vaibhave ye anitya sukha cāya*  
*tomāra kṛpāya se anāyāse pāya*

**Перевод:**

**“For those influenced by the māyā potency who wish for temporary pleasures, they easily attain them, by Your mercy.**

### Text 44

*sei sukha prāptyupāya śubha-karma yata*  
*niramile dharma-yajña-yoga-homa-vrata*

**Перевод:**

**“This sort of pleasure is achieved through auspicious activities (śubha-karma), thus dharma, yajña, yoga, homa, and vratas were created.**

### Text 45

*sei saba śubha-karma sadā jaḍa-maya*  
*cinmayī pravṛtti tāhe kabhu nā milaya*

**Перевод:**

**“All of these auspicious activities are always mundane. Their functions can never match anything spiritual. (10)**

**Комментарий:**

(10) Dharma means varṇāśrama etc. Yajña means the agniṣṭoma etc. Yoga means aṣṭāṅga-yoga etc. Homa means havana etc. Vrata means darśa-paurṇamāsya etc. Śubha-karma means iṣṭāpūrta etc. These auspicious activities are performed using material utensils, at specific times and in specific places. Viṣṇu is known as Yajñeśvara (the Lord of yajña) and is the actual doer of these activities, but the function of these activities is not directly spiritual. Without direct spiritual activities, an individual cannot experience the Supreme.

### Text 46

*tāhāra sādhane sādhya jaḍamaya phala*  
*ucca-loka, bhoga sukha tāhāte prabala*

**Перевод:**

**“By performing such sādhana (meant for auspicious activities) one obtains material benefits. By its power one attains the higher planets and enjoys material pleasures.**

### Text 47

*sei saba karma-bhoga nāhi ātma-śānti*  
*tāhāte prayāsa karā atiśaya bhrānti*

**Перевод:**

**“The ātmā does not find peace by enjoying the results of all these material actions. Endeavouring for them is a great mistake.**

### Text 48

*sei saba śubha-karma upāya haiyā*  
*anitya upeya sādhe loka-sukha diyā*

**Перевод:**

**“The result of performing all these auspicious activities is that one attains temporary mundane pleasure. (11)**

**Комментарий:**

(11) ‘Mundane pleasure’ (loka-sukha) refers to the temporary happiness of Svargaloka and other higher planets. Spiritual pleasure is different.

**Sei avasthā haite uddhārera upāya (The Method of Rising Above this State)**

### Text 49

*kabhu yadi sādhu-saṅge jānite se pāre*  
*āmi jīva kṛṣṇa-dāsa, yāya māyā-pāre*

**Перевод:**

**“If, by the association of sādhus, one understands, “I am a jīva who is Kṛṣṇa’s servant,” then one can cross beyond māyā.**

### Text 50

*se virala phala mātra sukṛti-janita*  
*tuccha karma-kāṇḍe nāhi karile vihita*

**Перевод:**

**“That rare result is only achieved due to sukṛti. The trivial activities prescribed in karma-kāṇḍa cannot bestow it.**

**Jñāna-kāṇḍa, brahma-laya sukha (Jñāna-kāṇḍa – the Happiness of Merging with Brahman)**

### Text 51

*āra yini māyāra yantraṇā-mātra jāni’*  
*mukti-lābhe yatnavān, tini hana jñānī*

**Перевод:**

**“One who knows that māyā only brings suffering, and who endeavours to attain mukti is a jñānī.**

### Text 52

*se saba lokera janya tumi dayāmaya*  
*jñāna-kāṇḍa brahma-vidyā diyācha niścaya*

**Перевод:**

**“You are most merciful to all people. Indeed, You have given jñāna-kāṇḍa and brahma-vidyā (knowledge of Brahman).**

### Text 53

*sei vidyā māyāvāda kariyā āśraya*  
*jaḍa mukta ha’ye brahme jīva haya laya*

**Перевод:**

**“Those who take refuge in the knowledge of māyāvāda become free from matter and such jīvas merge into Brahman.**

**Brahma-vastu ki? (What is the Nature of Brahman?)**

### Text 54

*sei brahma tava aṅga-kānti jyotirmaya*  
*virajāra pāre sthita tā’te haya laya*

**Перевод:**

**“Brahman is the effulgence of Your form, which is full of light. It is situated on the shore of the Virajā.**

### Text 55

*ye saba asure viṣṇu karena saṁhāra*  
*tāhārā-o sei brahme yāya māyā-pāra*

**Перевод:**

**“All the demons killed by Viṣṇu cross to the furthest shore of Māyā and merge into Brahman.**

**Kṛṣṇa-bahirmukha (Those Who are Averse to Kṛṣṇa)**

### Text 56

*karmī jñānī ubhaye-i kṛṣṇa-bahirmukha*  
*kabhu nāhi āsvādaya kṛṣṇa-dāsya-sukha*

**Перевод:**

**“Both the karmīs and jñānīs have turned their faces away from Kṛṣṇa. They never relish the pleasure found in service to Him.**

**Bhaktyunmukhī sukṛti (The Sukṛti of Those who Favour Bhakti)**

### Text 57

*bhaktira unmukhī sei sukṛti pradhāna*  
*tā’ra phale jīva bhakta-sādhu-saṅga pāna*

**Перевод:**

**“The greatest sukṛti is achieved by one who turns his face towards the process of bhakti. It results in that jīva attaining the association of sādhus. (12)**

**Комментарий:**

(12) Sukṛti is of three types – karmonmukhī (moving towards karma), jñānonmukhī (moving towards jñāna), and bhaktyunmukhī (moving towards bhakti). The first two types of sukrti deliver the results of enjoying the rewards of karma and the attainment of mukti. The last type of sukṛti gives rise to śraddhā in exclusive bhakti. To those who are ignorant, the various activities of śuddha-bhakti seem to be ordinary pious deeds.

### Text 58

*śraddhāvān haye kṛṣṇa-bhakta saṅga kare*  
*nāme ruci, jīve dayā, bhakti-patha dhare*

**Перевод:**

**“One who has śraddhā and associates with Kṛṣṇa’s devotees achieves a taste for the Holy Name, becomes compassionate to other jīvas, and walks on the path of bhakti.**

**Karmī o jñānīra prati kṛpāya gauṇa-patha vidhāna (Out of Mercy for the Karmī and Jñānī, the Lord Creates an Indirect Path)**

### Text 59

*dayāra sāgara tumi jīvera īśvara*  
*karmī jñānī bahirmukha uddhāre tatpara*

**Перевод:**

**“You are an ocean of mercy and the Lord of the jīvas. You are intent upon delivering the karmīs and jñānīs who have turned their faces from You.**

### Text 60

*karma-pathe jñāna-pathe pathika ye jana*  
*tāhāra uddhāra lāgi tomāra yatana*

**Перевод:**

**“You endeavour to deliver those who travel on the paths of karma and jñāna.**

### Text 61

*sei sei pathikera maṅgala cintiyā*  
*gauṇa-bhakti-patha eka rākhila kariyā*

**Перевод:**

**“Thinking of the benefit of those who travel on these paths, You have created the path of indirect bhakti. (13)**

**Комментарий:**

(13) By following the practice of varṇāśrama one pleases Hari. In this way the path of karma is an indirect path to bhakti.

**Karmīra pakṣe karmera gauṇa-bhakti patha (For the Karmīs, the Indirect Path of Bhakti is Mixed with Karma)**

### Text 62

*karmī varṇāśrame thāki’ sādhu-saṅga kari’*  
*karma-mājhe bhakti kare gauṇa-patha dhari’*

**Перевод:**

**“Remaining in the varṇāśrama system and associating with sādhus, the karmī walks on the path of indirect bhakti mixed with karma.**

### Text 63

*tāra’ kṛta karma saba hṛdaya śodhiyā*  
*tirohita haya śraddhā-bīje sthāna diyā*

**Перевод:**

**“By engaging in various acts of karma, his heart becomes purified. When impurities have disappeared, the seed of śraddhā is given a place in his heart.**

**Jñānīra gauṇa-patha (The Indirect Path of the Jñānī)**

### Text 64

*jñānī sukṛtira bale bhaktera kṛpāya*  
*ananya-bhaktite śraddhā anayase pāya*

**Перевод:**

**“By the power of his sukṛti and the mercy of a devotee, a jñānī can easily attain śraddhā in exclusive bhakti. (14)**

**Комментарий:**

(14) One may attain the association of devotee sādhus while on the path of jñāna, which is an indirect path to bhakti. In regards to the way of achieving pure bhakti, in the Rāmānanda-samvāda, Mahāprabhu referred to these two indirect paths (karma and jñāna) as external. He did not respect them.

### Text 65

*tumi bala, mora dāsa māyāra vipāke*  
*cāhe anya tuccha phala chāḍiyā āmāke*

**Перевод:**

**“You say, ‘My servant has fallen into the misery of māyā. Rejecting Me, he now desires other trivial rewards.**

### Text 66

*āmi jāni tā’ra yā’te haya sumaṅgala*  
*bhukti-mukti chāḍāiyā di-i bhakti-phala*

**Перевод:**

**“‘I know what is truly auspicious for him. I will make him abandon sense enjoyment and mukti and give him the result of bhakti.**

**Gauṇa-pathera prakriyā (The Method of the Indirect Path)**

### Text 67

*tā’ra kāma anusāre cālājñā tāhāre*  
*gauṇa-patha bhakti-mārge śraddhā di-i tā’re*

**Перевод:**

**“‘According to his desire, I will guide him on the indirect path of bhakti and award him śraddhā in that.’**

### Text 68

*e tomāra kṛpā prabhu tumi kṛpāmaya*  
*kṛpā nā karile kise jīva śuddha haya*

**Перевод:**

**“O most compassionate Lord, this is all Your mercy. If You do not give such mercy, how will the jīvas be purified?**

**Kalite gauṇa-pathera durdaśā (The Indirect Path is Difficult in Kali-yuga)**

### Text 69

*satya-yuge dhyāna-yoge kata ṛṣi-gaṇe*  
*śuddha kari’ dile prabhu nija-bhakti-dhane*

**Перевод:**

**“In Satya-yuga so many sages performed dhyāna-yoga (the yoga of meditation) and were purified by the Lord and given the treasure of bhakti unto You.**

### Text 70

*tretā-yuge yajña-karme aneka śodhile*  
*dvāpare arcana-mārge bhakti bilāile*

**Перевод:**

**“In Tretā-yuga many persons were purified by performing yajñas, and in Dvāpara-yuga they took to the path of arcana (Deity worship). You distributed bhakti to them.**

### Text 71

*kali-āgamane nātha jīvera durdaśā*  
*dekhi’ jñāna, karma, yoga chāḍila bharasā*

**Перевод:**

**“O Lord, when Kali-yuga arrived, seeing the troublesome state of the jīvas, You made them lose all hope in jñāna, karma, and yoga.**

### Text 72

*alpa āyu, bahu pīḍā, bala-buddhi-hrāsa*  
*ei saba upadrava jīve kaila grāsa*

**Перевод:**

**“Their lives are short, they suffer many tribulations, and their strength and intelligence decreases. All these disturbances devour the jīvas.**

### Text 73

*varṇāśrama-dharma āra sāṅkhya-yoga-jñāna*  
*kali-jīve uddhārite nahe balavān*

**Перевод:**

**“Varṇāśrama-dharma, saṅkhya, yoga, and jñāna have no power to deliver the jīvas in Kali-yuga.**

### Text 74

*jñāna-karma-gata ye bhaktira gauṇa-patha*  
*kaṇṭake saṅkīrṇa hañā haila vipatha*

**Перевод:**

**“The indirect path of bhakti, mixed with karma or jñāna, becomes a narrow and treacherous path strewn with thorns. (15)**

**Комментарий:**

(15) At the time of following the path of jñāna one may attain real sādhu-saṅga, become desireless (niṣkāma) and offer the results of one’s actions to the Supreme through the process of karma-yoga and this may lead one to the temple of Bhakti Devī – yet in the age of Kali these indirect paths have become corrupted. Instead of real sādhus, dharma-dvajīs (religious hypocrites) are more prominent. Activities that make one crave sense enjoyment are more prominent than activities that purify the heart. Thus, through the indirect path one does not attain auspiciousness. In Dvāpara-yuga, arcana was seen to be the main path, but now even that is mostly corrupted due to various types of depravity.

### Text 75

*pṛthak upāya dhari’ upeya sādhane*  
*vighna bahutara haila jīvera jīvane*

**Перевод:**

**“By practising this path of sādhana, the jīva finds many obstacles in life. (16)**

**Комментарий:**

(16) The means by which one attains the upeya (result) is known as upāya. The result of upāya, or sādhana, is known as upeya. Sādhana is another name for upāya. Sādhya is another name for upeya. By the mercy of the Supreme Lord, all jīvas can attain the highest upeya or sādhya. Karma and jñāna are not the primary sādhanas to attain this upeya. Why not? Because as they get close to the upeya, karma and jñāna disappear. Sādhana of the Holy Name is not like that. The Holy Name and the Supreme Lord are non-different. Therefore, the Holy Name itself remains both the sādhya or upeya and the sādhana or upāya. By knowing this truth one attains exceptional good fortune.

**Nāmālocanāra mukhya-patha (Understanding that the Name is the Primary Path)**

### Text 76

*prabhu tumi jīvera maṅgala cintā kari’*  
*kali-yuge nāma-saṅge svayaṁ avatari’*

**Перевод:**

**“O Prabhu, considering how to benefit the jīvas, in Kali-yuga You Yourself descended along with the Holy Name.**

### Text 77

*yuga-dharma pracārile nāma-saṅkīrtana*  
*mukhya-pathe jīva pāya kṛṣṇa-prema-dhana*

**Перевод:**

**“You taught nāma-saṅkīrtana as the yuga-dharma. By following this direct path, the jīvas can achieve the great wealth of kṛṣṇa-prema.**

### Text 78

*nāmera smaraṇe āra nāma-saṅkīrtana*  
*ei mātra dharma jīva karibe pālana*

**Перевод:**

**“Only the dharma of remembering the Holy Name and nāma-saṅkīrtana can save the jīvas.**

**Sādhya-sādhana o upāya upeyera abhedatā-krame nāmera mukhyatā (The Sādhya and Sādhana, and the Upāya and Upeya are Identical Paths and the Holy Name is Primary)**

### Text 79

*yeita sādhana, sei sādhya yabe haila*  
*upāya upeya-madhye bheda nā rahila*

**Перевод:**

**“Whatever is the sādhya is also the sādhana. No difference remains between the upāya and upeya.**

### Text 80

*sādhyera sādhane āra nāhi antarāya*  
*anāyāse tare jīva tomāra kṛpāya*

**Перевод:**

**“There are no obstacles in this sādhana and sādhya. By Your mercy it is easily achieved by the jīvas.**

### Text 81

*āmi-ta adhama ati majiyā viṣaye*  
*nā bhajinu nāma tava ati mūḍha ha’ye*

**Перевод:**

**“I am wretched and fully absorbed in mundane enjoyment. I never worshipped Your Name because I am such a great fool.”**

### Text 82

*dara dara dhārā cakṣe brahma-hari-dāsa*  
*paḍila prabhura pade chāḍiyā niḥśvāsa*

**Перевод:**

**With tears streaming profusely from his eyes, Brahmā Hari Dāsa fell sighing at the Lord’s feet.**

### Text 83

*hari-bhakta-bhakti mātre vinoda yāhāra*  
*hari-nāma cintāmaṇi jīvana tāhāra*

**Перевод:**

**Those whose only pleasure is found in bhakti to Hari and His devotees consider Hari-nāma Cintāmaṇi to be their very life.**

## Chapter 2. Nāma-grahaṇa-vicāra (A Deliberation on Chanting the Holy Name)

### Text 1

*gadāi gaurāṅga jaya jāhnavā-jīvana*  
*sītādvaita jaya śrīvāsādi bhakta-gaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**Glories to Gaura-Gadādhara, and the life of Jāhnavā (Nityānanda Prabhu). Glories to the husband of Sītā (Advaita Ācārya), to Śrīvāsa and all the devotees.**

### Text 2

*mahā-preme hari-dāsa karena rodana*  
*preme tā’re gauracandra dilā āliṅgana*

**Перевод:**

**With great prema, Hari Dāsa wept, and with love, Gauracandra embraced him.**

### Text 3

*balena tomāra sama bhakta kothā āra*  
*sarva-tattva-jñātā tumi sadā māyā-pāra*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord said, “Where is there another devotee like you? You know all spiritual truths, and you are always beyond the influence of māyā.**

**Ananya bhajanera śreṣṭhatā (The Greatness of Exclusive Bhajana)**

### Text 4

*nica-kule avatari’ dekhā’le sakale*  
*dhane māne kule śīle kṛṣṇa nāhi mile*

**Перевод:**

**“You descended here and appeared in a low family, showing everyone that it is not by wealth, respect, birth in a high family, or through mundane virtues that one is capable of connecting with Kṛṣṇa.**

### Text 5

*ananya-bhajane yāṅ’ra śraddhā atiśaya*  
*devatā apekṣā śreṣṭha sei mahāśaya*

**Перевод:**

**“One who has intense śraddhā in exclusive bhajana is superior even to the Devatās. He is a great personality.**

**Śrī-hari-dāsera nāmācāryatā (Śrī Hari Dāsa as the Ācārya of the Holy Name)**

### Text 6

*nāma-tattva sarva-sāra tomāra vidita*  
*ācāre, ācārya tumi, pracāre paṇḍita*

**Перевод:**

**“You understand nāma-tattva (the principle of the Holy Name), which is the essence of everything. You are a perfect example of proper conduct and you are most expert in propagation (of the Holy Name).**

### Text 7

*bala hari-dāsa, nāma-mahimā apāra*  
*śuniyā tomāra mukhe ānanda āmāra*

**Перевод:**

**“O Hari Dāsa, speak about the unlimited glories of the Holy Name. If I hear them from your mouth, I will feel bliss.**

**Vaiṣṇava-lakṣaṇa (The Symptoms of a Vaiṣṇava)**

### Text 8

*eka nāma yā’ra mukhe vaiṣṇava se haya*  
*tā’re gṛhī yatna kari’ mānibe niścaya*

**Перевод:**

**“If the Holy Name comes from someone’s mouth even once, he is a Vaiṣṇava. Indeed, a householder should endeavour to respect him. (1)**

**Комментарий:**

(1) Replying to the questions of the residents of Kulīnagrāma, Mahāprabhu said:prabhu kahena kṛṣṇa-sevā vaiṣṇava-sevana

*nirantara kara kṛṣṇa-nāma-saṅkīrtana*  
*prabhu kahe yāṅra mukhe śuni eka-bāra*  
*kṛṣṇa-nāma sei pūjya śreṣṭha sabākara*  
*kṛṣṇa-nāma nirantara yāṅhāra vadane*  
*sei vaiṣṇava-śreṣṭha bhaja tāṅhāra caraṇe*  
*yāṅhāra darśane mukhe āise kṛṣṇa-nāma*  
*tāṅhāre jāniha tumi vaiṣṇava-pradhāna*

**(The Lord said, “Serve Kṛṣṇa and offer service to the Vaiṣṇavas. Continuously perform kṛṣṇa-nāma-saṅkīrtana.” The Lord said, “Whoever’s mouth chants kṛṣṇa-nāma even once is worshippable and is the best of all. One who always chants kṛṣṇa-nāma is a first-class Vaiṣṇava, and you should serve his feet. By seeing such a person, the mouths of others will chant kṛṣṇa-nāma, so you should consider such a person as the best of Vaiṣṇavas.” (Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya-līlā 15.104, 15.106, 16.72, 16.74)**

**Vaiṣṇavatara-lakṣaṇa (The Symptoms of a Greater Vaiṣṇava)**

### Text 9

*nirantara yā’ra mukhe śuni kṛṣṇa-nāma*  
*sei se vaiṣṇavatara sarva-guṇa-dhāma*

**Перевод:**

**“That person from whose mouth one hears the constant chanting of kṛṣṇa-nāma is a greater Vaiṣṇava, the abode of all good qualities.**

**Vaiṣṇavatama-lakṣaṇa (The Symptoms of the Highest Vaiṣṇava)**

### Text 10

*vaiṣṇava uttama sei yāhāre dekhile*  
*kṛṣṇa-nāma āse mukhe kṛṣṇa-bhakti mile*

**Перевод:**

**“The highest Vaiṣṇava is he whom, just by seeing him, one feels kṛṣṇa–bhakti and kṛṣṇa-nāma appears in one’s mouth.**

### Text 11

*hena kṛṣṇa-nāma jīva ki-rūpe karibe*  
*tāhāra vidhāna tumi āmāre balibe*

**Перевод:**

**“How will the jīvas accept kṛṣṇa-nāma? Please tell Me what provision is being made for them?”**

### Text 12

*kara yuḍi hari-dāsa balena vacana*  
*prema gada-gada svara sajala nayana*

**Перевод:**

**Folding his hands, Hari Dāsa spoke. With feelings of prema, his voice choked up and his eyes were brimming with tears.**

**Nāmera svarūpa (The Intrinsic Nature of the Holy Name)**

### Text 13

*kṛṣṇa-nāma cintāmaṇi, anādi, cinmaya*  
*yei kṛṣṇa sei nāma, eka-tattva haya*

**Перевод:**

**(Hari Dāsa said) “Kṛṣṇa-nāma is like a touchstone (cintāmaṇi), it is beginningless and fully spiritual. Kṛṣṇa Himself and His Holy Name are one tattva. (2)**

**Комментарий:**

(2) A touchstone can give anything. The touchstone of kṛṣṇa-nāma delivers dharma, artha, kāma and mokṣa (piety, wealth, sense-enjoyment and liberation) to those who have material desires, and for those without material desires, it gives kṛṣṇa-prema.

### Text 14

*caitanya-vigraha nāma nitya-mukta-tattva*  
*nāma nāmī bhinna naya, nitya śuddha-sattva*

**Перевод:**

**“The Holy Name is the form of pure consciousness. Its nature is that it is always liberated. The Name and the Named are non-different, and it is always transcendentally pure.**

### Text 15

*e jaḍa-jagate tāṅ’ra akṣara ākāra*  
*rasa-rūpe rasikete sattva-avatāra*

**Перевод:**

**“Although the Holy Name descends into this mundane world in the form of syllables, it remains pure and is the very form of rasa which is tasted by rasikas (those devotees who relish transcendental mellows).**

### Text 16

*kṛṣṇa vastu haya cāri dharme paricita*  
*nāma rūpa guṇa karma anādi vihita*

**Перевод:**

**“Kṛṣṇa-vastu (the disposition of Kṛṣṇa) is understood through His four natural attributes (dharma) – namely His nāma, rūpa, guṇa and karma (name, form, qualities and activities) which are all conceived of as having no beginning. (3)**

**Комментарий:**

(3) An object (vastu) is known by its name, form, qualities and actions. Kṛṣṇa is the parama-vastu (Supreme Object). Therefore, His name, form, qualities and pastimes are the four attributes that reveal him. That which lacks identity cannot be recognised – Brahman, for example. Brahman is said to be devoid of qualities, thus Brahman is not a vastu. Bhagavat-tattva is the only exception and is definable in this way.

**Nāma nitya-siddha (The Holy Name is Eternally Perfect)**

### Text 17

*nitya vastu rasa-rūpa kṛṣṇa se advaya*  
*sei cāri paricaye vastu siddha haya*

**Перевод:**

**“As an eternal principle, and as the form of rasa, Kṛṣṇa is free from material dualities. These four attributes that He is known by are all perfect.**

### Text 18

*sandhinī śaktite tāṅ’ra cāri paricaya*  
*nitya-siddha-rūpe khyāta sarvadā cinmaya*

**Перевод:**

**“These four attributes appear through His sandhinī potency. They are renowned as eternally perfect, and always fully spiritual.**

### Text 19

*kṛṣṇa ākarṣaye sarva viśva-gata jana*  
*sei nitya dharma gata kṛṣṇa nāma dhana*

**Перевод:**

**“Kṛṣṇa attracts everyone in the universe. The wealth that is kṛṣṇa-nāma also has the same eternal quality.**

**Kṛṣṇa-rūpa nitya (The Eternal Form of Kṛṣṇa)**

### Text 20

*kṛṣṇa-rūpa kṛṣṇa haite sarvadā abheda*  
*nāma, rūpa eka vastu nāhika prabheda*

**Перевод:**

**“Kṛṣṇa and Kṛṣṇa’s form are identical. His name and form are one substance. They are never different.**

### Text 21

*śrī-nāma smarile rūpa āise saṅge saṅge*  
*rūpa nāma bhinna naya nāce nānā raṅge*

**Перевод:**

**“One who remembers the Holy Name, associates with the form. There is no difference between the Name and the form. They dance on different stages.**

**Kṛṣṇa-guṇa nityatva (The Eternality of Kṛṣṇa’s Qualities)**

### Text 22

*kṛṣṇa-guṇa catuḥ-ṣaṣṭhi ananta apāra*  
*yāṅ’ra nija-aṁśa-rūpe saba avatāra*

**Перевод:**

**“Kṛṣṇa possesses sixty-four qualities which are endless and boundless. In all His avatāras these qualities are only partially manifest. (4)**

**Комментарий:**

(4) Please see the Pramāṇa-mālā (‘garland of evidence’) in the fifth chapter. Sixty-four qualities exist in full within Kṛṣṇa. In Rāma and the other avatāras that appear from Nārāyaṇa, down to the svāṁśa-avatāras and vilāsa-avatāras, sixty qualities are manifest. In Girīśa (Śiva) and other Devatās, fifty-five qualities are partially manifested. In ordinary jīvas, fifty qualities are seen in the form of a portion of a portion. Amongst viṣṇu-tattva, Kṛṣṇa possesses four uncommon qualities, thus He is the topmost tattva.

### Text 23

*yāṅ’ra guṇa-aṁśe brahmā, śivādi īśvara*  
*yāṅ’ra guṇe nārāyaṇa ṣaṣṭhi-guṇeśvara*

**Перевод:**

**“Some of these qualities are partially present in Brahmā, Śiva, and other controlling Devatās. Being the Lord of all attributes, Nārāyaṇa possesses sixty qualities.**

### Text 24

*sei saba nitya-guṇe nitya nāma tāṅ’ra*  
*ananta saṅkhyāya vyāpta vaikuṇṭha-vyāpāra*

**Перевод:**

**“All these eternal qualities are constantly present in the Holy Name, and an unlimited number of qualities pervade Vaikuṇṭha.**

**Kṛṣṇa-līlāra nityatva (The Eternality of Kṛṣṇa’s Pastimes)**

### Text 25

*sei guṇa-taraṅgete līlāra vistāra*  
*goloke vaikuṇṭha, vraja saba cid-ākāra*

**Перевод:**

**“The waves of these qualities expand as the Lord’s pastimes. These perfectly spiritual pastimes are manifested in Goloka, Vaikuṇṭha, and Vraja.**

**Cid-vastute nāma, rūpa, guṇa, līlā vastu haite pṛthak naya (There is no Difference Between the Spiritual Name, Form, Qualities and Pastimes.)**

### Text 26

*nāma-rūpa-guṇa-līlā abhinna udaya*  
*acit samparke baddha-jīve bhinna haya*

**Перевод:**

**“When the Lord’s name, form, qualities, and pastimes appear, they are non-different from each other. The material attributes of the conditioned jīvas are different from each other. (5)**

**Комментарий:**

(5) Kṛṣṇa is infinite consciousness. Thus, His name, form, qualities and pastimes are fully spiritual and non-different from Him. The jīva is a finite particle of consciousness, therefore in his pure state, his name, form, qualities and activities are also of the nature of being finite consciousness and naturally non-different from him. By contrast, in this material world, the conditioned jīva is given a gross and subtle body, and then the name, form, qualities and activities of this body are different from him. They are an imitation (of his true spiritual characteristics). When, by the mercy of Kṛṣṇa, he is liberated, he is no longer like that.

### Text 27

*śuddha-jīve nāma, rūpa, guṇa, kriyā eka*  
*jaḍāśrita dehe bheda ei se viveka*

**Перевод:**

**“The names, forms, qualities and activities of a pure jīva are all one. But they can be distinguished as being different from each other when a jīva takes shelter of matter.**

### Text 28

*kṛṣṇe nāhi jaḍa-gandha, ataeva tāṅ’ya*  
*nāma-rūpa-guṇa-līlā eka-tattva bhāya*

**Перевод:**

**“There is not even a trace of anything material about Kṛṣṇa. His name, form, qualities and pastimes exist as one tattva.**

**Nāmera sarva-mūlatva (The Holy Name is at the Foundation of Everything)**

### Text 29

*ei cāri paricaya madhye nāma taṅ’ra*  
*sakalera ādi se pratīti sabākāra*

**Перевод:**

**“Amongst these four ways of understanding You (Your name, form, qualities, and pastimes), Your Name is foremost. This is accepted by everyone.**

### Text 30

*ataeva ‘nāma’ mātra vaiṣṇavera dharma*  
*nāme prasphuṭita haya rūpa, guṇa, karma*

**Перевод:**

**“Your Name alone is the dharma of the Vaiṣṇavas. Through the Holy Name, Your form, qualities and activities expand.**

### Text 31

*kṛṣṇera samagra līlā nāme vidyamāna*  
*nāma se parama-tattva tomāra vidhāna*

**Перевод:**

**“The totality of Kṛṣṇa’s pastimes are discovered through the medium of the Holy Name. Your process to understand the Absolute Truth is through the Holy Name.**

**Vaiṣṇava o vaiṣṇava-prāye bheda āche (The Difference Between a Real Vaiṣṇava and One who is Almost a Vaiṣṇava)**

### Text 32

*sei nāma baddha-jīva śraddhā-sahakāre*  
*śuddha-rūpe laile vaiṣṇava bali tāre*

**Перевод:**

**“A conditioned jīva may chant the Holy Name with śraddhā and upon attaining purity, he is said to be a Vaiṣṇava.**

### Text 33

*nāmābhāsa yā’ra haya se vaiṣṇava-prāya*  
*kṛṣṇa-kṛpā bale krame śuddha-bhāva pāya*

**Перевод:**

**“One who chants nāmābhāsa (a semblance of the Holy Name) is a vaiṣṇava-prāya (almost a Vaiṣṇava). By the power of Kṛṣṇa’s mercy he eventually achieves śuddha–bhāva (pure transcendental sentiments).**

**Ei māyika jagate kṛṣṇa-nāma o jīva ei duiṭi mātra cid-vyāpāra (In the World of Māyā, only the Name of Kṛṣṇa and the Jīva are Spiritual)**

### Text 34

*nāma sama vastu nāi e-bhava-saṁsāre*  
*nāme se parama-dhana kṛṣṇera bhāṇḍāre*

**Перевод:**

**“There is nothing within this material world of birth and death like the Holy Name. The Holy Name is the supreme wealth of Kṛṣṇa’s treasury.**

### Text 35

*jīva nije cid-vyāpāra, kṛṣṇa-nāma āra*  
*āra saba prāpañcika jagat saṁsāra*

**Перевод:**

**“The jīvas themselves and Kṛṣṇa’s Holy Name alone are spiritual. Everything else in this world of rebirth is comprised of the five gross elements. (6)**

**Комментарий:**

(6) In this mundane world, everything is māyika and consists of dull matter. By the will of Kṛṣṇa, some jīvas are trapped here. They are the only spiritual objects within the material world. Kṛṣṇa descends in the form of the Holy Name, and this is the second spiritual object in the material world. Therefore, there are only two kinds of spiritual realities in the material world – the jīvas and kṛṣṇa-nāma. Brahmā and the Devas are all said to be vibhinnāṁśa manifestations and they are counted amongst the jīvas.

**Mukhya gauṇa bhede nāma dui prakāra (The Difference Between Primary and Secondary Types of Holy Name)**

### Text 36

*mukhya, gauṇa bhede kṛṣṇa-nāma dvi-prakāra*  
*mukhya-nāmāśraye jīva pāya sarva-sāra*

**Перевод:**

**“Kṛṣṇa has two different kinds of Names – primary (mukhya) and secondary (gauṇa). By taking shelter of the primary Names, the jīvas achieve the highest benefit.**

### Text 37

*cil-līlā āśraya kari yata kṛṣṇa-nāma*  
*sei sei mukhya nāma sarva-guṇa dhāma*

**Перевод:**

**“The Names of Kṛṣṇa describing His transcendental pastimes are the primary Names. They are the abode of all divine qualities.**

**Mukhya-nāma (Primary Names)**

### Text 38-40

*govinda, gopāla, rāma, śrī-nanda-nandana*  
*rādhānātha, hari, yaśomatī-prāṇa-dhana*

*madana-mohana, śyāmasundara, mādhava*  
*gopīnātha, vraja-gopa-rākhāla, yādava*

*ei-rūpa nitya-līlā-prakāśaka nāma*  
*e-saba kīrtane jīva pāya kṛṣṇa-dhāma*

**Перевод:**

**“Govinda (the King of the cows and senses), Gopāla (the Protector of the cows), Rāma (the reservoir of divine bliss), Śrī Nanda-nandana (the Son of Nanda), Rādhānātha (the Lord of Rādhā), Hari (the Remover of obstacles), Yaśomatī-prāṇa-dhana (The Life-treasure of Yaśomatī), Madana-mohana (the Bewilderer of Kāmadeva), Śyāmasundara (He who is dark and beautiful), Mādhava (the Husband of the Goddess of fortune), Gopīnātha (the Lord of the gopīs), Vraja-gopa-rākhāla (the Protector of the cowherd boys of Vraja), and Yādava (the Lord of the Yadus) – such Names describe the eternal pastimes. By performing kīrtana of all these Names, the jīva attains Kṛṣṇa’s abode.**

**Gauṇa nāma o tāhāra lakṣaṇa (Secondary Names and their Attributes)**

### Text 41

*jaḍā-prakṛtira paricaye nāma yata*  
*prakṛtira guṇe gauṇa vedera sammata*

**Перевод:**

**“Those names in relation to dull matter have mundane qualities and are secondary. This is accepted by the Vedas.**

### Text 42

*sṛṣṭi-kartā, paramātmā, brahma, sthiti-kara*  
*jagat-saṁhartā, pātā, yajñeśvara, hara*

**Перевод:**

**“(These names are) Śṛṣṭi-kartā (the Creator), Paramātmā (the Indwelling Monitor), Brahma (the Expansive One), Sthiti-kara (the Maintainer), Jagat-saṁhartā (the Annihilator of the world), Pātā (the Guardian), Yajñeśvara (the Lord of yajñas), and Hara (the Remover of sins).**

**Mukhya o gauṇa nāmera phala-bheda (The Various Results from Primary and Secondary Names)**

### Text 43

*ei-rūpa nāma karma-jñāna-kāṇḍa-gata*  
*puṇya, mokṣa dāna kare śāstrera sammata*

**Перевод:**

**“Such names are found in karma-kāṇḍa and jñāna-kāṇḍa. They produce piety and liberation. This is the opinion of the śāstra.**

### Text 44

*nāmera ye mukhya phala kṛṣṇa-prema-dhana*  
*tā’ra mukhya-nāme mātra labhe sādhu-gaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“The wealth of kṛṣṇa-prema is the result of those Names that are primary. This is attained by the sādhus only through these primary Names. (7)**

**Комментарий:**

(7) Kṛṣṇa’s secondary names deliver the results of piety and liberation. Only Kṛṣṇa’s primary names are able to give prema.

**Nāma o nāmābhāsa-mātra phala-bheda (The Different Results of the Real Name and the Semblance of the Name)**

### Text 45-46

*eka kṛṣṇa-nāma yadi mukhe bāhirāya*  
*athavā śravaṇa-pathe antarete yāya*  
*śuddha-varṇa haya vā aśuddha-varṇa haya*  
*tā’te jīva tare’ ei śāstrera nirṇaya*

**Перевод:**

**“If kṛṣṇa-nāma comes out of the mouth or enters the ears even once, whether its syllables are pure or impure, that jīva is delivered. That is the verdict of the śāstra.**

### Text 47

*kintu eka kathā ithe āche suniścita*  
*nāmābhāsa haile vilambe haya hita*

**Перевод:**

**“But one thing should be said about this – for certain, any benefit attained by nāmābhāsa will be slow.**

### Text 48

*nāmābhāsa haile-o anya śubha haya*  
*prema-dhana kevala vilambe upajaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Other kinds of auspicious results may come from nāmābhāsa, but the treasure of prema only arises after a long time.**

### Text 49

*nāmābhāse pāpa-kṣaye śuddha-nāma haya*  
*takhana-i śrī-kṛṣṇa-prema labhaye niścaya*

**Перевод:**

**“The results of pāpa are destroyed by nāmābhāsa and eventually the pure Holy Name appears. Then, at that time, the chanter certainly attains śrī-kṛṣṇa-prema. (8)**

**Комментарий:**

(8) By nāmābhāsa all the results of pāpa are destroyed. When pāpā and anarthas are driven far away, śuddha-nāma dances on the tongue of the devotee. Then śuddha-nāma awards one kṛṣṇa-prema.

**Vyavahita vā vyavadhāne doṣe janme (The Fault Arising from Interruption or Separation)**

### Text 50

*kintu vyavahita hale haya aparādha*  
*sei aparādhe haya prema-lābha vādha*

**Перевод:**

**“However, vyavahita (an interruption in the Name) is an offence. This offence halts the attainment of prema.**

### Text 51

*nāma-nāmī bheda-buddhi vyavadhāna haya*  
*vyavahita thākile kadāpi prema naya*

**Перевод:**

**“To consider the Name and the Named to be different is an interruption. While an interruption remains there can be no prema.**

**Vyavadhāna dui prakāra (The Two Types of Separation)**

### Text 52

*varṇa-vyavadhāna āra tattva-vyavadhāna*  
*vyavadhāna dvi-prakāra vedera vidhāna*

**Перевод:**

**“Separation of syllables and separation of tattvas – these are the two types of separation described in the Vedas.**

**Māyāvāda-i nāme tattva-vyavadhāna kare (The Māyāvāda Conception of Separation of Tattvas in Regards to the Name)**

### Text 53

*tattva-vyavadhāna māyāvāda duṣṭa mata*  
*kalira jañjāla ei śāstra asammata*

**Перевод:**

**“The nefarious philosophy of māyāvāda believes in the separation of tattvas (i.e.that kṛṣṇa-tattva and kṛṣṇa-nāma-tattva are separate). This polluted Kali-yuga misconception is not the verdict of the śāstra. (9)**

**Комментарий:**

(9) Varṇa-vyavadhāna (the separation of syllables) is like this – in the word ‘haṭhikari’ the first and last syllables create the word ‘hari’ but because ‘ṭhika separates it in the middle, the result of the Holy Name are obstructed. In the word ‘hārāma’ there is no separation. Thus, in hārāma, there is some hint (of the Name), which means it can give the results of liberation. Tattva-vyavadhāna (separation of spiritual truths) is extremely bad. In reality, there is no difference between kṛṣṇa-nāma and Kṛṣṇa. If the māyāvādīs say that they accept kṛṣṇa-nāma and Brahman as different, then one should know this imaginative speculation to be tattva-vyavadhāna. This destroys everything.

**Vyavadhānāviddha nāma-i śuddha-nāma (The Name which Cuts Through Separation is Śuddha–nāma)**

### Text 54

*ataeva śuddha kṛṣṇa-nāma yāṅra mukhe*  
*tāṅhāke vaiṣṇava jāni’ sadā sevi’ sukhe*

**Перевод:**

**“Therefore, whoever’s mouth chants pure kṛṣṇa-nāma, I consider them to be a Vaiṣṇava and I will serve them happily.**

**Anartha yata naṣṭa haya tata-i nāmābhāsatva dūra haya o cinmaya nāma prakāśa pāya (When Anarthas are Destroyed and Nāmābhāsa is Cast Far Away, the Divine Name Becomes Manifest)**

### Text 55

*nāmābhāsa bhedi’ śuddha-nāma labhibāre*  
*sad-guru sevibe jīva yatna-sahakāre*

**Перевод:**

**“Penetrating through nāmābhāsa, one attains śuddha-nāma. This occurs when the jīva serves a genuine guru with great care.**

### Text 56

*bhajane anartha-nāśa yei kṣaṇe pāya*  
*cit-svarūpa nāme nāce bhaktera jīhvāya*

**Перевод:**

**“When anarthas are destroyed in bhajana, at that moment the spiritual form of the Holy Name dances upon the devotee’s tongue.**

### Text 57

*nāma se amṛta-dhārā nāhi chāḍe āra*  
*nāma-rase matta jīva nāce anivāra*

**Перевод:**

**“Then, one cannot give up that stream of nectar flowing from the Holy Name. Intoxicated by that nāma-rasa (the nectar of the Name), the jīva dances incessantly.**

### Text 58

*nāma nāce, jīva nāce, nāce prema-dhana*  
*jagat nācāya, māyā kare palāyana*

**Перевод:**

**“The Name dances, the jīva dances, the treasure of prema dances. They cause the world to dance and māyā flees!**

**Yāhāra nāme śraddhā haya tāhāra-i nāme adhikāra haiyā thāke nāme sarva-śakti āche (Whoever has faith in the Holy Name is Eligible to Chant. All Potencies are Within the Name)**

### Text 59

*nāme adhikāra nara-mātre kaile dāna*  
*sarva-śakti nāme prabhu karile vidhāna*

**Перевод:**

**“Every human being has been given the eligibility to chant the Holy Name. The Lord has invested all His potencies within the Holy Name.**

### Text 60

*yā’ra śraddhā haya nāme, sei adhikārī*  
*yā’ra mukhe kṛṣṇa-nāma sei se ācārī*

**Перевод:**

**“Whoever has śraddhā in the Name is qualified. Whoever’s mouth chants kṛṣṇa-nāma is virtuous.**

**Deśa-kāla-aśaucādira vādhā nāme nāi (There are No Barriers Concerning Place, Time or Impurity etc. in Regards to the Name)**

### Text 61

*deśa-kāla-aśaucādi niyama sakāla*  
*śrī-nāma-grahaṇe nāi, nāma se prabala*

**Перевод:**

**“All rules concerning place, time, impurity etc. have no effect on Śrī Nāma, because the Holy Name is most powerful.**

**Kali-jīvera nāme niṣkapaṭa viśvāsa haile-i nāme adhikāra haila (The Jīvas of Kali-yuga who Have Firm Faith in the Holy Name, Have the Right to Chant the Name)**

### Text 62

*dāne, yajñe, snāne, jape āche ta vicāra*  
*kṛṣṇa-saṅkīrtane mātra śraddhā adhikāra*

**Перевод:**

**“There are considerations when offering charity, performing yajñas, taking bath at holy places, and chanting japa. But with kṛṣṇa-saṅkīrtana, one becomes qualified simply by śraddhā. (10)**

**Комментарий:**

(10) In giving charity and other pious acts one must consider the purity and impurity of the time, place, and circumstances. But in relation to kṛṣṇa-saṅkīrtana, only śraddhā makes one qualified and nothing else is considered.

### Text 63

*yuga-dharma hari-nāma ananya-śraddhāya*  
*ye kare āśraya tāra sarva-lābha haya*

**Перевод:**

**“Anyone who, with unswerving śraddhā, takes shelter of chanting hari-nāma which is the yuga-dharma for this age, attains everything.**

### Text 64

*kali-jīva niṣkapaṭe kṛṣṇera saṁsāre*  
*avasthita haye kṛṣṇa-nāma sadā kare*

**Перевод:**

**“The jīvas of Kali-yuga should enter Kṛṣṇa’s family with sincerity and constantly chant kṛṣṇa-nāma.**

**Nāmera anukūla viṣaya grahaṇa o pratikūla visaya varjana (Accepting those Things Favourable to the Holy Name and Rejecting those that are Unfavourable)**

### Text 65

*bhajanera anukūla sarva kārya kari’*  
*bhajanera pratikūla saba parihari’*

**Перевод:**

**“One should accept everything favourable for bhajana and reject everything that is unfavourable for bhajana.**

### Text 66

*kṛṣṇera saṁsāre thāki’ kāṭāye jīvana*  
*nirantara hari-nāma karena smaraṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“Remaining in Kṛṣṇa’s family, one should pass this life constantly remembering hari-nāma.**

**Ananya buddhite nāma grahaṇa karibe (Chanting the Holy Name with Unswerving Concentration)**

### Text 67

*āra kona dharma, karma kabhu nā karibe*  
*svatantra-īśvara-jñāne anye nā pūjibe*

**Перевод:**

**“One should not follow any other dharma or karma. One should not consider any controlling deity to be independent (of Kṛṣṇa) and worship them.**

### Text 68

*kṛṣṇa-nāma, bhakta-sevā satata karibe*  
*kṛṣṇa-prema-lābha tāra avaśya haibe*

**Перевод:**

**“One should always chant kṛṣṇa-nāma and serve the devotees, then one will certainly achieve kṛṣṇa-prema.”**

### Text 69

*hari-dāsa kāṅdi’ prabhu-caraṇe paḍiyā*  
*nāme anurāga māge caraṇa dhariyā*

**Перевод:**

**Weeping, Hari Dāsa fell at the Lord’s feet, and holding them, he begged Him for attachment to the Holy Name.**

### Text 70

*hari-dāsa-pade bhaktivinoda yāhāra*  
*hari-nāma cintāmaṇi jīvana tāhāra*

**Перевод:**

**That Hari-nāma Cintāmaṇi has become the very life of Bhaktvinoda who remains at the feet of Hari Dāsa.**

## Chapter 3. Nāmābhāsa Vicāra (A Deliberation on Nāmābhāsa)

### Text 1

*gadāi gaurāṅga jaya jāhnavā jīvana*  
*sītādvaita jaya śrīvāsādi bhakta-jana*

**Перевод:**

**Glories to Gadāi-Gaurāṅga, and to the life of Jāhnavā Devī (Nityānanda)! Glories to the husband of Sītā (Advaita)! Glories to Śrīvāsa and all the devotees!**

### Text 2

*hari-dāse mahāprabhu sadaya haiyā*  
*uṭhāya takhana padma-hasta prasāriyā*

**Перевод:**

**Mahāprabhu extended His lotus hand, and then raised Hari Dāsa up.**

### Text 3

*bale’ śuna hari-dāsa āmāra vacana*  
*nāmābhāsa spaṣṭa-rūpe bujhāo ekhana*

**Перевод:**

**He said, “Listen to My words, Hari Dāsa. Now explain nāmābhāsa clearly.**

### Text 4

*nāmābhāsa bujhāile nāma śuddha habe*  
*anāyāse jīva nāma-guṇe tare yābe*

**Перевод:**

**“One who understands nāmābhāsa will attain śuddha-nāma. Through the attributes of the Holy Name a jīva can easily be delivered.**

**Nāmābhāsa—megha kujjhaṭikā-rūpa ajñāna o anartha (Nāmābhāsa – Ignorance and Anarthas Are Like Clouds and Fog)**

### Text 5

*nāma sūrya-sama nāśe māyā andhakāra*  
*megha-kujjhaṭikā nāme ḍhāke bāra bāra*

**Перевод:**

**“The Holy Name, like the sun, destroys the darkness of māyā. However, again and again, clouds and fog cover the sun of the Holy Name.**

### Text 6

*jīvera ajñāna āra anartha sakala*  
*kujjhaṭikā-megha-rūpe haya ta’ prabala*

**Перевод:**

**“All the jīva’s ignorance and anarthas are like dense clouds and fog. (1)**

**Комментарий:**

(1) Kṛṣṇa and kṛṣṇa-nāma are non-different and are like a transcendental sun. They destroy the dark nature of māyā. Out of compassion for the baddha–jīvas, the sun of the Holy Name manifests in the world. The baddha-jīva’s ignorance is like fog. The baddha-jīva’s anarthas are like clouds which cover the sun of the Holy Name with darkness, and that darkness covers the eyes of the baddha-jīva. The sun is huge, so it cannot be covered. But when shadows fall upon the eyes of the jīva, it is said that the sun is covered.

### Text 7

*kṛṣṇa-nāma-sūrya citta-gagaṇe uṭhila*  
*kujjhaṭikā-megha punaḥ tāṅhāke ḍhākila*

**Перевод:**

**“When the sun of kṛṣṇa-nāma rises in the sky of the heart, once again the fog and clouds cover it.**

**Ajñāna kujjhaṭikā, svarūpa bhrama (The Fog of Ignorance Creates Confusion About Spiritual Reality)**

### Text 8

*nāmera ye cit-svarūpa tāhā nāhi jāne*  
*se ajñāna kujjhaṭikā andhakāra āne*

**Перевод:**

**“When one does not know the cit-svarūpa (transcendental nature) of the Holy Name, the fog of ignorance ushers in darkness.**

### Text 9

*kṛṣṇa sarveśvara bali’ nāhi jāne yei*  
*nānā deve pūji’ karma-mārge bhrame sei*

**Перевод:**

**“One who does not know Kṛṣṇa as Sarveśvara (the Controller of all things) will worship many Devas on the path of karma and become bewildered.**

### Text 10

*jīve cit-svarūpa bali’ nāhi yā’ra jñāna*  
*māyā jaḍāśraye tā’ra satata ajñāna*

**Перевод:**

**“The jīva who has no knowledge of his cit-svarūpa accepts the mundane shelter of māyā and always remains in ignorance.”**

### Text 11

*tabe hari-dāsa bale āja āmi dhanya*  
*mama-mukhe nāma kathā śunibe caitanya*

**Перевод:**

**Then Hari Dāsa said, “Today I am most fortunate. Śrī Caitanya will hear topics about the Holy Name from my mouth.**

### Text 12

*kṛṣṇa, jīva – prabhu, dāsa, jaḍātmikā-māyā*  
*ye nā jāne tā’ra śire ajñānera chāyā*

**Перевод:**

**“Kṛṣṇa and the jīva are master and servant. Māyā is characterised by gross matter. When one does not know this, he lives under the shadow of ignorance. (2)**

**Комментарий:**

(2) The Holy Name is transcendental by nature, Kṛṣṇa’s nature is that He has sovereignty over all things. The nature of the Devas is that they are all servants of Kṛṣṇa. The jīvas are transcendental by nature, māyā is inert matter, and when the jīvas do not comprehend these tattvas, they are in ignorance. Kṛṣṇa is the master, the jīva is the servant, and māyā is characterised by gross matter. When they understand this, the jīvas are no longer in ignorance.

**Megha-anartha – asat-tṛṣṇā, hṛdaya-daurbalya, aparādha (The Clouds of Anarthas – The Thirst for Material Things, Weakness of Heart and Offences)**

### Text 13

*asat-tṛṣṇā, hṛdaya-daurbalya, aparādha*  
*anartha e-saba megha-rūpe kare bādha*

**Перевод:**

**“The thirst for material things, weakness of heart, and offences – these anarthas are like clouds that create obstacles. (3)**

**Комментарий:**

(3) Asat-tṛṣṇā is the thirst for material things that have no connection to Kṛṣṇa, in other words, the greed for mundane objects. Asat-tṛṣṇā, hṛdaya-daurbalya (weakness of heart) and aparādha (offences) are the cloud-like anarthas of the jīva.

### Text 14

*nāma-sūrya-raśmi ḍhāke, nāmābhāsa haya*  
*svataḥ-siddha kṛṣṇa-nāme sadā ācchādaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Nāmābhāsa is present When the rays from the sun of the Holy Name are covered. However, pure kṛṣṇa-nāma, which is perfect within itself, always remains uncovered.**

**Nāmābhāsera avadhi (The End of Nāmābhāsa)**

### Text 15

*sambandha-tattvera jñāna yāvat nā haya*  
*tāvat se nāmābhāsa jīvera āśraya*

**Перевод:**

**“As long as he has no knowledge of sambandha-tattva (one’s relationship with Kṛṣṇa), the jīva takes shelter of nāmābhāsa.**

### Text 16

*sādhaka yadyapi pāya sad-guru āśraya*  
*bhajana-naipuṇye megha-ādi dūra haya*

**Перевод:**

**“If a sādhaka takes shelter of a genuine guru and becomes expert in bhajana, the clouds and fog are driven far away.**

**Sambandha, abhidheya, prayojana (Relationship, Method and Attainment)**

### Text 17

*megha-kujjhaṭikā gele nāma-divākara*  
*prakāśa haiyā bhakte dena prema-vara*

**Перевод:**

**“When the clouds and fog are gone, the Holy Name shines forth and manifests the highest type of prema, then awards that to the devotee.**

### Text 18

*sad-guru sambandha-jñāna kariyā arpaṇa*  
*abhidheya-rūpe karāna nāmānuśīlana*

**Перевод:**

**“A genuine guru bestows the knowledge of sambandha (one’s relationship with Kṛṣṇa). This leads to abhidheya (the method of attaining that relationship) wherein the Holy Name is cultivated.**

### Text 19

*nāma-sūrya svalpa-kāle prabala haiyā*  
*anartha-kujjhaṭikā dena tāḍāiyā*

**Перевод:**

**“In a short time, the sun of the Holy Name will shine forth brightly and dispels the fog of anarthas.**

### Text 20

*prayojana-tattva tabe dena prema-dhana*  
*prāpta-prema-jīva kare’ nāma-saṅkīrtana*

**Перевод:**

**“Then prayojana-tattva (knowledge concerning the highest attainment) gives the great treasure of prema, which is attained when the jīva performs nāma-saṅkīrtana.**

**Sambandha-jñāna (Knowledge of One’s Relationship with Kṛṣṇa)**

### Text 21

*sad-guru-caraṇe jīva śraddhā-sahakāre*  
*prathame sambandha-jñāna pāya suvicāre*

**Перевод:**

**“With śraddhā in the feet of a genuine guru, the jīva first receives the proper understanding of sambandha-jñāna.**

### Text 22

*kṛṣṇa nitya-prabhu āra jīva nitya-dāsa*  
*kṛṣṇa-prema nitya, jīva svabhāva prakāśa*

**Перевод:**

**“Kṛṣṇa is the eternal master and the jīvas are His eternal servants. Through kṛṣṇa-prema the eternal, intrinsic nature of the jīva manifests.**

### Text 23

*kṛṣṇa-nitya-dāsa jīva, tāhā vismariyā*  
*māyika jagate phire sukha anveṣiyā*

**Перевод:**

**“The jīva is an eternal servant of Kṛṣṇa, yet he forgets this. Being thrown into the māyika world, he searches for happiness.**

### Text 24

*māyika jagat haya jīva-kārāgāra*  
*jīvera vaimukhya-doṣe daṇḍa-pratikāra*

**Перевод:**

**“The māyika world is a prison house for the jīvas, where punishment is given to correct those jīvas who are infected by the defect of being averse (to Kṛṣṇa). (4)**

**Комментарий:**

(4) The fourteen worlds, known as Devī-dhāma, are a prison house for those jīvas who are averse to Kṛṣṇa. It is not a place for happiness or enjoyment. Here, material enjoyment is temporary, thus it is especially difficult. Punishment is the remedy, and through that punishment the jīvas are purified.

### Text 25

*tabe yadi jīva sādhu-vaiṣṇava-kṛpāya*  
*sambandha-jñānete punaḥ kṛṣṇa-nāma pāya*

**Перевод:**

**“However, by the mercy of a Vaiṣṇava sādhu, a jīva may attain sambandha-jñāna, along with kṛṣṇa-nāma (5)**

**Комментарий:**

(5) “I am finite consciousness and an eternal servant of Kṛṣṇa. Kṛṣṇa is Infinite consciousness and my only Master. This gross material world is a prison house meant for my purification.” This knowledge is known as sambandha-jñāna.

### Text 26

*tabe pāya prema-dhana sarva-dharma-sāra*  
*yāhāra nikaṭe sāyujyādira dhikkāra*

**Перевод:**

**“Then he attains the treasure of prema, which is the essence of all dharma. Such a person feels disgust towards impersonal liberation (sāyujyā) and the like.**

### Text 27

*yāvat sambandha-jñāna sthira nāhi haya*  
*tāvat anarthe nāmābhāsera āśraya*

**Перевод:**

**“When he has no steady knowledge of sambandha, then he will maintain anarthas and take shelter of nāmābhāsa. (6)**

**Комментарий:**

(6) Until, by the guru’s mercy, sambandha–jñāna arises, then the anartha of ignorance will remain with the jīva – thus, while he chants the Holy Name, it will be nāmābhāsa and not śuddha-nāma.

**Nāmābhāsera phala (The Result of Nāmābhāsa)**

### Text 28

*nāmābhāsa daśāte-o aneka maṅgala*  
*jīvera avaśya haya sukṛti prabala*

**Перевод:**

**“The stage of nāmābhāsa gives many auspicious results, and certainly the jīva attains great sukṛti. (7)**

**Комментарий:**

(7) Nāmābhāsa is counted as the best amongst the different kinds of puṇya a jīva can perform. In comparison with dharma, vows, yoga, fire sacrifices and all other types of auspicious activities, nāmābhāsa gives the best results.

### Text 29

*nāmābhāse naṣṭa haya āche pāpa yata*  
*nāmābhāse mukti haya kali haya hata*

**Перевод:**

**“By nāmābhāsa, pāpa is destroyed. By nāmābhāsa, liberation is achieved and the effects of Kali-yuga are halted.**

### Text 30

*nāmābhāse nara haya, supaṅkti-pāvana*  
*nāmābhāse sarva-roga haya nivāraṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“By nāmābhāsa, a man can purify merely by his presence. By nāmābhāsa, all diseases are cured.**

### Text 31

*sakala āśaṅkā nāmābhāse dūra haya*  
*nāmābhāsī sarvāriṣṭa haite śānti pāya*

**Перевод:**

**“All fears go far away from nāmābhāsa. All misfortunes are nullified for one who chants nāmābhāsa.**

### Text 32

*yakṣa, rakṣa, bhūta, preta, graha-samudaya*  
*nāmābhāse sakala anartha dūre yāya*

**Перевод:**

**“Yakṣas, rākṣasas, bhūtas, pretas, malefic planets and all anarthas go far away from nāmābhāsa.**

### Text 33

*narake patita loka sukhe mukti pāya*  
*samasta prārabdha-karma nāmābhāse yāya*

**Перевод:**

**“Those who have fallen into a hellish condition are liberated and all prārabdha-karma (results from previous activities) go away due to nāmābhāsa.**

### Text 34

*sarva-vedādhika, sarva-tīrtha haite baḍa*  
*nāmābhāsa sarva-śubha-karma-śreṣṭhatara*

**Перевод:**

**“Nāmābhāsa is superior to all the Vedas and all sacred tīrthas. Nāmābhāsa is the greatest of all auspicious karmika activities.**

**Nāmābhāsera vaikuṇṭhādi-prāpakatva (The Attainment of Vaikuṇṭha by Nāmābhāsa)**

### Text 35

*dharma, artha, kāma, mokṣa catur-varga-dātā*  
*sarva-śakti dhare nāmābhāse jīva-trātā*

**Перевод:**

**“Nāmābhāsa awards the four goals – dharma, artha, kāma and mokṣa (religiosity, wealth, sense enjoyment, and liberation). Being all-powerful, nāmābhāsa delivers the jīvas.**

### Text 36

*jagat-ānanda-kara śreṣṭha-pada-prada*  
*agatira eka gati sarva-śreṣṭha-pada*

**Перевод:**

**“It gives bliss to the world and grants the highest abode. For those without any shelter, it is the only shelter, and the best of all realms.**

### Text 37

*vaikuṇṭhādi loka-prāpti nāmābhāse haya*  
*viśeṣataḥ kali-yuge sarva-śāstra kaya*

**Перевод:**

**“One will attain Vaikuṇṭha and other spiritual abodes through nāmābhāsa. All the śāstra state that it is specifically for Kali-yuga.**

**Saṅketa, parihāsa, stobha o helā ei cāri-prakāra nāmābhāsa (Indirect Chanting, Joking, Disrespect and Neglect – these are the Four Types of Nāmābhāsa)**

### Text 38

*catur-vidha nāmābhāsa ei mātra jāni*  
*saṅketa o parihāsa, stobha, helā māni*

**Перевод:**

**“I am only aware of four types of nāmābhāsa. I know of saṅketa (indirect), parihāsa (joking), stobha (disrespect), and helā (neglect). (8)**

**Комментарий:**

(8) Saṅketa, parihāsa, stobha, and helā – these are the four types of nāmābhāsa when chanting the Holy Name. Thus, altogether there are four kinds of nāmābhāsa. From helā to stobha, from stobha to parihāsa, and from parihāsa to saṅketa, the faults become progressively less.

**Saṅketa-rūpa nāmābhāsera prakāra-dvaya (The Two Types of Saṅketa Nāmābhāsa, or Indirect Chanting)**

### Text 39-40

*viṣṇu-lakṣya kari’ jaḍa-buddhye nāma laya*  
*anya lakṣya kari’ viṣṇu-nāma uccāraya*

*saṅgete dvi-vidha ei haya nāmābhāsa*  
*ajāmila sākṣī tāra śāstrete prakāśa*

**Перевод:**

**“Chanting the Holy Name referring to Viṣṇu, but with a material conception, and unintentionally chanting the Holy Name of Viṣṇu – these are the two kinds of this nāmābhāsa. The śāstra reveals Ajāmila as an example (of the first one).**

### Text 41

*yavana-sakala mukta habe anāyāse*  
*‘hārām’ ‘hārām’ bali’ kahe nāmābhāse*

**Перевод:**

**“All the Yavanas are easily delivered when they say, ‘Hārām! Hārām!’ This is said to be nāmābhāsa (of the second type).**

### Text 42

*anyatra saṅkete yadi haya nāmābhāsa*  
*tathāpi nāmera teja nā haya vināśa*

**Перевод:**

**“Even if this nāmābhāsa is referring to something else (other than Kṛṣṇa), the potency of the Holy Name is not destroyed.**

**Parihāsa-nāmābhāsa (Chanting in Jest)**

### Text 43

*parihāse kṛṣṇa-nāma yei jana kare*  
*jarāsandha sama sei e-saṁsāre tare*

**Перевод:**

**“That person who chants kṛṣṇa-nāma in jest, just like Jarāsandha, crosses over this world of birth and death.**

**Stobha-nāmābhāsa (Chanting Disrespectfully)**

### Text 44

*aṅga-bhaṅgī caidya sama kare nāmābhāsa*  
*stobha mātra haya tabu nāśe bhava-pāśa*

**Перевод:**

**“While making angry gestures, even Caidya (Śiśupāla) chanted nāmābhāsa by chanting with disrespect and was still able to slash the bonds of this material world.**

**Helā-nāmābhāsa (Chanting with Negligence)**

### Text 45

*mana nāhi deya āra avajñā bhāvete*  
*‘kṛṣṇa’ ‘rāma’ bale helā-nāmābhāsa tā’te*

**Перевод:**

**“Absentmindedly and with a disrespectful mood, when one chants ‘Kṛṣṇa’ and ‘Rāma,’ that is helā–nāmābhāsa (chanting negligently).**

### Text 46

*ei saba nāmābhāse mleccha-gaṇa tare*  
*viṣayī, alasa-jana ei patha dhare*

**Перевод:**

**“All these kinds of nāmābhāsa can deliver the mlecchas. Those persons who are materialists and lazy follow this path.**

**Śraddhā o helā-nāmābhāsera bheda (Chanting with Śraddhā and Chanting with Negligence as Two Types of Nāmābhāsa)**

### Text 47

*śraddhā kari kare nāma anartha-sahita*  
*śraddhā-nāma haya sei tomāra vihita*

**Перевод:**

**“You have ordained that when one chants with śraddhā but also with anarthas, this is śraddhā–nāmābhāsa.**

### Text 48

*saṅketādi avajñā paryanta bhāva dhari’*  
*nāma kare helāya ye śraddhā parihari*

**Перевод:**

**“Beginning with saṅketa, where one chants in a disrespectful mood, up to chanting the Holy Name with helā – this is chanting without śraddhā.**

### Text 49

*nāmābhāsa avadhi se helā-nāma haya*  
*tāhāte-o mukti labhe pāpa haya kṣaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Helā–nāmābhāsa is limited to destroying the results of pāpa and awarding liberation. (9)**

**Комментарий:**

(9) By chanting the Holy Name negligently, one attains results up to the point of liberation. But one cannot express what the results of chanting the Holy Name negligently with śraddhā are! When śraddhā in the Holy Name appears along with sambandha-jñāna, the result of that will be the rise of rati (affection for Kṛṣṇa). Anarthas are removed very quickly with śraddhā–nāmābhāsa.

**Anartha-nāśe nāmābhāsa nāma haiyā prema deya (Nāmābhāsa Destroys Anarthas and Gives Rise to Prema)**

### Text 50

*kṛṣṇa-prema chāḍi’ saba nāmābhāse pāya*  
*nāmābhāse punaḥ śuddha-nāma ha’ye yāya*

**Перевод:**

**“Nāmābhāsa can give everything except kṛṣṇa-prema. By progressing with nāmābhāsa, one eventually comes to śuddha-nāma.**

### Text 51

*anartha-vigate yabe śuddha-nāma haya*  
*kṛṣṇa-prema tabe tā’ra haibe niścaya*

**Перевод:**

**“When anarthas have gone, śuddha-nāma appears. Then kṛṣṇa-prema certainly manifests.**

### Text 52

*nāmābhāse sākṣāt se prema dite nāre*  
*nāma haye prema deya vidhi-anusāre*

**Перевод:**

**“Nāmābhāsa itself cannot give prema directly. But by following the proper procedures, the Holy Name will give prema.**

**Nāmābhāsa o nāmāparādhera bheda (The Difference Between Nāmābhāsa and Nāmāparādha)**

### Text 53

*ataeva nāma-aparādha parihari*  
*nāmābhāsa kare yei tā’re nati kari*

**Перевод:**

**“Thus, I offer my respects to anyone who avoids the offences to the Holy Name and chants nāmābhāsa.**

### Text 54

*karma, jñāna haite ananta śreṣṭhatara*  
*bali’ nāmābhāse jāni, ohe sarveśvara*

**Перевод:**
