**“O Sarveśvara, I declare that nāmābhāsa is unlimitedly superior to karma and jñāna.**

### Text 55

*rati-mūlā śraddhā yadi śuddha-bhāve haya*  
*tabe ta’ viśuddha-nāma haibe udaya*

**Перевод:**

**“If (through nāmābhāsa) there is śraddhā, which is the foundation of rati, one will become purified. Then the pure Name will arise.**

**Chāyā o pratibimba bhede ābhāsa dui prakāra – chāyā nāmābhāsa (Chāya and Pratibimba as the Two Kinds of Ābhāsa – Chāya Nāmābhāsa)**

### Text 56

*ābhāsa dvividha haya pratibimba chāyā*  
*śraddhābhāsa dvi-prakāra saba tava māyā*

**Перевод:**

**“There are two kinds of ābhāsa (glimmers) – pratibimba (reflection) and chāyā (shadow). These two types create śraddhābhāsa (a glimmer of śraddhā). These are all Your māyā potency.**

### Text 57

*chāyā-śraddhābhāse chāyā-nāmābhāsa haya*  
*sei nāmābhāse jīvera śubha prasavaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Chāyā-śraddhābhāsa (a shadow of a glimmer of śraddhā) produces chāyā-nāmābhāsa (a shadow of a glimmer of the Holy Name). This nāmābhāsa creates auspiciousness for the jīvas. (10)**

**Комментарий:**

(10) In many places in the śāstra we find words like nāmābhāsa (a glimmer of the Holy Name), vaiṣṇavābhāsa (a glimmer of a Vaiṣṇava), śraddhābhāsa (a glimmer of faith), bhāvābhāsa (a glimmer of ecstasy), ratyābhāsa (a glimmer of attraction), premābhāsa (a glimmer of prema), muktyābhāsa (a glimmer of liberation) etc. In all these, the word ābhāsa (glimmer) is most appropriate. These will be discussed in this chapter. The natural explanation of ābhāsa is of two types – svarūpābhāsa (a direct glimmer) and pratibimbābhāsa (a reflected glimmer). In svarūpābhāsa, direct light appears in a diminished way, just as there is dim light when clouds cover the effulgence of the sun. In pratibimbābhāsa, natural light appears in a distorted way. Thus it is said, ābhāsas tu mṛṣā buddhir avidyā-kāryam ucyate (‘A distorted reflection of knowledge is known as ignorance’). Just as light is reflected upon water, when the sun of the Holy Name is covered by the fog and clouds of the jīva’s ignorance, the contracted light of that sun can only be seen very faintly. This state of nāmābhāsa awards many auspicious results to the world. When the light of the Holy Name is reflected in the lake of māyāvāda, that is pratibimba-nāmābhāsa. From this, sāyujya (impersonal liberation) and other results may arise, but the ultimate benefit of prema will not manifest. This kind of nāmābhāsa is actually a great offence to the Holy Name. Because of this, it is not said to be real nāmābhāsa. Only chāyā-nāmābhāsa is said to be actual nāmābhāsa, and that has four divisions. This contemptible pratibimba-nāmābhāsa should be driven far away. It is seen that all the śāstra worship chāyā-nāmābhāsa. Chāyā-nāmābhāsa comes from anarthas born of ignorance, anarthas stemming from evil philosophies create pratibimba-nāmābhāsa which is considered an offence that obstructs bhakti. A Vaiṣṇava–prāya, or Vaiṣṇavābhāsa, can be respected as a kaniṣṭha or prākṛta-bhakta (materialistic devotee) as long as he does not commit the offence of following māyāvāda. Why not? By proper association he can quickly attain auspiciousness. Therefore, śuddha-bhaktas will befriend him and have mercy on him as if he were a child. They will not ignore him as they do the hateful māyāvādīs. One who only has ordinary śraddhā is first engaged only in Deity worship and, as he advances through pūjā, he engages in serving the devotees and Bhagavān. Then in this way, he develops sambandha–jñāna and attains the gift of bhakti. But if someone is seen to have staunch faith in māyāvāda, then he should certainly be ignored.

**Pratibimba nāmābhāsa (A Reflected Glimmer of the Holy Name)**

### Text 58

*anya jīve śuddhā-śraddhā kariyā darśana*  
*nija-mane śraddhābhāsa āne yei jana*

**Перевод:**

**“One who observes pure śraddhā in other jīvas may attain śraddhābhāsa within.**

### Text 59

*bhoga-mokṣa-vāñchā tāhe thāke nitya miśi*  
*aśrame abhīṣṭa-lābhe yate divā-niśi*

**Перевод:**

**“They may also desire bhoga (sense enjoyment) or mokṣa (liberation) and strive day and night in order to attain them.**

### Text 60

*śraddhāra lakṣaṇa mātra śraddhā tāhe naya*  
*tā’ke pratibimba-śraddhābhāsa śāstre kaya*

**Перевод:**

**“There may be external symptoms of śraddhā, but it is not actual śraddhā. The śāstra says that this is pratibimba-śraddhābhāsa.**

### Text 61

*pratibimba-śraddhābhāse nāmābhāsa yata*  
*pratibimba-nāmābhāsa haya avirata*

**Перевод:**

**“Wherever there is pratibimba-śraddhābhāsa, there will always be pratibimba-nāmābhāsa.**

**Pratibimba-nāmābhāse māyāvāda kapaṭatā utpanna kare (The Deception of Māyāvāda is born from Pratibimba Nāmābhāsa)**

### Text 62

*ei nāmābhāse māyāvāda duṣṭa-mata*  
*praveśiyā śaṭhatāya haya pariṇata*

**Перевод:**

**“This kind of nāmābhāsa leads to the evil philosophy of māyāvāda and, entering into that, one becomes a deceiver.**

**Kapaṭa pratibimba-nāmābhāsa nāmāparādha (This Deceptive Pratibimba Nāmābhāsa is Actually Nāmāparādha)**

### Text 63

*nitya-sādhya nāme sādhana buddhi kari’*  
*nāmera mahimā nāśi’ aparādhe mari*

**Перевод:**

**“One who thinks that the Holy Name is simply a means to attain a higher eternal goal, disgraces the glories of the Holy Name and that offence destroys him.**

**Перевод:**

**Translator’s Note: The Holy Name is both the means and the end.**

**Chāyā nāmābhāsa o pratibimba-nāmābhāsera bheda (The Difference Between Chāyā Nāmābhāsa and Pratibimba Nāmābhāsa)**

### Text 64

*chāyā-nāmābhāse mātra hayata ajñāna*  
*hṛdaya-daurbalya haite anartha-vidhāna*

**Перевод:**

**“Chāyā-nāmābhāsa only comes from ignorance, and due to a state of hṛdaya-daurbalya (weakness of heart).**

### Text 65

*sei saba doṣe nāma karena mārjana*  
*pratibimba-nāmābhāse doṣera vardhana*

**Перевод:**

**“The Holy Name cleanses all such faults. But pratibimba-nāmābhāsa increases those defects.**

**Māyāvāda o bhakti – iṅhārā paraspara viparīta dharma māyāvāda-i aparādha (Māyāvāda and Bhakti – How Māyāvāda Philosophy is the Opposite to this and is an Offence.)**

### Text 66

*kṛṣṇa-nāma-rūpa-guṇa-līlādi sakala*  
*māyāvādi-mate mithyā, naśvara sakala*

**Перевод:**

**“All the names, forms, qualities, and pastimes etc. of Kṛṣṇa are considered by the māyāvādīs to be false and temporary.**

### Text 67

*sei mate prema-tattva nitya nāhi haya*  
*bhakti-viparīta māyāvāda suniścaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Their opinion is that the principle of prema is not eternal. Undoubtedly, māyāvāda is the complete opposite of bhakti.**

### Text 68

*bhakti-vairī madhye māyāvādera gaṇana*  
*ataeva māyāvādī aparādhī hana*

**Перевод:**

**“Māyāvāda is counted amongst the enemies of bhakti. Thus, māyāvādīs are offenders.**

### Text 69

*māyāvādī-mukhe nāma nāhi bāhirāya*  
*nāma bāhirāya, tabu nāmatva nā pāya*

**Перевод:**

**“The Holy Name does not come from the mouth of a māyāvādī. If the Name does emerge from it, it is not the actual Holy Name.**

### Text 70

*māyāvādī yadi kare nāma-uccāraṇa*  
*nāmake anitya bali’ labhaye patana*

**Перевод:**

**“If a māyāvādī chants the Holy Name, considering that it is not eternal, he will fall down.**

### Text 71

*nāmera nikaṭe bhoga-mokṣera prārthanā*  
*nāmera nikaṭe śāṭhya phalete yātanā*

**Перевод:**

**“He may pray to the Holy Name for sense enjoyment or liberation, but the Holy Name cheats him and instead the result is only misery.**

**Māyāvādīra aparādha kakhana chāḍe (When the Māyāvādī Abandons His Offence)**

### Text 72

*tabe yadi māyāvādī bhukti-mukti-āśa*  
*chāḍiyā karaye nāma ha’ye kṛṣṇa-dāsa*

**Перевод:**

**“However, if a māyāvādī gives up the desire for sense enjoyment and liberation and chants the Holy Name, then he will become kṛṣṉa-dāsa.**

### Text 73

*tabe tā’re chāḍe māyāvāda duṣṭa-mata*  
*anutāpa-saha haya nāma anugata*

**Перевод:**

**“Then he will reject the evil philosophy of māyāvāda, and feeling remorse, will become a follower of the Holy Name.**

### Text 74

*sādhu-saṅge kare punaḥ śravaṇa, kīrtana*  
*su-sambandha-jñāna tā’ra ude’ tatakṣaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“In the company of sādhus he will constantly hear and chant, and true sambandha-jñāna will emerge.**

### Text 75

*aviśrānta nāma kare’ paḍe cakṣu-jala*  
*nāma-kṛpā pāya, citta haya ta sabala*

**Перевод:**

**“He will never tire of the Holy Name and tears will fall from his eyes. By attaining the mercy of the Holy Name, his heart will become very strong.**

**Bhaktike anitya baliyā māyāvāda aparādha haiyāche (By Stating that Bhakti is Not Eternal, Māyāvāda is Offensive)**

### Text 76

*kṛṣṇa-rūpa, kṛṣṇa-dāsya, jīvera svabhāva*  
*māyāvāda anitya kalpita bale saba*

**Перевод:**

**“Māyāvada claims that Kṛṣṇa’s form, and servitorship to Kṛṣṇa which is the jīva’s inherent position, are completely imaginary and temporary.**

### Text 77

*hena māyāvāda nāma-aparāde gaṇi*  
*māyāvāda haya sarva vipadera khani*

**Перевод:**

**“Therefore I count māyāvāda to be among the offences to the Holy Name. Māyāvāda is the repository of all other types of misfortune.**

**Māyāvādī nāmābhāse muktyābhāsa-rūpe sāyujya lābha kare (By Nāmābhāsa the Māyāvādī Attains Sāyujya, the Glimmer of Liberation)**

### Text 78

*nāmābhāsa kalpa-taru māyāvādi-jane*  
*abhīṣṭa arpaṇa kare sāyujya-nirvāṇe*

**Перевод:**

**“Like a desire tree, nāmābhāsa gives the māyāvādīs the liberation of sāyujya-nirvāṇa that they crave.**

### Text 79

*sarva-śakti nāme āche tāi nāmābhāse*  
*pratibimba haile-o dena muktyābhāse*

**Перевод:**

**“When the Holy Name, which contains all potencies, is reflected as pratimbimba-nāmābhāsa, it gives muktyābhāsa (a glimmer of liberation).**

### Text 80

*pañca-vidha mukti madhye sāyujya ābhāsa*  
*bhava-kleśa nāśe mātra phale sarva-nāśa*

**Перевод:**

**“Amongst the five types of mukti, sāyujya (impersonal liberation) is an ābhāsa (a glimmer of liberation). It eliminates all suffering, but results in destroying everything.**

**Māyāvādī nitya-sukha pāya nā (A Māyāvādī Never Attains Eternal Bliss)**

### Text 81

*māyāya mohita jana tāhe sukha māne*  
*sukhābhāsa mātra pāya sāyujya-nirvāṇe*

**Перевод:**

**“Baffled by māyā, such persons think that they will achieve bliss. When they attain sāyujya-nirvāṇa, they only attain sukhābhāsa (a glimmer of happiness).**

### Text 82

*sac-cit-ānanda-sevā parama nirvṛti*  
*sāyujya nā pāya kabhu hata-kṛṣṇa-smṛti*

**Перевод:**

**“Service unto He who is eternal, blissful, and cognizant is the highest kind of liberation. But that is never achieved by attaining sāyujya, where one’s remembrance of Kṛṣṇa is erased.**

### Text 83

*yāṅhā nāhi bhakti-prema-nityatā viśvāsa*  
*nitya-sukha kaiche tāhe haibe prakāśa*

**Перевод:**

**“How can everlasting happiness ever be revealed to one who believes that bhakti and prema are transitory?**

**Chāyā-nāmābhāsī duṣṭa-mate nā praveśa karile krame śuddha=nāma pāiyā thākena (One Who Chants Chāyā Nāmābhāsa Does Not Follow Deviant Philosophies, But Eventually Attains Śuddha-Nāma)**

### Text 84

*chāyā-nāmābhāsī nāhi jāne duṣṭa-mata*  
*matavāde citta-bale nahe tā’ra hata*

**Перевод:**

**“One who chants chāyā-nāmābhāsa does not accept deviant philosophies. His strong mind is not ruined by concocted theories.**

### Text 85

*se kevala nāhi jāne yathārtha prabhāva*  
*se prabhāva jñāna-dāna nāmera svabhāva*

**Перевод:**

**“He cannot understand the strength of the Holy Name. It is the very nature of the Name to award knowledge.**

### Text 86

*meghācchanne sūrya-prabhā pratīta nā haya*  
*kintu meghe nāśi’ sūrya karena udaya*

**Перевод:**

**“When the sunlight is covered by clouds it cannot be displayed. But when the clouds dissipate, the sun appears.**

### Text 87

*chāyā-nāmābhāsī dhanya sad-guru-prabhāve*  
*alpa-dine nāma-prema anāyāse pābe*

**Перевод:**

**“When one who chants chāyā-nāmābhāsa is empowered by a genuine guru, then after a few days he attains nāma-prema without any effort.**

**Bhaktera māyāvādīra saṅga avaśya parityājya (Devotees Must Certainly Abandon the Company of Māyāvādīs)**

### Text 88

*māyāvādī-saṅga teṅha satarke chāḍiyā*  
*śuddha-nāma-parāyaṇa tuṣibe seviyā*

**Перевод:**

**“Someone who carefully avoids the company of māyāvādīs should render service unto those who are attached to śuddha-nāma.**

### Text 89

*ei ta tomāra ājñā śrī-kṛṣṇa-caitanya*  
*sei ājñā yei pāle sei jīva dhanya*

**Перевод:**

**“O Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya, that jīva who follows Your instructions is most fortunate.**

### Text 90

*ye nā pāle tava-ājñā, sei jīva chāra*  
*koṭī janme kichutei nā habe uddhāra*

**Перевод:**

**“That jīva who does not follow Your instructions is useless and contemptible. He cannot be delivered even after millions of births.**

### Text 91

*ku-saṅga chāḍiye prabhu rākha tava pāya*  
*tava pāda-padma vinā nā dekhi upāya*

**Перевод:**

**“O Prabhu, by Your protection one can avoid bad association. Other than Your lotus feet, I see no other way.”**

### Text 92

*hari-dāsa pada-dvandve vinoda yāhāra*  
*hari-nāma-cintāmaṇi sadā gāna tāra*

**Перевод:**

**One who takes pleasure in the two feet of Hari Dāsa, continuously sings this Hari-nāma Cintāmaṇi.**

## Chapter 4. Nāmāparādha – Sādhu-nindā (Offences to the Holy Name – Criticising Sādhus)

*satāṁ nindā nāmnaḥ paramam aparādhaṁ vitanute*  
*yataḥ khyātiṁ yātaṁ katham u sahate tad‑vigarhām*

**Criticising devotees is the greatest offence that one can make to the Holy Name.**  
**How can the Name tolerate condemnation of those who spread His glories?**

### Text 1

*gadādhara-prāṇa jaya jāhnavā-jīvana*  
*jaya sītā-nātha śrīvāsādi bhakta-jana*

**Перевод:**

**Glories to the Life of Gadādhara (Mahāprabhu) and to the Life of Jāhnavā (Nityānanda)! Glories to the Lord of Sītā (Advaita) Glories to Śrīvāsa and the devotees.**

### Text 2

*prabhu bale ‘hari-dāsa’ – ebe sa-vistāra*  
*nāma-aparādha vyākhyā kara ataḥpara*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord said, “Hari Dāsa, now explain in detail the offences to the Holy Name.”**

### Text 3

*haridāsa bale – ‘prabhu’ more yā balābe*  
*tāhāi baliba āmi tomāra prabhāve*

**Перевод:**

**Hari Dāsa said, “Lord, by Your potency, I will say whatever You make me say.**

**Daśa-vidha nāmāparādha (The Ten Types of Offences to the Holy Name)**

### Text 4

*nāma-aparādha daśa-vidha śāstre kaya*  
*sei aparādhe mora baḍa haya bhaya*

**Перевод:**

**“In the śāstra it is said that there are ten types of offences to the Holy Name. I greatly fear these offences. (1)**

**Комментарий:**

(1) These are the ten offences – (1) Criticising Sādhus. (2) Considering the Devas to be independent, and considering Kṛṣṇa’s name, form, qualities and pastimes to be different from Kṛṣṇa Himself. (3) Disregarding the guru who reveals nāma-tattva. (4) Offending the śāstra that describes the glories of the Holy Name. (5) Creating imaginary meanings for the Holy Name when the śāstra has already described the glories and results of the Holy Name. (6) Performing pāpa on the strength of chanting the Name. (7) Giving instructions on the Name to those who lack śraddhā. (8) Equating hari-nāma with mundane auspicious activities. (9) Being inattentive when chanting the Holy Name. (10) Remaining addicted to the concept of “I and mine” and showing no love for the Name, even after knowing His glories.

### Text 5

*eka eka kari’ āmi baliba sakala*  
*aparādhe vāṅci yā’te deha more bala*

**Перевод:**

**“Please give me the strength so that I can speak about all these offences one by one**

### Text 6-8

*sādhu-nindā, anya-deva svātantrya manana*  
*nāma-tattva-guru āra śāstra-vinindana*

*hari-nāme artha-vāda, kalpita manana*  
*nāma-bale pāpa, śraddhā-hīne nāmārpaṇa*

*anya śubha-karmera samāna kṛṣṇa-nāma*  
*e-kathā mānile aparādha aviśrāma*

**Перевод:**

**“Criticising sādhus, considering the various Devas to be independent (of Kṛṣṇa), showing disrespect to the nāma-tattva-guru and the śāstra, creating a mundane interpretation of hari-nāma, or considering the Name to be imaginary, performing pāpa on the strength of the Name, giving the Holy Name to those without śraddhā, and equating kṛṣṇa-nāma with auspicious material activities. I will now continue to explain these offences.**

### Text 9

*nāmete anavadhāna haya aparādha*  
*tāhāke purāṇa-kartā balena pramāda*

**Перевод:**

**“The author of the Purāṇas (Vyāsa) states that anyone who is inattentive to these offences is insane.**

### Text 10

*nāmera māhātmya jāne tabu nāhi bhaje*  
*ahaṁ-mama-āsaktite saṁsārete maje*

**Перевод:**

**“One who knows the glories of the Holy Name but does not worship Him, and remains attached to the concept of \`I’ and \`mine’ becomes immersed in the world of repeated birth and death.**

**Sādhu-nindāi prathama aparādha (The First Offence – Criticising Sādhus)**

### Text 11

*sādhu-nindā prathamāparādha bali’ jāni*  
*ei aparādhe jīvera haya sarva-hāni*

**Перевод:**

**“Now I will speak about the first offence – criticising sādhus. By this offence everything is ruined for the jīva.**

**Svarūpa o taṭastha lakṣaṇa-bhede sādhu lakṣaṇa-dvaya vicāra (Deliberating Upon the Two Symptoms of a Sādhu – the Natural and Marginal Qualities)**

### Text 12

*sādhura lakṣaṇa tumi baliyācha prabho*  
*ekādaśe uddhavere kṛṣṇa-rūpe vibho*

**Перевод:**

**“O Lord, in the form of Kṛṣṇa, You have explained the qualities of a sādhu to Uddhava in the Eleventh Canto (of the Bhāgavatam).**

## Text 13-16\<strong\> \</strong\>

*dayālu, sahiṣṇu, sama, droha-śūnya-vrata*  
*satya-sāra, viśuddhātmā, parahite rata*

*kāme akṣubhita buddhi, dānta, akiñcana*  
*mṛdu, śuci, parimita-bhojī, śānta-mana*

*anīha, dhṛtimān, sthira, kṛṣṇaika-śaraṇa*  
*apramatta, sugambhīra, vijita-ṣaḍ-guṇa*

*amānī, mānada, dakṣa, avañcaka, jñānī*  
*ei saba lakṣaṇete sādhu bali jāni*

**Перевод:**

**“He is compassionate, tolerant, impartial, devoid of malice, the very essence of truthfulness, pure-hearted, benevolent to others, unagitated by desires, intelligent, mild-mannered, disinterested in material possessions, polite, clean, regulated in his eating, peaceful at heart, without worldly desires, sense-controlled, exclusively surrendered to Kṛṣṇa, vigilant, very sober, victorious over the six urges, prideless, respectful, expert, non-duplicit and wise. I declare that I know a sādhu to have all these symptoms.**

### Text 17

*ei saba lakṣaṇa prabho haya dvi-prakāra*  
*svarūpa-taṭastha-bhede kariba vicāra*

**Перевод:**

**“O Lord, all these qualities are of two kinds – svarūpa (natural) and taṭastha (marginal). Now I will describe them both. (2)**

**Комментарий:**

(2) Direct attributes found within an object are called its svarūpa-lakṣaṇa. Other qualities in relation to that object, which are like ‘guests’ that may arise in that object, are known as taṭastha-lakṣaṇa.

**Svarūpa-lakṣaṇai pradhāna lakṣaṇa, tad-āśraye taṭastha-lakṣaṇa udaya haya (The Svarūpa Lakṣaṇa is the Principle Quality, and When Taṭastha Lakṣaṇas Arise, They Take Shelter of That)**

### Text 18

*kṛṣṇaika-śaraṇa haya svarūpa-lakṣaṇa*  
*taṭastha-lakṣaṇe anya guṇera gaṇana*

**Перевод:**

**“Exclusive surrender to Kṛṣṇa is the only svarūpa-lakṣaṇa. All other qualities are counted as taṭastha-lakṣaṇa.**

### Text 19

*kona bhāgye sādhu-saṅge nāme ruci haya*  
*kṛṣṇa-nāma gāya kare kṛṣṇa-pādāśraya*

**Перевод:**

**“When by good fortune someone attains the company of sādhus and a taste for the Holy Name, they chant kṛṣṇa-nāma and take shelter of Kṛṣṇa’s feet.**

### Text 20

*svarūpa-lakṣaṇa sei haita haila*  
*gāite gāite nāma anya-guṇa āila*

**Перевод:**

**“This is how the svarūpa-lakṣaṇa appears. By continuous chanting of the Holy Name, other good qualities come.**

### Text 21

*anya guṇa-gaṇa tāi taṭastha gaṇana*  
*avaśya vaiṣṇava dehe habe saṅghaṭana*

**Перевод:**

**“All other qualities are counted as taṭastha. They will certainly appear in the body of a Vaiṣṇava.**

**Varṇāśrama liṅga, nānā-prakāra veśa dvārā sādhutva haya nā, kṛṣṇaika-śaraṇatāi sādhura-lakṣaṇa (One Does not Become a Sādhu by the External Signs of Varṇāśrama and Various Kinds of Clothing – the Symptom of a Sādhu is Exclusive Surrender to Kṛṣṇa)**

### Text 22

*varṇāśrama cihna nānā-****veṣera*** *racanā*  
*sādhura lakṣaṇe kabhu nā haya gaṇanā*

**Перевод:**

**“Varṇāśrama is comprised of various kinds of clothing and paraphernalia, but these are not counted amongst the qualities of a sādhu.**

### Text 23

*śrī-kṛṣṇa-śaraṇāgati sādhura lakṣaṇa*  
*tāra mukhe haya kṛṣṇa-nāma-saṅkīrtana*

**Перевод:**

**“The quality of a sādhu is śrī-kṛṣṇa-śaraṇāgati (surrender unto Śrī Kṛṣṇa). From his mouth comes kṛṣṇa-nāma-saṅkīrtana.**

### Text 24

*gṛhī, brahmacārī, vānaprastha, nyāsi-bhede*  
*śūdra, vaiśya, kṣatra, vipra-gaṇera prabhede*

**Перевод:**

**“Gṛhī, brahmacārī, vānaprastha, and nyāsī are the first division (of varṇāśrama). Śūdra, vaiśya, kṣatra, and vipra are the second division. (3)**

**Комментарий:**

(3) Those who marry within their own varṇa are called gṛhasthas. Those who practise celibacy before marriage are called brahmacārīs. One who leaves for the forest in his old age is called a vānaprastha. One who becomes renounced and leaves his home is called a nyāsī or a sannyāsī.

### Text 25

*sādhutva kakhana nāhi haibe nirṇīta*  
*kṛṣṇaika-śaraṇa sādhu śāstrera vihita*

**Перевод:**

**“When one has the qualities of a sādhu, one’s position is not determined (by these divisions of varṇāśrama). The śāstra ordains that one is a sādhu due to his exclusive surrender to Kṛṣṇa.**

**Gṛha-sādhu-lakṣaṇa (The Symptoms of a Householder Sādhu)**

### Text 26

*raghunātha-dāse lakṣye kariyā sevāra*  
*gṛhi-sādhu-jane śikhāyecha ei sāra*

**Перевод:**

**“On account of his service, You taught Raghunātha Dāsa the essence of how to be a householder sādhu. (4)**

**Комментарий:**

(4) Raghunātha Dāsa was the ornament on the head of the Kāyastha dynasty. A native of Saptagrāma, he is known as ‘Dāsa Gosvāmī’ and is counted amongst the Six Gosvāmīs.

### Text 27

*sthira haye ghare yāo, nā hao vātula*  
*krame krame pāya loka bhava-sindhu-kula*

**Перевод:**

**“(You told him) ‘Be peaceful! Go home! Don’t become a madman! Eventually a person can cross beyond the shores of the ocean of birth and death.**

### Text 28

*markaṭa-vairāgya chāḍa loka dekhāiyā*  
*yathāyogya viṣaya bhuñja anāsakta hañā*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Give up monkey-like renunciation and showing off to the public. Whatever material things are appropriate, for now you should accept them without attachment. (5)**

**Комментарий:**

(5) A person in whose heart steady renunciation has not yet appeared, but who externally wears kaupīna, sannyāsa cloth etc, bears the markings of a markaṭa-vairāgi (a monkey renunciant).

### Text 29

*antare niṣṭhā kara bāhye loka-vyavahāra*  
*acire śrī-kṛṣṇa tomāya karibe uddhāra*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Deep within yourself remain focused, but externally you may behave like an ordinary man. Very soon, Śrī Kṛṣṇa will save you.’**

**Gṛha-tyāgī sādhu-lakṣaṇa (The Qualities of the Sādhu Who has Renounced Household Life)**

### Text 30

*punaḥ tumi tā’ra dekhi vairāgya-grahaṇa*  
*ei mata śikṣā dile apūrva śravaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“Later, when you saw that he had accepted renunciation, You gave him these instructions which are wonderful to hear –**

### Text 31

*grāmya-kathā nā śunibe, grāmya-vārtā nā kahibe*  
*bhāla nā khāibe āra bhāla nā paribe*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Do not listen to common talk and do not speak such words. Do not eat luxuriously nor dress nicely.**

### Text 32

*amānī, mānada, kṛṣṇa-nāma sadā labe*  
*vraje rādhā-kṛṣṇa-sevā mānase karibe*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Do not expect any honour, but offer all respect to others. Always chant kṛṣṇa-nāma, and within your mind render service to Rādhā-Krṣṇa in Vraja.’**

**Gṛhī o gṛha-tyāgīra ubhayera-i svarūpa lakṣaṇa (The One Quality Shared by Both the Householder and Renunciate)**

### Text 33

*svarūpa-lakṣaṇa eka sarvatra samāna*  
*āśramādi bhede pṛthak taṭastha vidhāna*

**Перевод:**

**“The svarūpa-lakṣaṇa is the one quality that is respected everywhere. The differences of āśrama etc. are taṭastha.**

### Text 34

*ananya-śaraṇe yadi dekhi durācāra*  
*tathāpi se sādhu bali sevya sabākāra*

**Перевод:**

**“If I see someone who has completely surrendered (to Kṛṣṇa) behaving badly, I will still say, ‘He is a sādhu.’ Everyone should serve him. (6)**

**Комментарий:**

(6) Taking exclusive refuge in Kṛṣṇa and no one is else is the svarūpa-lakṣaṇa of bhakti. Whoever has this certainly has taṭastha-lakṣaṇas. But if someone takes exclusive shelter of Kṛṣṇa, the taṭastha-lakṣaṇas may only manifest in him partly, and not completely – thus it is observed that some misconduct may be there in him, yet he is still a sādhu.

### Text 35

*ei ta’ śrī-kṛṣṇa-vākya gītā-bhāgavate*  
*ihāke pūjiba yatne sadā sarva-mate*

**Перевод:**

**“This is the statement of Śrī Kṛṣṇa in Bhagavad-gītā and the Bhāgavata. Therefore I will always endeavour to worship such a devotee constantly.**

### Text 36

*ihāte āche ta’ eka nigūḍha siddhānta*  
*kṛpā kari’ jānāyecha tāi pāi anta*

**Перевод:**

**“Thus, this siddhānta is particularly confidential. Tell me, is there any end to such mercy?**

**Pūrva-pāpera gandhāvaśeṣa o pūrva-pāpa lakṣya kariyā yini kṛṣṇaika-śaraṇa sādhura nindā karena, tini nāmāparādhī (One who Criticises a Sādhu, Who has Exclusively Surrendered to Kṛṣṇa, for His Previous Pāpa, or Remaining Traces of Such Deeds, is an Offender to the Holy Name)**

### Text 37

*kṛṣṇa-nāme ruci yabe haibe udaya*  
*eka-nāme pūrva-pāpa haibeka kṣaya*

**Перевод:**

**“When a taste for kṛṣṇa-nāma arises and somebody even once chants the Holy Name, the results of his previous pāpa are destroyed.**

### Text 38

*pūrva-pāpa gandha tabu thāke kichu-dina*  
*nāmera prabhāve krama hañā paḍe kṣīṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“Traces of previous pāpa may still remain in him for some days, but by the power of the Holy Name they too are eventually destroyed. (7)**

**Комментарий:**

(7) When there is a taste for the Holy Name (nāma-ruci) the results of previous pāpa cannot remain. A trace of previous pāpa may remain, but that will also be destroyed after a few days.

### Text 39

*śīghra sei pāpa-gandha vidūrita haya*  
*parama dharmātmā bali haya paricaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Very soon, any trace of bad deeds goes away. Then such a person becomes known as parama-dharmātmā (a supremely virtuous person).**

### Text 40

*ye kayeka dina sei gandha nāhi yāya*  
*sādhāraṇa-jana-cakṣe pāpa bali’ bhāya*

**Перевод:**

**“During the period that this trace does not disappear, to the eyes of ordinary people he may still be considered as impious.**

### Text 41

*se pāpa dekhiyā yei sādhu-nindā kare*  
*pūrva-pāpa lakṣi’ punaḥ avajñā ācare*

**Перевод:**

**“Seeing his transgressions, they may criticise that sādhu. Also, pointing out his previous bad deeds, they may show contempt towards him. (8)**

**Комментарий:**

(8) If one is fixated on a devotee’s previous pāpa before he surrendered to the Lord, or on that pāpa which remains but is almost eradicated, then this is considered to be criticism of a Vaiṣṇava and is a great offence.

### Text 42

*sei ta’ pāṣaṇḍī, vaiṣṇavera nindā-doṣe*  
*nāma-aparādha maji’ paḍe kṛṣṇa-roṣe*

**Перевод:**

**“Such a person is an atheist! By criticising a Vaiṣṇava, he commits an offence to the Holy Name. Kṛṣṇa becomes angry with him and he falls down.**

**Kṛṣṇaika-śaraṇatāi sādhu-lakṣaṇa, āpanāke sādhu baliyā paricaya deoyā dāmbhikatā (The Quality of a Sādhu is that He Takes Exclusive Shelter of Kṛṣṇa, But it is Arrogance to Identify Oneself as a Sādhu)**

### Text 43

*kṛṣṇaika-śaraṇa mātra kṛṣṇa-nāma gāya*  
*sādhu-nāme paricita kṛṣṇera kṛpāya*

**Перевод:**

**“One who takes exclusive shelter of Kṛṣṇa and chants kṛṣṇa-nāma becomes known as a sādhu, by the mercy of Kṛṣṇa.**

### Text 44

*kṛṣṇa-bhakta vyatīta nāhika sādhu āra*  
*āmi sādhu bali haya dambha-avatāra*

**Перевод:**

**“Only the devotees of Kṛṣṇa, and no one else, are sādhus. But those who declare, ‘I am a sādhu!’ are the avatāras of arrogance! (9)**

**Комментарий:**

(9) Dambha-avatāra (‘The avatāra of arrogance’) refers to proud people who wrap themselves in the flag of religiosity (dharma-dhvajīs), who dress like sādhus only to live comfortably.

**Svalpākṣare sādhu-nirṇaya (A Few Brief Words on Deciding Who is a Sādhu)**

### Text 45

*se balibe āmi dīna kṛṣṇaika-śaraṇa*  
*kṛṣṇa-nāma yāra mukhe sādhu sei jana*

**Перевод:**

**“One who says, ‘I am a fallen person who has taken exclusive shelter of Kṛṣṇa’ and whose mouth chants kṛṣṇa-nāma – such a person is a sādhu.**

### Text 46-47

*tṛṇa haite hīna bali’ āpanāke jāne*  
*sahiṣṇu tarura nyāya āpanāke māne*

*nije ta’ amānī āra sakale mānada*  
*tāra mukhe kṛṣṇa-nāma kṛṣṇa-rati-prada*

**Перевод:**

**“A person who considers himself to be lower than straw, who is tolerant like a tree, who does not desire honour from others, who gives respect to all, and whose mouth chants kṛṣṇa-nāma – such a person can give kṛṣṇa-rati (affection towards Kṛṣṇa).**

**Nāma-parāyaṇa vaiṣṇava-i sādhu, tan-nindā-i aparādha (A Vaiṣṇava Who is Absorbed in the Name is Indeed a Sādhu, and Blaspheming Him is an Offence)**

### Text 48

*hena sādhu-mukhe yabe śuni eka nāma*  
*vaiṣṇava baliyā tā’re kariba praṇāma*

**Перевод:**

**“Any sādhu from whose mouth I hear the Holy Name even once, I call him a Vaiṣṇava and offer respects unto him.**

### Text 49

*vaiṣṇava se jagad-guru jagatera bandhu*  
*vaiṣṇava sakala-jīve sadā kṛpā-sindhu*

**Перевод:**

**“A Vaiṣṇava is the guru of the whole world. He is a friend to the world. A Vaiṣṇava is always an ocean of mercy to all jīvas.**

### Text 50

*e-hena vaiṣṇava-nindā yei jana kare*  
*narake paḍibe se janma-janmāntare*

**Перевод:**

**“Anyone who blasphemes such a Vaiṣṇava falls down into a hellish condition birth after birth.**

### Text 51

*bhakti labhibāre āra nāhika upāya*  
*bhakti-labhe sarva-jīva vaiṣṇava-kṛpāya*

**Перевод:**

**“There is no other way to attain bhakti – every jīva must attain bhakti by the mercy of a Vaiṣṇava.**

### Text 52

*vaiṣṇava dehete thāke śrī-kṛṣṇera śakti*  
*sei deha-sparśe anye haya kṛṣṇa-bhakti*

**Перевод:**

**“Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s potency rests within the body of a Vaiṣṇava. Just by touching his body others may achieve kṛṣṇa-bhakti. (10)**

**Комментарий:**

(10) Hlādinī (the pleasure-giving potency) and sandhinī (the knowledge potency) merge and become the bhakti-śakti (the potency of devotion). Bhakti is acquired in this way – a siddha-bhakta (perfected devotee) transmits the bhakti-śakti to a sādhaka-bhakta (practising devotee). Then he becomes perfect and transmits bhakti to other sādhaka-jīvas. By specifically engaging in the spiritual activities of bhakti, the ātmā eventually becomes situated in perfection. When the ātmā is free from aversion to bhakti and is inclined towards it, then a perfected devotee, who is full of mercy, transmits bhakti to that ātmā. This is a secret.

### Text 53

*vaiṣṇava-adharāmṛta āra pada-jala*  
*vaiṣṇavera pada-rajaḥ tina mahā-bala*

**Перевод:**

**“The nectar from the lips of a Vaiṣṇava (i.e. his food or drink), the water that has washed his feet, and the dust from his feet – these three things are most powerful.**

**Vaiṣṇavera śakti sañcāra (The Transmission of a Vaiṣṇava’s Potency)**

### Text 54

*vaiṣṇava-nikaṭe yadi baise katakṣaṇa*  
*deha-haite haya kṛṣṇa-śakti niḥsaraṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“If one sits near a Vaiṣṇava, after some time one will experience Kṛṣṇa’s potency emanating from his body.**

### Text 55

*sei śakti śraddhāvān hṛdaye paśiyā*  
*bhaktira udaya kare deha kāṅpāiyā*

**Перевод:**

**“When that potency enters the heart of somebody who has śraddhā, bhakti arises and their body trembles.**

### Text 56

*ye basila vaiṣṇavera nikaṭe śraddhāya*  
*tāhāra hṛdaye bhakti haibe udaya*

**Перевод:**

**“For one who has śraddhā and remains close to a Vaiṣṇava, bhakti arises within his heart.**

### Text 57

*prathame āsibe tāra mukhe kṛṣṇa-nāma*  
*nāmera prabhāve pābe sarva-guṇa-grāma*

**Перевод:**

**“When kṛṣṇa-nāma first enters his mouth, by the influence of the Holy Name he obtains all good qualities.**

**Vaiṣṇavera ki ki doṣa dharile vaiṣṇava-nindā haya – jāti-doṣa, pūrva-doṣa, naṣṭa-prāya avaśiṣṭa doṣa, kādācitka doṣa (Finding Fault With a Vaiṣṇava is Criticising a Vaiṣṇava – Finding fault with His Birth, His Previous Faults, Those Faults that have Almost Disappeared, and His Momentary Faults)**

### Text 58

*vaiṣṇavera jāti āra pūrva-doṣa dhare*  
*kādācitka doṣa dekhi’ yei nindā kare*

**Перевод:**

**“A person who finds faults with a Vaiṣṇava’s birth, or with his previous or momentary faults, is guilty of criticising.**

### Text 59

*naṣṭa-prāya doṣa la’ye kare apamāna*  
*yama-daṇḍe kaṣṭa pāya se-saba ajñāna*

**Перевод:**

**“Considering those faults that have almost disappeared is also offensive. All this is ignorance and such a person will be punished by the rod of Yama. (11)**

**Комментарий:**

(11) Anyone who criticises a Vaiṣṇava’s birth, his faults due to occasional carelessness, defects in him that have almost been eradicated, or faults in his character prior to his coming to the path of śaraṇāgati, is guilty of criticising a Vaiṣṇava, and is an offender of Vaiṣṇavas. Such a person will not attain nāma-ruci (a taste for the Holy Name). One who takes shelter of bhakti with śraddhā becomes a pure Vaiṣṇava. The aforementioned four types of faults can rarely be noticed in him. There is no possibility of any other faults in him.

### Text 60

*vaiṣṇavera mukhe nāma-māhātmya pracāra*  
*se vaiṣṇava-nindā kṛṣṇa nāhi sahe āra*

**Перевод:**

**“A Vaiṣṇava’s mouth always propagates the glories of the Holy Name. Kṛṣṇa will not tolerate anyone who criticises such a Vaiṣṇava.**

### Text 61

*dharma-yoga-yāga-jñāna-kāṇḍa parihari*  
*ye bhajila kṛṣṇa-nāma sei sarvopari*

**Перевод:**

**“Anyone who rejects dharma, yoga, yāga, and the jñāna-kāṇḍa and who worships kṛṣṇa-nāma is the best.**

**Anya deva-śāstra-nindādi-śūnya nāmāśrayī sādhu (A Sādhu who Takes Shelter of the Holy Name is Devoid of Offences to Other Devas and the Śāstra)**

### Text 62

*anya-deva, anya-śāstra nā kari’ nindana*  
*nāmera āśraya laya śuddha-sādhu-jana*

**Перевод:**

**“A sādhu who takes shelter of the Holy Name does not criticise different Devas or other śāstra.**

### Text 63

*se-sādhu gṛhastha hauk athavā sannyāsī*  
*tāṅhāra caraṇa-reṇu pāite prayāsī*

**Перевод:**

**“Whether that sādhu is a gṛhastha or a sannyāsī, I desire to attain the dust of his feet.**

### Text 64

*yāra yata nāme rati se tata vaiṣṇava*  
*vaiṣṇavera krama ei mate anubhava*

**Перевод:**

**“One is a Vaiṣṇava according to the extent of his attraction to the Holy Name. A Vaiṣṇava is determined according to this. (12)**

**Комментарий:**

(12) To the extent that one is attracted to the Holy Name of Kṛṣṇa, he is a Vaiṣṇava.

### Text 65

*ithe varṇāśrama, dhana, pāṇḍitya, yauvana*  
*kona kārya nāhi kare rūpa-bala-jana*

**Перевод:**

**“His position in varṇāśrama, his wealth, learning, youth, beauty, strength, or amount of followers is not a consideration.**

### Text 66

*ataeva yini karilena nāmāśraya*  
*sādhu-nindā chāḍibena e-dharma niścaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Thus, anyone who takes shelter of the Holy Name will avoid criticising sādhus. Indeed, that is his inherent nature.**

### Text 67

*nāmāśrayā śuddhā-bhakti bhakta-bhakti-rūpā*  
*bhakta-bhakti-vivarjitā haile virūpā*

**Перевод:**

**“A devotee who takes shelter of the Holy Name and engages in pure bhakti is the very embodiment of bhakti. A ‘devotee’ who is without bhakti is ugly and monstrous.**

### Text 68

*yāṅhā sādhu-nindā tāṅhā nāhi bhakti-sthiti*  
*ataeva aparādhe tathā pariṇati*

**Перевод:**

**“A person who criticises a sādhu has no standing in bhakti. Therefore, his offence makes him averse (towards bhakti).**

### Text 69

*sādhu-nindā chāḍi’ bhakta sādhu-bhakti kare*  
*sādhu-saṅga sādhu-sevā ei dharmācare*

**Перевод:**

**“A devotee avoids criticising sādhus and should be devoted to them. Association with sādhus and serving them is the real practice of dharma.**

**Asat-saṅga dui prakāra – strī-saṅgī (The Two Types of Bad Association – Inappropriate Association with Females)**

### Text 70

*asat-saṅga-tyāge haya vaiṣṇava-ācāra*  
*asat-saṅge haya sādhu-avajñā apāra*

**Перевод:**

**“The conduct of a Vaiṣṇava is that he always avoids bad association. By bad association one continuously shows contempt for sādhus.**

### Text 71

*asat se dvi-prakāra sarva-śāstre kaya*  
*sei duiyera madhye yoṣit-saṅgī eka haya*

**Перевод:**

**“The śāstra says that bad association is of two types. Of the two, one of these is inappropriate association with females. (13)**

**Комментарий:**

(13) Shunning bad association is the primary conduct for a Vaiṣṇava. Bad association is of two types – improper association with women and with non-devotees. For the female devotee, improper association with a man is also said to be bad association. Illicit association with women, and association with women according to religious principles, are the two kinds of association with females.

### Text 72

*yoṣit-saṅgi-saṅgī punaḥ tāra madhye gaṇya*  
*tāra saṅga-tyāge jīva haibeka dhanya*

**Перевод:**

**“Keeping company with a man who is too attached to females is also counted as bad association. A jīva who avoids such company becomes fortunate. (14)**

**Комментарий:**

(14) Association with a man too attached to women is an obstacle to bhakti.

**Yoṣit-saṅgī kāhāke bale? (Who is Said to be Attached to Women?)**

### Text 73

*kṛṣṇere saṁsāre ye dāmpatya-dharma thāke*  
*asat baliyā śāstra nā bale tāhāke*

**Перевод:**

**“A married husband and wife may remain together in a family centred around Kṛṣṇa. The śāstra does not say that this is immoral.**

### Text 74

*adharma-saṁyoge āra straiṇa-bhāve rata*  
*yoṣit-saṅgī jana duṣṭa, śāstrera sammata*

**Перевод:**

**“That corrupt person who engages in illicit sex and is mentally infatuated with females, is a yoṣīt-saṅgī (one who is attached to women). That is the opinion of the śāstra.**

**Dvitīya prakāra asat – kṛṣṇete abhakta tina prakāra (The Second Type of Bad Association – Those who are Not Devotees of Kṛṣṇa are of Three Kinds)**

### Text 75

*kṛṣṇete abhakta asat dvitīya prakāra*  
*māyāvādī, dharma-dhvajī, nirīśvara āra*

**Перевод:**

**“One who is not a devotee of Kṛṣṇa (abhakta) – this is the second type of bad association. This includes the māyāvādi, the dharma-dhvajī (religious hypocrites who wrap themselves in the flag of religion), and atheists. (15)**

**Комментарий:**

(15) Māyāvādī refers to those who do not consider the form of Bhagavān to be eternal, who believe that Kṛṣṇa and His other forms are comprised of māyā, and who think that the jīvas are also made of māyā. Dharma-dhvajī means those who internally have no bhakti or renunciation and who dress deceptively only in order to make a living.

**Yini balena, ei saba lokera nindāke-o sādhu-nindā bale, tini-o varjya (Those Who Declare that the Condemnation of All these People is an Offence to Sādhus, are also to be Rejected)**

### Text 76

*varjile e-saba saṅga sādhu-nindā naya*  
*ihāke ye nindā-bale sei varjya haya*

**Перевод:**

**“Giving up the association of all these types of people is not an offence to sādhus. Anyone who claims that it is an offence is useless!**

### Text 77

*ei-saba saṅga chāḍi’ ananya-śaraṇa*  
*kṛṣṇa-nāma kari’ pāya kṛṣṇa-prema-dhana*

**Перевод:**

**“Anyone who avoids all these types of people, but takes exclusive shelter of the Lord and no one else, and chants kṛṣṇa-nāma, attains the great wealth of kṛṣṇa-prema.**

**Vaiṣṇavābhāsa, prākṛta-vaiṣṇava, vaiṣṇava-prāya o kaniṣṭha-vaiṣṇava – ei sakala ekai kathā (The Glimmer of a Vaiṣṇava, the Materialistic Vaiṣṇava, the Marginal Vaiṣṇava and the Neophyte Vaiṣṇava – All these Words Mean the Same Thing)**

### Text 78

*sādhu-sevā-hīna arce laukika śraddhāya*  
*prākṛta-vaiṣṇava haya vaiṣṇavera prāya*

**Перевод:**

**“One who possesses ordinary śraddhā and who worships the Deity but does not serve the sādhus is a prākṛta-vaiṣṇava (a materialistic Vaiṣṇava), a vaiṣṇava-prāya (a marginal Vaiṣṇava).**

### Text 79

*vaiṣṇava-ābhāsa sei nahe ta’ vaiṣṇava*  
*kemane pāibe sādhu-saṅgera vaiṣṇava*

**Перевод:**

**“He is a vaiṣṇavābhāsa (a glimmer of a Vaiṣṇava) and not a real Vaiṣṇava. Somehow he must get the association of sādhus to become a Vaiṣṇava.**

### Text 80

*ataeva kaniṣṭha madhyete tā’re gaṇi*  
*tā’re kṛpā karibena vaiṣṇava āpani*

**Перевод:**

**“Therefore, I consider him to be a kaniṣṭha-vaiṣṇava (a neophyte Vaiṣṇava). By the mercy (of the devotees), he may become a Vaiṣṇava.**

**Madhyama vaiṣṇava (The Intermediate Vaiṣṇava)**

### Text 81-82

*kṛṣṇa-prema, kṛṣṇa-bhakte maitrī ācaraṇa*  
*bāliśete kṛpā āra dveṣī upekṣaṇa*

*karile madhyama bhakta śuddha-bhakta hana*  
*kṛṣṇa-nāmne adhikāra karena arjana*

**Перевод:**

**“One who develops kṛṣṇa-prema, who displays friendly behaviour to Kṛṣṇa’s devotees, who is compassionate to the innocent and avoids those who are envious, such a person is a madhyama-bhakta. Very soon he becomes a pure devotee and attains the qualification to chant kṛṣṇa-nāma.**

**Uttama vaiṣṇava (The Highest Vaiṣnava)**

### Text 83-84

*sarvatra yāṅhāra haya kṛṣṇa-daraśana*  
*kṛṣṇe sakalera sthiti kṛṣṇa prāṇa-dhana*  
*vaiṣṇavāvaiṣṇava-bheda nāhi thāke tāṅra*  
*vaiṣṇava uttama tini kṛṣṇa-nāma-sāra*

**Перевод:**

**“One who sees Kṛṣṇa everywhere, who understands that everything rests within Kṛṣṇa, and who considers Kṛṣṇa to be the wealth of his life, who sees no difference between Vaiṣṇavas and non-Vaiṣṇavas, such a person is an uttama-vaiṣṇava (the highest Vaiṣṇava). Kṛṣṇa-nāma is essentially everything for him.**

**Madhyama vaiṣṇava-i sādhu sevā karena (An Intermediate Vaiṣṇava Must Serve Sādhus)**

### Text 85

*ataeva madhyama vaiṣṇava mahāśaya*  
*sādhu-sevā-rata sadā thākena niścaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Therefore, a respectable madhyama-vaiṣṇava must certainly serve this kind of sādhu constantly. (16)**

**Комментарий:**

(16) From the stage of a madhyama-vaiṣṇava one starts to give due consideration to who is a pure Vaiṣṇava. A madhyama has the eligibility to deliberate upon who is a Vaiṣṇava and who is not a Vaiṣṇava, because he needs to serve a pure Vaiṣṇava. If he gives up discriminating who is a Vaiṣṇava and who is not a Vaiṣṇava, then the madhyama-vaiṣṇava will commit an offence to the Vaiṣṇavas. With earnestness, he should seek out a pure Vaiṣṇava and serve him. When an uttama-vaiṣṇava makes no distinction between a Vaiṣṇava and a non-Vaiṣṇava, then how can he serve the Vaiṣṇavas? The uttama-vaiṣṇava sees no difference between enemies and friends, therefore how can he distinguish between Vaiṣṇavas and non-Vaiṣṇavas?

**Prākṛta-vaiṣṇava nāmābhāsera adhikārī (The Materialistic Devotee is Qualified for Nāmābhāsa)**

### Text 86

*prākṛta-vaiṣṇava yei vaiṣṇavera prāya*  
*nāmābhāse adhikārī sarva-śāstra gāya*

**Перевод:**

**“All the śāstra states that the prākṛta-vaiṣṇava, or vaiṣṇava-prāya, is eligible to chant nāmābhāsa.**

**Madhyama-vaiṣṇava nāmādhikārī o nāmāparādha vicāra karibena (The Intermediate Vaiṣṇava is Qualified for the Holy Name, But Must be Cautious about Offences to the Name)**

### Text 87

*madhyama-vaiṣṇava-mātra nāme adhikārī*  
*śrī-nāma-bhajane aparādhera vicārī*

**Перевод:**

**“The madhyama-vaiṣṇava is eligible to chant the Name, but he must take offences into consideration when engaged in śrī nāma-bhajana.**

### Text 88

*uttama-vaiṣṇave aparādha asambhava*  
*sarvatra dekhena tini kṛṣṇera vaibhava*

**Перевод:**

**“It is impossible for an uttama-vaiṣṇava to make offences, because he sees Kṛṣṇa’s opulence everywhere.**

### Text 89

*nija nija adhikāra kariyā vicāra*  
*sādhu-nindā aparādha kari’ parihāra*

**Перевод:**

**“In consideration of one’s own qualification, one should abstain from the offence of criticising sādhus. (17)**

**Комментарий:**

(17) It is necessary to know one’s qualification by considering one’s own nature. Knowing this qualification and performing nāma-saṅkīrtana with fixed determination is the dharma of a Vaiṣṇava.

### Text 90

*sādhu-saṅga, sādhu-sevā, nāma-saṅkīrtana*  
*sarva-jīve dayā ei bhakta-ācaraṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“Keeping company with sādhus, serving them, performing nāma–saṅkīrtana, and showing mercy to all jīvas – this is the conduct of a devotee.**

**Sādhu-nindā ghaṭile ki karā kartavya? (What Should One Do if they Have Offended a Sādhu?)**

### Text 91

*pramāde yadyapi ghaṭe sādhu-vigarhaṇa*  
*tabe anutāpe dhari’ se sādhu-caraṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“If out of foolishness one has offended a sādhu, then with remorse one should catch hold of that sādhu’s feet.**

### Text 92

*kāṅdiyā baliba, prabho kṣami’ aparādha*  
*e-duṣṭa-nindake kara vaiṣṇava-prasāda*

**Перевод:**

**Weeping, one should say, ‘O prabhu, please forgive my offence! Kindly give the mercy of a Vaiṣṇava to this wicked offender.’**

### Text 93

*sādhu baḍa dayāmaya tabe ārdra-mane*  
*kṣamibena aparādha kṛpā āliṅgane*

**Перевод:**

**“The sādhu, being full of compassion, with a tender heart will then forgive the offence and mercifully embrace him. (18)**

**Комментарий:**

(18) In this way, Gopāla Cāpāla’s offence to a Vaiṣṇava was forgiven. Kindly look in the pramāṇa-mālā (the garland of śāstrika evidence).

### Text 94

*ei-ta prathama aparādhera vicāra*  
*śrī-caraṇe nivedinu ājñā anusāra*

**Перевод:**

**“According to Your instruction, I place this description of the first offence at Your divine feet.”**

### Text 95

*hari-dāsa pāda-padme bhramara ye-jana*  
*hari-nāma-cintāmaṇi tāṅhāra jīvana*

**Перевод:**

**May this Hari-nāma Cintāmaṇi become the very life of those persons who swarm like bumblebees at the lotus feet of Hari Dāsa.**

## Chapter 5. Devāntare svātantrya-jñānāparādha (The Offence of Considering the Devās to be Independent)

*śivasya śrī‑viṣṇor ya iha guṇa‑nāmādi‑sakalaṁ*  
*dhiyā bhinnaṁ paśyet sa khalu hari‑nāmāhita‑karaḥ*

**“Those who see any difference between the auspicious Śrī Viṣṇu and any of His names, qualities etc. are certainly offenders to the Name of Hari.”**

### Text 1

*jaya gadādhara-prāṇa jāhnavā-jīvana*  
*jaya sītānātha jaya gaura bhakta-jana*

**Перевод:**

**Glories to Mahāprabhu, the life of Gadādhara! Glories to Nityānanda, the life of Jāhnavā Devī! Glories to the husband of Sītā (Advaita)! Glories to the devotees of Gaura!**

### Text 2

*hari-dāsa bale, tabe kari’ yoḍa-hāta*  
*dvitīyāparādha ebe śuna jagannātha*

**Перевод:**

**Then, placing his hands together, Hari Dāsa said, “O Lord of the universe, kindly hear about the second offence.**

**Viṣṇu-tattva (The Truth Concerning Viṣṇu)**

### Text 3

*parama advaya-jñāna viṣṇu para-tattva*  
*cit-svarūpa jagadīśa sadā śuddha-sattva*

**Перевод:**

**“Viṣṇu is Supreme non-dual knowledge and the Absolute Truth. The form of the Lord of the Universe is transcendental and He is eternally situated in the state of śuddha-sattva (pure goodness).**

### Text 4

*goloka-vihārī kṛṣṇa se tattvera sāra*  
*catuḥ-ṣaṣṭhi guṇe alaṅkṛta rasādhāra*

**Перевод:**

**“Kṛṣṇa, who sports in Goloka, is the essence of reality. Decorated with sixty-four qualities, He is the basis of rasa.**

### Text 5

*ṣaṣṭhi-guṇa nārāyaṇa svarūpe prakāśa*  
*sei ṣaṣṭhi-guṇa viṣṇu-sāmānya-vilāsa*

**Перевод:**

**“Sixty of these qualities are manifest in the form of Nārāyaṇa, and these sixty qualities are, to a certain extent, in Viṣṇu’s pastimes also.**

### Text 6

*puruṣāvatāre āra svāṁśa-avatāre*  
*sei ṣaṣṭhi-guṇa spaṣṭa kārya anusāre*

**Перевод:**

**“These sixty qualities are visible in the puruṣāvatāras and the svāṁśāvatāras based upon their activities. (1)**

**Комментарий:**

(1) The transcendentally pure Viṣṇu, or Nārāyaṇa, the Master of Paravyoma, is the vilāsa-vigraha (pastime-form) of Kṛṣṇa the Lord of Goloka. Saṅkarṣaṇa-Viṣṇu, who resides in Paravyoma, goes to the Kāraṇa Ocean and manifests the form of Mahā-Viṣṇu, the first puruṣāvatāra. Entering into every universe, Mahā-Viṣṇu expands as Garbhodakaśāyī. That Puruṣa is the aggregate of all things. Entering every jīva, He becomes Kṣīrodakaśāyī-Viṣṇu. These are the three puruṣāvatāras. From Kṣīrodakaśāyī, the various svāṁśāvatāras such as Matsya, Kūrma etc. are manifest. All these viṣṇu-tattva forms possess sixty qualities. Śaktyāveśāvatāras (jīvas who are empowered) such as Paraśurāma, Buddha, and Pṛthu are all vibhinnāṁśas (separated expansions).

**Viṣṇura vibhinnāṁśera prakāra bheda, jīvera pañcāśat-guṇa (Viṣṇu’s Different Vibhinnāṁśa Expansions and the Fifty Qualities Possessed by Jīvas)**

### Text 7

*viṣṇura ye vibhinnāṁśa dui ta prakāra*  
*pañcāśat-guṇa jīve bindu bindu tā’ra*

**Перевод:**

**“Viṣṇu’s vibhinnāṁśas are of two kinds. Fifty qualities are found in minute ‘droplets’ within the jīva.**

**Giriśādi devatā vibhinnāṁśa haiyāo sāmānya jīva nan tāṅhārā pañcāśat-guṇa-viśiṣṭa (Śiva and the other Devas are Also Vibhinnāṁśas, But They Are Not Ordinary Jīvas Since They Possess Fifty-five Qualities)**

### Text 8

*giriśādi-deve ei guṇa-pañcāśat*  
*tad-adhika parimāṇe sarvadā saṁyuta*

**Перевод:**

**“Girīśa (Śiva) and the Devas always possess these fifty qualities, but to a greater extent. (2)**

**Комментарий:**

(2) The greater the number of ‘droplets’ present in a jīva, the greater the number of all these qualities.

### Text 9

*tad-vyatīta āra pañca guṇa aṁśa-māne*  
*prakāśita āche taba vicitra-vidhāne*

**Перевод:**

**“In addition, another five qualities partially manifest, and these are also wonderfully present within them. (3)**

**Комментарий:**

(3) In addition to these fifty qualities, Śiva and the other Devas also possess five more which manifest partially within them. In other words, all these qualities are not complete except in viṣṇu-tattva.

**Ṣaṣṭhi-guṇe viṣṇutva (The Sixty Qualities Present in Viṣṇu)**

### Text 10

*sei pañca-pañcāśat guṇa-pūrṇa tāya*  
*viṣṇute virājamāna sarva-śāstre gāya*

**Перевод:**

**“These fifty-five qualities are present within Viṣṇu in full – all the śāstra declare this.**

### Text 11

*tad-vyatīta āra pañca-guṇa nārāyaṇe*  
*āche tāra sattā kabhu nāhi anya-jane*

**Перевод:**

**“Apart from these, five other qualities are present within Nārāyaṇa. They do not exist in anyone else.**

### Text 12

*ṣaṣṭhi-guṇe viṣṇu-tattva parama-īśvara*  
*giriśa-ādi anya-deva tāṅhāra kiṅkara*

**Перевод:**

**“The Supreme Controller, viṣṇu-tattva, possesses sixty qualities. Girīśa (Śīva) and the other Devas are His servants.**

### Text 13

*vibhinnāṁśa giriśādi jīva-śreṣṭhatara*  
*viṣṇu sarva-jīveśvara sarva-deveśvara*

**Перевод:**

**“Girīśa and the Devas are all vibhinnāṁśa. Viṣṇu is the Lord of all jīvas and Lord of all the Devas.**

**Ajñāna-vyakti anya devatāra sahita viṣṇu-ke samāna mane kare (Ignorant Persons Consider Viṣṇu and the Other Devas to be Equal)**

### Text 14

*anya-deva saha sama viṣṇu-ke ye māne*  
*se baḍa ajñāna īśa-tattva nāhi jāne*

**Перевод:**

**“Extremely ignorant people who think that other Devas are equal to Viṣṇu do not understand īśa-tattva (the truth concerning the Supreme Controller).**

### Text 15

*e-jaḍa-jagate viṣṇu parama-īśvara*  
*giriśādi yata deva tāṅra vidhikara*

**Перевод:**

**“Within this mundane world, Viṣṇu is the Supreme Controller. Girīśa and other Devas carry out His instructions. (4)**

**Комментарий:**

(4) ‘Carry out His commands’ means that they are servants.

### Text 16

*keha bale māyāra tri-guṇe tridiveśa*  
*sarvadā samāna brahma-tattva saviśeṣa*

**Перевод:**

**“Some say that when Brahman, who is always homogenous, contacts the three modes of māyā, He attains qualities and becomes three Devas (Brahmā, Viṣṇu and Śiva). (5)**

**Комментарий:**

(5) This is the concept of the māyāvādī. They state that Brahman is nirviṣeśa (devoid of qualities). But when coming into contact with the three modes of material nature, it becomes saviśeṣa (with qualities) and manifests as three Devas (Brahmā, Viṣṇu and Śiva).

**Nānā-vidha vādānuvādera siddhānta (The Conclusion in Regards to the Debate Amongst Various Doctrines)**

### Text 17

*śāstrera siddhānte tabu pūjya nārāyaṇa*  
*brahmā śiva sṛṣṭi-laya-kāryera kāraṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“The śāstra concludes that Nārāyaṇa is worshipable. Brahmā and Śiva are responsible for the tasks of creation and destruction.**

### Text 18

*vāsudeve chāḍi’ yei anya-deve bhaje*  
*īśvara chāḍiyā sei saṁsārete maje*

**Перевод:**

**“People who reject Vāsudeva to worship other Devas instead, become absorbed in the world of repeated birth and death due to their rejection of the Supreme Controller.**

### Text 19

*keha bale, viṣṇu para-tattva baṭe jāni*  
*sarva-viṣṇumaya viśva veda-vākya māni*

**Перевод:**

**“Some say that, ‘I know that Viṣṇu is the Supreme Truth. I accept that the Vedas state that Viṣṇu is present everywhere in the world.**

### Text 20

*ataeva sarva-deve viṣṇu-adhiṣṭhāna*  
*sarva-devārcane haya viṣṇura samāna*

**Перевод:**

**“Therefore, Viṣṇu resides within all the Devas, and so worship of the Devas is the same as worshipping Viṣṇu.’**

### Text 21

*eita niṣedha-para vākya, vidhi naya*  
*anya-deva-pūjāra niṣedha ei haya*

**Перевод:**

**“Statements such as these are forbidden because such worship is not enjoined. To worship other Devas in this way is prohibited. (6)**

**Комментарий:**

(6) One should not claim that Viṣṇu resides within all the Devas and then proceed to worship them. By worshipping Viṣṇu one worships all other Devas. Therefore, it is not necessary to worship other Devas separately.

### Text 22

*sarva viṣṇu-maya viśva e-kathā balile*  
*viṣṇu-pūjā kaile saba deve pūjā mile*

**Перевод:**

**“One may say that Viṣṇu presides everywhere within the world. By worshipping Viṣṇu one also worships all the Devas.**

### Text 23

*taru-mūle jala dile śākhāra ullāsa*  
*pallave ḍhālile jala vṛkṣera vināśa*

**Перевод:**

**“If one waters the root of a tree, the branches will thrive. If one only waters the leaves, the tree will perish.**

### Text 24

*ataeva pūji viṣṇu, anya-deva tyaji’*  
*tāhātei anya-deva kāye-kāye pūji*

**Перевод:**

**“Thus, if I abandon other Devas and only worship Viṣṇu, then I will attain the result of worshipping all the forms of the other Devas.**

### Text 25

*ei vidhi – vedera sammata cira-dina*  
*durvipāke ei vidhi chāḍe arvācīna*

**Перевод:**

**“From ancient times the Vedas have accepted this decree. There is only misery for those fools who reject this rule. (7)**

**Комментарий:**

(7) Misery means that due to the shortsightedness of the jīva, he worships a Deva similar to his own nature. The worship of Viṣṇu, which is in transcendental goodness (śuddha-sattva), is eternal and in accordance with Vedic philosophy. But this remains unknown by those who are guided by stupidity.

### Text 26

*māyāvāda-doṣe jīva kali-āgamane*  
*bahu-deva pūje viṣṇu-sāmānya-darśane*

**Перевод:**

**“When the age of Kali arrived and the jīva became contaminated by māyāvāda, he believed in the philosophy that the worship of many Devas was equal to the worship of Viṣṇu.**

### Text 27

*eka eka deva eka eka phala-dātā*  
*sarva-phala-dātā-viṣṇu sakalera pātā*

**Перевод:**

**“Each individual Deva offers a specific benediction, but Viṣṇu bestows all boons and protects all.**

### Text 28

*kāmi-jana yadi tattva jānibāre pāre*  
*viṣṇu-pūji phala pāya, chāḍe’ devāntare*

**Перевод:**

**“If persons with material desires could understand this truth, they would abandon the Devas and attain results from worshipping Viṣṇu.**

**Gṛhastha vaiṣṇavera kartavya vidhāna (The Duties of a Householder Vaiṣṇava)**

### Text 29

*gṛhastha haiyā yei viṣṇu-bhakta haya*  
*sarva-kārye kṛṣṇa-pūje chāḍiyā saṁśaya*

**Перевод:**

**“The gṛhastha who is a devotee of Viṣṇu should, without any doubt, worship Kṛṣṇa in all his activities.**

### Text 30

*niṣekādi śmaśānānta saṁskāra yata*  
*tāhāte pūjaye kṛṣṇa veda-mantra-mata*

**Перевод:**

**“For various saṁskāras (purificatory ceremonies), beginning with niṣeka and terminating at the cremation ground, he should worship Viṣṇu with the mantras of the Vedas.**

### Text 31

*viṣṇu-vaiṣṇavera pūjā vedete vidhāna*  
*deva-pitṛ-gaṇe kṛṣṇa-nirmālya-pradāna*

**Перевод:**

**“Following the instructions of the Vedas, he should worship Viṣṇu and the Vaiṣṇavas. He should offer Kṛṣṇa’s remnants to the Devas and Pitṛs.**

### Text 32

*māyāvādi-mate pitṛ-śrāddha yei kare’*  
*yebā anya-deva pūje’ aparādhe mare*

**Перевод:**

**“Those who follow the philosophy of the māyāvādīs when offering śrāddha to the Pitṛs and worshipping the Devas are doomed due to their offence.**

### Text 33

*viṣṇu-tattve dvaita-buddhi nāma-aparādha*  
*sei aparādhe tāra haya bhakti-vādha*

**Перевод:**

**“Imposing a dualistic conception upon viṣnu-tattva is an offence to the Holy Name. By this offence one’s progress in bhakti is obstructed.**

### Text 34

*śivādi-devatā-gaṇe pṛthak īśvara*  
*mānile nāmāparādha haya bhayaṅkara*

**Перевод:**

**“Those who think that Śiva and the other Devas are independent controllers commit a dreadful offence to the Holy Name. (8)**

**Комментарий:**

(8) “Viṣṇu is the Supreme Controller, Śiva and the other Devas are also Supreme Controllers” – one who thinks in this way, that there are many Supreme Controllers, falls down due to this offence. Thus, it is not an offence to consider or worship that Deva as a guṇavatāra or a servant of Viṣṇu. The various Devas are Viṣṇu’s potencies. Their own individual potencies are imperfect.

### Text 35

*viṣṇu-śakti parā-śakti haite deva-yata*  
*bhinna-śakti siddha naya vedera sammata*

**Перевод:**

**“The Devas manifest from viṣṇu–śakti, the Lord’s superior energy. The Vedas never accept that their various potencies are superior to His.**

### Text 36

*śiva-brahmā-gaṇapati-sūrya-dikpāla*  
*kṛṣṇa-śakti-balete īśvara cira-kāla*

**Перевод:**

**“In ancient times, Śiva, Brahmā, Gaṇapati, Sūrya, and the Dikpālas were invested with Kṛṣṇa’s potency and became controllers.**

### Text 37

*ataeva pareśvara eka-mātra jāni*  
*āra saba-deve tāṅra śakti-madhye gaṇi*

**Перевод:**

**“Thus, I understand that Kṛṣṇa is the sole Pareśvara (Supreme Controller) and all the Devas are counted as His potencies.**

### Text 38

*ataeva sarva-kārye karma-jaḍa-bhāva*  
*chāḍiyā gṛhastha pāya bhaktira sad-bhāva*

**Перевод:**

**“Therefore, a gṛhastha should totally reject material conceptions in the execution of all his duties, and strive towards attaining a proper devotional mood.**

**Kirūpa vaiṣṇava gārhasthya dharma kariben (How Does a Vaiṣṇava Perform Householder Dharma?)**

### Text 39

*bhaktira sad-bhāve thāki sat-kriyā-karaṇe*  
*deva-pitṛ-gaṇe tuṣe nirmālya-arpaṇe*

**Перевод:**

**“He performs various rituals with a proper devotional mood, and he satisfies the Devas and Piṭrs by offering the remnants (of Kṛṣṇa) to them.**

### Text 40

*bahu-deva-devī pūjā karibe varjana*  
*kṛṣṇa-bhakta bali’ sabe karibe tarpaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“He gives up the worship of many Devas and Devīs and, because they are all devotees of Kṛṣṇa, they are pleased with him.**

### Text 41

*śrī-kṛṣṇa-vaiṣṇavārcane sarva-phala pāya*  
*nāme aparādha nahe sadā nāma gāya*

**Перевод:**

**“By worshipping Śrī Kṛṣṇa and the Vaiṣṇavas, all results are attained. He who does this avoids committing offences to the Holy Name and chants the Name constantly.**

**Varṇa-catuṣṭayera jīvana-yātrā vidhi (The Rules of Maintaining One’s Life in Relation to the Four Varṇas)**

### Text 42

*jagate mānava-gaṇa varṇa-dharmācari’*  
*karibeka deha-yātrā dharma-patha dhari’*

**Перевод:**

**“Those humans who follow the duties of varṇa in this world walk on the path of dharma as they travel in this material body. (9)**

**Комментарий:**

(9) The jīvas who are present in this world should follow the system of varṇāśrama while traversing in the material body – this is sanātana-dharma. This varṇa-dharma has been established by the ṛṣis and was implemented throughout the whole society in the pious land of Bhārata. In other countries, though this system has not been refined, it still exists in one form or another. Human nature cannot find completeness without varṇa. Saṅkaras and antyajas may gradually attain good fortune by following pure conduct, maintaining a lifestyle free from pāpa and entering the family of Kṛṣṇa – such rules are eternal.

**Antyajera vidhi (The Rules for Low-Born People)**

### Text 43

*saṅkara-antyaja-sabe tyaji’ nīca-dharma*  
*śūdrācāre kare sadā saṁsārera karma*

**Перевод:**

**“The saṅkara and antyaja should completely renounce their low-class nature and activities, and always accept the role of śūdras in the material world.**

### Text 44

*saṅkara-antyaja thākibeka śūdrācāre*  
*cātur-varṇa vinā dharma nāhika saṁsāre*

**Перевод:**

**“The saṅkara and antyaja should follow the conduct of the śudras, because without the four varṇas there is no dharma in this world.**

**Varṇa-dharmera dvārā jīvana-yātrā kariyā saṁsāri vyakti bhakti-pathe bhāvārjana kariben (Living Within Varṇa-Dharma, a Resident of this World Walks on the Path of Bhakti)**

### Text 45

*cātur-varṇya varṇa-dharme karibe saṁsāra*  
*śuddha-kṛṣṇa-bhakti-bale habe sadācāra*

**Перевод:**

**“In this world, one should be situated in one of the four varṇas within varṇa-dharma, execute pure kṛṣṇa-bhakti and always follow proper conduct.**

### Text 46

*cātur-varṇa yadyapi śrī-kṛṣṇa nāhi bhaje*  
*varṇa-dharmācāre thāki’ rauravete maje*

**Перевод:**

**“Even if one belongs to the four varṇas, if he does not worship Śrī Kṛṣṇa, despite following the rules of conduct found in varna-dharma, he will fall into Raurava (one of the lower planetary systems).**

### Text 47

*varṇa vinā gṛhasthera nāhi āra dharma*  
*varṇa-dharmācāre gṛhasthera saba karma*

**Перевод:**

**“Without varṇa, there is no other dharma for a gṛhastha. All a gṛhastha’s activities are found within the practices of varṇa-dharma.**

### Text 48

*varṇa-dharme e-saṁsāra nirvāha karibe*  
*yāvad-artha-parigrahe śrī-kṛṣṇa bhajibe*

**Перевод:**

**“In this world one should follow varṇa-dharma and, according to his means, he should worship Śrī Kṛṣṇa.**

### Text 49

*nisargataḥ vidhi-vādhya ye paryanta nara*  
*varṇa-dharma sva-nirvāhe karibe ādara*

**Перевод:**

**“During the time a man is under the rules of this world, he should take care to conduct himself according to varṇa-dharma.**

### Text 50

*bhakti-yoga-nāme ei tattva-nirūpaṇa*  
*bhakti-yoge bhāvodaya siddhānta-vacana*

**Перевод:**

**“This science is defined by the name ‘bhakti-yoga.’ In bhakti-yoga, bhāva arises – this is said to be the culmination of perfection (siddhānta).**

### Text 51

*bhāvodaye vidhira pravṛtti nāhi raya*  
*bhāvodita-kārye deha-yātrā siddha haya*

**Перевод:**

**“When bhāva arises, there is no need to follow the scriptural rules. One’s journey within this material body attains perfection at the appearance of bhāva. (10)**

**Комментарий:**

(10) As long as the jīva finds it necessary to follow rules and regulations, he must be situated in varnāśrama. As he continues in his bhajana, bhāva eventually arises within him. When bhāva appears, the jīva naturally becomes beautiful and as the regulated jīva ascends, he abandons the dictates of scriptural rules. Ordinary jīvas cannot understand this, as śuddha-bhāva appears on its own accord.

### Text 52

*gṛhi-vaiṣṇavera ei advaya-sādhana*  
*śrī-viṣṇu-advaya-tattve dvaita-nivartana*

**Перевод:**

**“The resolute sādhana of a grhastha Vaiṣṇava is to accept Śrī Viṣṇu – that Absolute Truth who is one without a second.**

**Nāma-nāmī o guṇa-guṇīra abhede viṣṇu-jñāna śuddha haya (The Pure Knowledge that Viṣṇu Is Non-Different From His Name and Qualities)**

### Text 53

*āra eka kathā āche dvaita-nivartane*  
*viṣṇu-nāma, viṣṇu-rūpa, viṣṇu-guṇa-gaṇe*

**Перевод:**

**“Another point is that there is no duality concerning Viṣṇu’s name, form and qualities.**

### Text 54

*viṣṇu-haite pṛthak-rūpe nā mānibe kabhu*  
*advaya-akhaṇḍa-viṣṇu cinmayatve vibhu*

**Перевод:**

**“One should not think that Viṣnu is different from His form. Viṣṇu is non-dual, complete, transcendental, and infinite.**

### Text 55

*ajñānete yadi haya dvaita-upadrava*  
*nāmābhāsa haya tāra prema asambhava*

**Перевод:**

**“If, due to ignorance, one is troubled by the misconception of duality (as regards the Lord’s name, form and qualities) then nāmābhāsa will occur and the attainment of prema will be impossible.**

### Text 56

*sad-guru-kṛpāya sei anartha-vināśa*  
*bhajite bhajite śuddha-nāmera prakāśa*

**Перевод:**

**“By the mercy of a genuine guru, this anartha will be destroyed. By continually engaging in bhajana, śuddha-nāma will eventually manifest.**

**Māyāvādīra kutarka o aparādha (The Fallacious Arguments and Offence of the Māyāvādīs)**

### Text 57

*mata-vāda-jñāne dvaita haila pravartana*  
*aparādha haya āra nahe nivartana*

**Перевод:**

**“If duality (concerning Kṛṣṇa’s name, form and qualities), based upon the ideology of certain philosophies, is introduced then an offence is committed which cannot be retracted.**

### Text 58

*māyāvādī bale brahma haya para-tattva*  
*nirviśeṣa-nirvikāra-nirākāra-sattva*

**Перевод:**

**“Māyāvādīs state, ‘The Supreme Truth is Brahman, which is devoid of qualities, unchangeable, and without form.**

### Text 59

*viṣṇu-rūpa, viṣṇu-nāma māyāya kalpita*  
*māyā-antardhāne viṣṇu hana brahma-gata*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Viṣṇu’s forms and names are the imaginary creations of māyā. With the disappearance of māyā, Viṣṇu merges into Brahman.’**

### Text 60

*e-saba kutarka mātra satya śūnyavāda*  
*para-tattve sarva-śakti abhāva-pramāda*

**Перевод:**

**“All these are simply fallacious arguments and in truth they are śūnyavāda. To claim that the Supreme Truth is devoid of all potencies is madness.**

### Text 61

*śaktimān brahma yei, sei viṣṇu haya*  
*nāmera vivāda-mātra vedera nirṇaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Brahman is the possessor of all potencies and He is Viṣṇu. The only difference is in their names – this is the verdict of the Vedas. (11)**

**Комментарий:**

(11) Māyāvādīs are narrow-minded. Observing that there is variety in the material world, they assume that spiritual existence must have no variety. Because they have incomplete faith, they imagine that Brahman is without rasa and dry. They cannot accept that Brahman naturally possesses names, forms, qualities, and pastimes. Considering this, they think that Brahman becomes known as Viṣṇu. Māyāvāda is very unfortunate for the jīvas, and pure devotees stay far away from that narrow doctrine and believe in the name, form, qualities and pastimes of Bhagavan.

**Viṣṇu o brahma-tattvera sambandha (The Relationship Between Viṣṇu and Brahma Tattva)**

### Text 62

*viṣṇu para-tattva tāra nirviśeṣa dharma*  
*saviśeṣa dharma saha haya eka marma*

**Перевод:**

**“Viṣṇu is the Supreme Reality. He possesses an intrinsic feature without qualities and an intrinsic feature with qualities – yet He remains one.**

### Text 63

*viṣṇura acintya-śakti virodha-bhañjana*  
*anāyāse kari’ kare saundarya sthāpana*

**Перевод:**

**“Viṣṇu’s acintya-śakti (inconceivable potency) dispels all contradictions and easily establishes His beauty. (12)**

**Комментарий:**

(12) Fully accepting the Supreme Lord’s special acintya-śakti, which does not establish nirviśeṣa exclusively, all opposing arguments are cast far away.

### Text 64

*jīva-buddhi sahajete ati alpatara*  
*acintya-śaktira bhāva nā kare gocara*

**Перевод:**

**“By nature, the intelligence of the jīva is very meagre. They cannot fathom the acintya-śakti.**

### Text 65

*nija-buddhye cāhe eka sthāpite īśvara*  
*khaṇḍa-jñāne pāya, brahma-tattvete avara*

**Перевод:**

**“Desiring to establish the Supreme Controller with their intelligence, they only attain partial knowledge of the lowest conception of Brahman.**

### Text 66

*viṣṇura parama pada chāḍi’ devārcita*  
*brahme baddha haya nāhi bujhe hitāhita*

**Перевод:**

**“If they reject the divine feet of Viṣṇu and worship the Devas, or if they are attached to Brahman, they will never understand what is beneficial for them and what is not beneficial. (13)**

**Комментарий:**

(13) Viṣṇur – those who reject Viṣnu’s feet, which are worshipped by all the Devatās, have a fragmented intellect and, being bound only to an imaginary Brahman, they cannot comprehend what is beneficial and what is not beneficial.

### Text 67

*cinmaya-svarūpa-jñāna ye bujhite jāne*  
*viṣṇu, viṣṇu-nāma-guṇa eka kari’ māne*

**Перевод:**

**“Those who understand this knowledge concerning the transcendental nature of the Supreme know that Viṣṇu and His name and qualities are one.**

### Text 68

*ei-ta viśuddha jñāna śrī-kṛṣṇa-svarūpa*  
*sambandha buddhite labhi’ bhaje nāma-rūpa*

**Перевод:**

**“This is pure, transcendental knowledge of Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s form and inherent nature. When one receives knowledge of sambandha (one’s relationship with Kṛṣṇa), one worships Him in the form of the Holy Name.**

**Śiva o viṣṇura ki rūpe abheda-buddhi karibe (Understanding How Śiva and Viṣṇu are Non-different)**

### Text 69

*jaḍa-nāma, jaḍa-rūpa-guṇe yei bheda*  
*se-bheda cit-tattve nāi eita prabheda*

**Перевод:**

**“There are differences between material names, forms and qualities. Within cit-tattva (spiritual reality), there is no difference amongst these.**

### Text 70

*viṣṇu-tattve bheda-jñāna anartha-vikāra*  
*śivete viṣṇute bheda ati avicāra*

**Перевод:**

**“To cultivate bheda-jñāna (the understanding of differentiation between the Lord’s name, form etc.) in regards to viṣṇu-tattva, is an anartha and an aberration. To consider Śiva to be distinct from Viṣṇu is a great error. (14)**

**Комментарий:**

(14) It is faulty to consider differentiation in regards to viṣṇu-tattva. If one thinks that Śiva and the other Devatās are independent of Viṣṇu, then this consideration of difference arises.

**Bhakta o māyāvādīra ācāra o pravṛtti bheda (The Difference in the Conduct and Tendencies of a Devotee and a Māyāvādī)**

### Text 71

*nāmaika-śaraṇa yei bhakta mahājana*  
*ekeśvara kṛṣṇe bhaji’ chāḍe anya jana*

**Перевод:**

**“A devotee who is a mahājana takes exclusive shelter of the Holy Name. He worships Kṛṣṇa as the only Supreme Controller and rejects anyone else.**

### Text 72

*anya-deva, anya-śāstra nindā nāhi kare*  
*kṛṣṇa-dāsa bali’ anye pūje samādare*

**Перевод:**

**“He does not criticise other Devas and other śāstra. He worships and regards them as servants of Kṛṣṇa. (15)**

**Комментарий:**

(15) Why doesn’t a devotee of Kṛṣṇa criticize other Devas and other śāstra? Because he throws dry logic far away. Other śāstra try to establish the superiority of other Devas only to give approval to the eligibility of the path that some jīvas take. All the śāstras are trying to make them qualified in transcendental reality in order to make them into devotees of Kṛṣṇa; therefore one should not criticize different Devatās and śāstra. That criticism is an offence. Rejecting the notion of bheda-jñāna (that the Devas are independent controllers) one will attain the mercy of pure kṛṣṇa-bhakti.

### Text 73

*prati-dina gṛhi-bhakta nirmālya arpaṇe*  
*deva-pitṛ-sarva-jīve karena tarpaṇe*

**Перевод:**

**“Each day a householder devotee offers remnants (of Kṛṣṇa) in order to please the Devas, Pitṛs, and all the jīvas.**

### Text 74

*yathā yathā anya-deve karena darśana*  
*kṛṣṇa-dāsa bali’ tāṅre karena vandana*

**Перевод:**

**“Whenever he sees other Devas, he considers them to be servants of Kṛṣṇa and offers them respect.**

### Text 75

*māyāvādi-gaṇa yadi viṣṇu-pūjā kare*  
*prasāda-nirmālya bhakta nāhi laya ḍare*

**Перевод:**

**“If māyāvādīs perform worship of Viṣṇu, out of fear, the devotees will not accept such prasāda remnants.**

### Text 76

*māyāvādī hari-nāme aparādhī haya*  
*tāhāra pradatta pūjā hari nāhi laya*

**Перевод:**

**“Māyāvādīs are offenders to hari–nāma. Whatever worship they offer is not accepted by Hari.**

### Text 77

*anya-deva nirmālya grahaṇe aparādha*  
*śuddha-bhakti-sādhane sarvadā sādhe vāda*

**Перевод:**

**“Accepting the remnants of other Devas is an offence. In the practice of śuddha-bhakti such remnants are always avoided.**

### Text 78

*tabe yadi śuddha-bhakta śrī-kṛṣṇa pūjiyā*  
*anya-deve pūjā kare tat-prasāda diyā*

**Перевод:**

**“However, if a pure devotee worships Śrī Kṛṣṇa, he may also worship other Devas and then offer them that prasāda (which was offered to Kṛṣṇa).**

### Text 79

*se-prasāda grahaṇete nāhi aparādha*  
*sei-rūpa devārcane nāhi bhakti-vādha*

**Перевод:**

**“To accept such prasāda is not an offence. When worship of the Devas is done in this way, it is not an obstacle to bhakti.**

### Text 80

*śuddha-bhakta nāma-aparādhī nāhi haya*  
*nāma kari’ prema pāya, nāme deya jaya*

**Перевод:**

**“A pure devotee is never an offender to the Name. He chants the Holy Name, attains prema, and the Name bestows success upon him.**

**Ei aparādhera pratikāra (The Remedy for this Offence)**

### Text 81

*pramāde yadyapi haya anye viṣṇu-jñāna*  
*tabe anutāpe kari viṣṇu-tattva-dhyāna*

**Перевод:**

**“However, if out of foolishness one thinks Viṣṇu to be equal to others then, repenting, one should meditate upon viṣṇu-tattva.**

### Text 82

*śrī-viṣṇu-smariyā kari aparādha kṣaya*  
*yatne dekhi, āra nā se-aparādha haya*

**Перевод:**

**“By remembrance of Śrī Viṣṇu, one’s offence will be removed. Then one should be careful not to commit that offence again. (16)**

**Комментарий:**

(16) There is no greater penance in the world than the remembrance of Viṣṇu. Throughout the Vedas it is stipulated that brāhmaṇas who are in danger should take darśana of Viṣṇu’s feet. It is said that remembering kṛṣṇa-nāma and taking darśana of Viṣṇu’s feet are the same.

### Text 83

*pūrva-doṣa-kṣamā-śīla bhaktera bāndhava*  
*dayāra sāgara kṛṣṇa kṣamāra arṇava*

**Перевод:**

**“Kṛṣṇa is an ocean of forgiveness and an ocean of mercy. He is the friend of the devotees and is forgiving – He will pardon their previous mistakes.**

### Text 84

*bahu-deva-sevi-saṅga kariba varjana*  
*ekeśvara vaiṣṇavera kariba pūjana*

**Перевод:**

**“Abandoning the company of those who worship many Devas, a Vaiṣṇava worships the one Supreme Controller.**

### Text 85

*hari-dāsa-pade bhaktivinoda ye jana*  
*hari-nāma-cintāmaṇi tāṅhāra jīvana*

**Перевод:**

**Hari-nāma Cintāmaṇi is the life of Bhaktivinoda – that personality who resides at the feet of Hari Dāsa.**

## Chapter 6. Guror avajñā (Neglecting the Guru)

*guror-avajñā*

**“To neglect or disobey the guru.”**

### Text 1

*pañca-tattva jaya jaya śrī-rādhā-mādhava*  
*jaya navadvīpa-vraja-yamunā-vaiṣṇava*

**Перевод:**

**Glories to the Pañca-Tattva, glories to Śrī Rādhā-Mādhava. Glories to Navadvīpa, Vraja, the Yamunā and the Vaiṣṇavas.**

### Text 2

*hari-dāsa bale – prabhu kari nivedana*  
*tṛtīyāparādha nāme ye rūpe ghaṭana*

**Перевод:**

**Hari Dāsa said, “Oh Lord, on Your request, I will now speak about the third aparādha to the Name and what occurs when it appears.”**

### Text 3

*vistāri’ baliba āmi tomāri ājñāya*  
*yei saba aparādha guru-avajñāya*

**Перевод:**

**“By your order, I will describe in detail all the offences that proceed from neglecting the guru.”**

### Text 4

*bahu yoni bhrami’ mānava-śarīra,*  
*durlabha, śubhada ati*  
*tathāpi anitya, pāileka yei,*  
*yāvat jīvane sthiti*

**Перевод:**

**“After wandering through many wombs, one attains a human form which is very rare and extremely fortunate. However, having attained that status in life, such a body is also temporary.” (1)**

**Комментарий:**

(1) Wandering continuously through sixteen-thousand wombs, by the power of ajñāta-sukṛti (unknown spiritual merit), the jīva is awarded a human body. The human form is rare, because in the human body one may perform sādhana that leads towards spirituality, which cannot be executed in other bodies. In the body of a Deva one may only enjoy the results of previous karma, but one may not perform pious activities. In the form of animals, birds etc. there is a lack of knowledge and one cannot independently perform pious activities. Only the human body is suitable to perform bhajana of the Supreme.

### Text 5

*parama-maṅgala, labhibāre tare,*  
*yadi nā yatana kare*  
*punarāya bhave, anitya śarīra*  
*labhiyā ābāra mare*

**Перевод:**

**“If no endeavour is made to attain the highest auspiciousness, then one must take birth again in this mundane world, accept a temporary body, and die once again.”**

### Text 6

*subodha ye haya, durlabha nṛ-deha,*  
*labhiyā bhava saṁsāre*

**Перевод:**

**“One should understand how rare it is to attain the human form within the cycle of birth and death.”**

**Saṁsārī jīva avaśya sad-guru āśraya karibe (The Bound Jīva Must Take Shelter of a Genuine Guru)**

**Перевод:**

**“The guru is the captain of the ship, and one should take shelter of him, then by Kṛṣṇa’s favour, one can cross over (the ocean of birth and death). (2)**

**Комментарий:**

(2) The guru is the only captain of the ship to deliver the fallen jīvas across the ocean of repeated birth and death. All those persons who do not take refuge in the guru’s feet, but attempt to cross the ocean of birth and death by the power of their own intelligence are great fools. Without following the instructions of the guru, one can never achieve perfection in this world. How can one become perfect in order to attain the highest benefit without the instructions of a guru? One who is accomplished in the matter of transcendence is suitable to be a guru.

### Text 7

*śānta kṛṣṇa-bhakta, lakṣaṇa ye guru,*  
*sa-dainya vacane tāṅre*  
*santoṣa kariyā, kṛṣṇa-dīksā la’ya,*  
*yāya saṁsārera pāre*

**Перевод:**

**“One of the characteristics of a guru is that he is a peaceful devotee of Kṛṣṇa. One should inquire from such a person submissively, satisfy him and accept kṛṣṇa-dīkṣā from him. In this way one can cross over saṁsāra.**

### Text 8

*sahaje jīvera, āche kṛṣṇe mati*  
*vṛthā tarke tāhā yāya*  
*vitarka chāḍiyā, sumati āśraye,*  
*guru hate mantra pāya*

**Перевод:**

**“It is easy for the jīva’s intelligence to be fixed upon Kṛṣṇa when useless rhetoric has disappeared. By rejecting such logic and taking shelter of true wisdom, one should accept mantra from a guru.**

### Text 9

*gṛhī-jīva-gaṇa, varṇāśrame thāki,*  
*sad-guru āśraya kare \|*

**Перевод:**

**“Those jīvas who are householders should remain situated within varṇāśrama and take shelter of a genuine guru.**

**Brāhmaṇādi ucca-varṇe sat-pātra thākile tini haibāra yogya (Brāhmaṇas and Others from Higher Varṇas Should be Accepted as Guru if They are Suitable.)**

**Перевод:**

**“A brāhmaṇa is the ācārya of all the other varṇas if he follows kṛṣṇa-bhakti.**

### Text 10

*brāhmaṇa-kulete, supātra abhāve,*  
*anya kule dīkṣā pāya*  
*uccavarṇa guru, gṛhīra ucita,*  
*guru-śiṣya parīkṣāya*

**Перевод:**

**“In the absence of a qualified person from a brāhmaṇa family, one should accept dīkṣā from someone of another type of lineage. A householder should accept a guru from the higher varṇas. The guru and disciple should examine each other.**

**Varṇa-vicāra apekṣā supātrera vicāra adhika śreyaḥ (Consideration of Qualification is More Important than Varṇa)**

### Text 11

*kṛṣṇa-tattva-vettā, prakṛta ye haya,*  
*se haite pāre guru*  
*kibā vipra, śūdra, ki gṛhī, sannyāsī*  
*guru hana kalpa-taru*

**Перевод:**

**“It is natural that one who is conversant with kṛṣṇa–tattva is eligible to be a guru. Whether he is a brāhmaṇa, a śūdra, a householder or a sannyāsī, the guru is like a wish-fulfilling tree.**

### Text 12

*varṇera maryādā, pātrera vicāre,*  
*paramārthe laghu ati*  
*supātra milana, prayojana sadā,*  
*yadi cāi śuddhā rati*

**Перевод:**
