**“Considering suitability based upon the reputation of a particular varṇa is not very relevant from the highest perspective. Finding the proper qualifications is always the necessity if one desires to attain pure rati (attachment to the Lord).**

### Text 13

*supātrera prāpti, mūla-prayojana*  
*pavitra suvarṇa hena*  
*tāhe ucca-varṇa, labhile saṁyoga,*  
*sohāgā suvarṇe yena*

**Перевод:**

**“Proper eligibility is the foundation for accepting someone (as guru). Such a person is like pure gold. If one comes across such a person amongst the higher varṇas, then that person is as beautiful as gold. (3)**

**Комментарий:**

(3) One should accept a qualified guru. A guru from the higher varṇas is pleasing to society. Therefore, if he can find a suitable guru amongst the higher varṇas, a gṛhastha need not seek one amongst the lower varṇas. But it should be kept in mind that someone of a higher varṇa, or a kula-guru (family preceptor), who has no proper qualification should not be accepted as guru.

**Gṛha-tyāgī agṛhi-gurvāśraya karite pāren (A Householder who Renounces His Home Should Take Shelter of A Guru Who is not a Householder)**

### Text 14

*ye kona kāraṇe, sei gṛhi-dharma*  
*chāḍi’ anyāśrama laya*  
*tāhe paramārtha, nā pāiyā śeṣe*  
*sādhu-guru anveṣaya*

**Перевод:**

**“For some reason, if one renounces gṛhastha-dharma and accepts another āśrama, but ultimately cannot achieve the highest attainment, he should look for a sādhu-guru.**

### Text 15

*tāhāra pakṣete, agṛhī ācārya*  
*praśasta sakala mate*  
*tāṅra dīkṣā śikṣā, pāiyā se jana,*  
*bhāse nāma-rasāmṛte*

**Перевод:**

**“All accept that a non-gṛhastha ācārya is most suitable for such a person. Taking dīkṣā and śikṣā from him, that person will swim in the nectar of nāma-rasa. (4)**

**Комментарий:**

(4) One who renounces householder life must accept a genuine guru. He should accept a guru who has also renounced his home.

**Gṛhi-bhakta gṛha-tyāga karile-o pūrva-guru tyāga karite haya nā (The Householder Devotee who Renounces His Home Should not Reject His Previous Guru)**

### Text 16

*gṛhī bhakta-jane, virāga labhile,*  
*chāḍaye saṁsāra-vidhi*  
*tabu pūrva-guru, caraṇa-āśraya*  
*karibe jīvanāvadhi*

**Перевод:**

**“A householder devotee who attains the stage of renunciation should abandon the regulations of family life. But for his whole life he should continue to accept the shelter of the feet of his previous guru.**

### Text 17

*gṛhi-jana-madhye, gṛhi-guru ‘śasta,*  
*yadi śuddha-bhakta hana*  
*natuvā agṛhī, suyogya haile,*  
*guru-yogya sarva-kṣaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“Amongst householders, a householder guru is glorious if he is a pure devotee. Yet, one who has already renounced his home should search for an appropriate guru. At all times, the guru must be competent. (5)**

**Комментарий:**

(5) If a householder cannot find a genuine householder guru, then he may accept a genuine guru who is not a householder.

### Text 18

*sad-guru pāiyā, bhaktite bhajite,*  
*bhāvera udaya yabe*  
*saṁsāra virakti, saṁsāra chāḍiyā,*  
*vairāgī haibe tabe*

**Перевод:**

**“Having accepted a genuine guru, one should worship him with devotion. When bhāva arises, one becomes indifferent to the world and renounces it. Then he becomes a renunciant.**

**Yini vairāgya āśraye laibena tini vairāgī guru kariben (One Who Takes Refuge in Renunciation Should Accept a Guru who is a Renunciant)**

### Text 19

*vairāgya āśrama, grahaṇete tyāgī-*  
*puruṣa haibe guru*  
*tāṅhāra caraṇe, śikhibe virāga,*  
*guru śikṣā-kalpa-taru*

**Перевод:**

**“One in the vairāgya-āśrama (sannyāsa) should accept a renunciant as his guru. At his feet one will learn about renunciation. Such a guru is a wish-fulfilling tree of spiritual instructions. (6)**

**Комментарий:**

(6) A householder who has accepted the vairāgya-āśrama should seek out a suitable renunciant-guru and accept the clothes and paraphernalia appropriate for the stage of a mendicant (bhikṣu-āśrama).

**Dīkṣā o śikṣā-guru ubhayakei samāna sammāna karā āvaśyaka (The Dīkṣā and Śikṣā-Gurus Should Both be Given Equal Respect)**

### Text 20

*dīkṣā-śikṣā bhede, guru du’ prakāra*  
*ubhaye samāna māna*  
*arpibe sujana, paramārtha dhana,*  
*anāyāse yadi cāna*

**Перевод:**

**“Dīkṣa and śikṣā are the two kinds of gurus, and both should be respected equally. If they so desire, they can easily bestow the highest wealth unto a fit recipient.**

### Text 21

*kṛṣṇa-nāma-mantra, dena dīkṣā-guru*  
*śikṣā-guru tattva-dātā*  
*vaiṣṇava sakala, śikṣā-guru hana,*  
*sarva-śubha-janayitā*

**Перевод:**

**“The dīkṣā–guru gives the kṛṣṇa–nāma mantra, and the śikṣā–guru imparts tattva (knowledge of bhakti). All Vaiṣṇavas can become śīkṣa–gurus and are thus the creators of all auspiciousness. (7)**

**Комментарий:**

(7) There are two classes of guru. The one who gives mantra-dīkṣā is the dīkṣā-guru. Those who give teachings on sambandha-tattva are śikṣā-gurus. Only can have only one dīkṣa-guru.

**Sampradāyera ādi-gurura śikṣā avalambana kariyā ācaraṇa karibe (One Must Behave According to the Teachings of the Ādi-Guru of the Sampradāya)**

### Text 22

*sādhu-sampradāye, ācārya sakala*  
*śikṣā-guru pratiṣṭhita*  
*ādyācārya yini, guru-śiromaṇi*  
*pūji tāṅ’re yathocita*

**Перевод:**

**“All the ācāryas in the sādhu–sampradāya worship the ādi–ācārya (original guru), who is the crest-jewel of gurus – the śikṣā–guru who established the sampradāya. (8)**

**Комментарий:**

(8) ‘Sādhu-sampradāya’ means a Vaiṣṇava sampradāya. The sādhu-paramparā imparts mantras, tattva (knowledge), and teachings concerning sādhya (the goal) and sādhana (the method how to attain the goal). In order to protect oneself from evil sampradāyas such as those of the māyāvādīs etc., one should accept a guru from a sādhu-sampradāya. One should respect the teachings imparted by the original ācāryas of the sādhu-sampradāyas: Śrī Rāmānuja, Śrī Madhva-muni, Śrī Nimbāditya and Śrī Viṣṇu-svāmī are the original ācāryas of their respective sampradāyas.

### Text 23

*tāṅ’ra su-siddhānta, anugata ha’ye*  
*nā māniba anya śikṣā*  
*tāṅhāra ādeśa, pāliba yatane,*  
*nā laiba anya dīkṣā*

**Перевод:**

**“They follow his wonderful philosophical conclusions and do not accept any other philosophy. They endeavour to carry out his teachings and do not accept dīkṣā anywhere else.**

**Sampradāya guru varaṇa karā kartavya (One Must Accept a Guru From Within a Sampradāya)**

### Text 24

*sampradāya guru-gaṇe śikṣā guru jāni*  
*anya-mata paṇḍitera śikṣā nāhi māni*

**Перевод:**

**“These gurus are conversant with the teachings of the original gurus of the sampradāya. They do not accept the teachings of any other paṇḍita who expounds another philosophy.**

### Text 25

*sei mate suśikṣita sādhu sucarita*  
*dīkṣā-guru-yogya sadā jāne supaṇḍita*

**Перевод:**

**“One who carefully studies these teachings is a sādhu of good character. One should know that such a true paṇḍita is always qualified to be a dikṣā–guru.**

**Māyāvādīra nikaṭa kṛṣṇa-mantra laile paramārtha haya nā (One Never Attains the Greatest Benefit by Accepting Kṛṣṇa-mantra from a Māyāvādī)**

### Text 26

*māyāvādi-mate thāke kṛṣṇa-mantra laya*  
*tāra paramārtha lābha kabhu nāhi haya*

**Перевод:**

**“One will never attain the highest spiritual benefit by accepting kṛṣṇa-mantra from someone who follows māyāvāda philosophy.**

**Śuddha-bhakta vyatīta anyake guru karibe nā (Except for a Pure Devotee, No One Else Can be a Guru)**

### Text 27

*ye anyāya śikhe, yei śikṣā deya āra*  
*ubhaye narake yāya, nā pāya uddhāra*

**Перевод:**

**“Both the person who expounds a different philosophy and the person who accepts it go to hell. They cannot be saved.**

### Text 28

*śuddha-bhakti chāḍi’ yini śikhilena vāda*  
*tāṅhāra jīvana mātra vāda-visaṁvāda*

**Перевод:**

**“The life of one who rejects śuddha–bhakti and teaches another philosophy is simply spent in arguments and counter-arguments.**

### Text 29

*se kemane guru habe, uddhāribe jīve*  
*āpani asiddha anye kibā śubha dibe*

**Перевод:**

**“How can such a person become a guru and deliver the jīvas? If he is imperfect, how can he bestow auspiciousness upon others?**

### Text 30

*ataeva śuddha-bhakta ye se kene naya*  
*upayukta guru haya, sarva-śāstre kaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Thus, how can someone who is not a śuddha–bhakta be eligible to be guru? This is stated in all the śāstra.**

**Guru-tattva (The Principle of Guru)**

### Text 31

*dīkṣā-guru śikṣā-guru duṅhe kṛṣṇa-dāsa*  
*duṅhe vraja-jana kṛṣṇa-śaktira prakāśa*

**Перевод:**

**“The dīkṣā–guru and śikṣā–guru are both kṛṣṇa–dāsa. They are residents of Vraja and manifestations of Kṛṣṇa’s śakti.**

### Text 32

*guruke sāmānya jīva nā jānibe kabhu*  
*guru—kṛṣṇa-śakti kṛṣṇa-preṣṭha nitya-prabhu*

**Перевод:**

**“One should never consider the guru to be an ordinary jīva. The guru is Kṛṣṇa’s śakti, most dear to Kṛṣṇa, and he is one’s eternal master. (9)**

**Комментарий:**

(9) One should never consider the guru to be an ordinary jīva. Devotion must be given to the guru as one who is nourished by Kṛṣṇa’s svarūpa-śakti and as a personal associate of Kṛṣṇa. To think of the guru as Kṛṣṇa is the philosophy of the māyāvādīs, and not that of the pure Vaiṣṇavas. Sādhu devotees are extremely careful of this. If it is evident that māyāvāda has entered into one’s sādhana, then all of one’s sādhana is ruined.

### Text 33

*ei buddhi-saha sadā guru-bhakti kare*  
*sei guru-bhakti-bale saṁsārete tare*

**Перевод:**

**“With this mindset, one should always have guru–bhakti. By the power of such guru–bhakti, one can cross over the repeated cycle of birth and death.**

**Guru pūjā (The Worship of Guru)**

### Text 34

*agre guru-pūjā pare śrī-kṛṣṇa-pūjana*  
*gurudeve śrī-kṛṣṇa-prasāda-samarpaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“One should worship the guru first, and worship Śrī Kṛṣṇa after. Gurudeva gives Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s mercy. (10)**

**Комментарий:**

(10) One worships the guru by offering him āsana (a seat), pādya (foot-wash), arghya (ceremonial water), snānīya (bathing), vastra (garments), and ābharaṇa (ornaments), and with his permission one then worships the Divine Couple. After that worship, the prasāda such as drinks etc. should be first offered to the guru, and then to other Vaiṣnavas and the Devatās. Then prasāda may be offered to the pitṛs.

### Text 35

*guru ājñā la’ye kṛṣṇa pūjibe yatane*  
*śrī-guru smariyā kṛṣṇa balibe vadane*

**Перевод:**

**“Following the guru’s instructions, one should carefully worship Kṛṣṇa. Remembering Śrī Guru, one should chant the Name of Kṛṣṇa.**

**Gurute ki rūpe śraddhā karā ucita (What Kind of Faith Should one Have in the Guru?)**

### Text 36

*gurute avajñā yā’ra tā’ra aparādha*  
*sei aparādhe tā’ra haya bhakti-vādha*

**Перевод:**

**“To neglect the guru is an offence. This offence obstructs one’s bhakti.**

### Text 37

*guru-kṛṣṇa vaiṣṇavete sama bhakti kari’*  
*nāmāśraye śuddha-bhakta śīghra yāya tari’*

**Перевод:**

**“Having equal bhakti for the guru, Kṛṣṇa and the Vaiṣṇavas, and by taking shelter of the Holy Name, a śuddha–bhakta is quickly delivered.**

### Text 38

*gurute acalā śraddhā kare yei jana*  
*śuddha-nāma-bale sei pāya prema-dhana*

**Перевод:**

**“By steady śraddhā in the guru, and by the power śuddha–nāma, the great wealth of prema is attained.**

**Kon sthāne guru tyāga karite haibe (In Which Situation One Should Reject the Guru)**

### Text 39

*tabe yadi e rūpa ghaṭanā kabhu haya*  
*asat-saṅge gurura yogyatā haya kṣaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Through asat–saṅga a person’s eligibility to become guru may be ruined if events such as the following occur.**

### Text 40

*prathame chilena tini sad-guru pradhāna*  
*pare nāma-aparādhe hañā hata-jñāna*

**Перевод:**

**“At first, he may be the best amongst sad–gurus, but after making offences to the Holy Name, his knowledge is destroyed.**

### Text 41

*vaiṣṇave vidveṣa kari’ chāḍe nāma-rasa*  
*krame krame hana artha-kāminīra vaśa*

**Перевод:**

**“Becoming envious of the Vaiṣṇavas, he gives up nāma–rasa. Eventually he becomes a slave to wealth and women.**

### Text 42

*sei guru chāḍi’ śiṣya śrī-kṛṣṇa-kṛpāya*  
*sad-guru labhiyā punaḥ śuddha-nāma gāya*

**Перевод:**

**“Rejecting that guru, and by the mercy of Śrī Kṛṣṇa, a disciple may again find a sad–guru who chants śuddha–nāma.**

**Guru-śiṣya sambandhera pūrvei parasparera parīkṣā (Before the Guru-Disciple Relationship, They Must Examine One Another)**

### Text 43

*ayogya-śiṣyere guru kariben daṇḍa*  
*bhajiyā ayogya-guru śiṣya haya paṇḍa*

**Перевод:**

**“A guru that accepts an unqualified disciple is punished. The disciple who worships an unqualified guru becomes hopeless.**

### Text 44

*duṅhera yogyatā yata dina sthira raya*  
*paraspara sambandha kakhana tyajya naya*

**Перевод:**

**“As long as they are both qualified, their relationship should not be abandoned. (11)**

**Комментарий:**

(11) The relationship between guru and disciple is eternal. As long as there is mutual eligibility, their relationship will not be broken. If the guru becomes wicked, the disciple must give him up, and if the disciple becomes wicked, then the guru must give him up. If not, then both will fall down. Kindly look at the Pramāṇa-māla for further evidence about giving up such a relationship.

**Śuddha-guru parīkṣā kariyā varaṇa karibe (After Examining the Pure Guru, One Should Accept Him)**

### Text 45

*sad-gurura prati yei avajñā ācare*  
*se pāpiṣṭha aparādhī sarvatra saṁsāre*

**Перевод:**

**“One who disrespects the sad–guru is the worst miscreant and offender in the whole world.**

### Text 46

*ataeva prathame viśeṣa yatna kari’*  
*śuddha-bhakte laibena guru-rūpe vari’*

**Перевод:**

**“Thus, at the very beginning one should take great care in finding a śuddha–bhakta to be one’s guru. (12)**

**Комментарий:**

(12) Before a guru is accepted, śāstra instructs that the guru and disciple should test and examine one another. This does not refer to the kula-guru (family guru). If a kula-guru is qualified then there is no issue. If he is unqualified, then one should search out a guru amongst sādhu-gurus. At the time of purchasing something, one examines the object and wants to know where it is from, thus only someone who is extremely unfortunate would not endeavour to properly examine and select a guru who is the greatest friend of the jīvas. An unqualified kula-guru should be begged for permission and shown respect, then one should bid farewell to him and search out a sad-guru.

### Text 47

*guru-tyāga-kleśa yena kabhu nāhi ghaṭe*  
*e rūpa cintile kabhu nā paḍe saṅkaṭe*

**Перевод:**

**“One should be concerned that he should never have to undergo the misery of having to reject his guru and fall into such a calamity.**

### Text 48

*guru yathā bhakti-hīna śiṣya tā’ra prāya*  
*ataeva śuddha-guru la’be parīkṣāya*

**Перевод:**

**“Just as the guru may be devoid of bhakti, so the disciple may also be. Therefore, the pure guru must examine him.**

### Text 49

*sad-guru-avajñā – aparādha bhayaṅkara*  
*ei aparādhe naṣṭa haya deva-nara*

**Перевод:**

**“To disrespect the sad-guru is a dreadful offence. When such an offence is committed, Devas and humans are doomed.**

**Guru sevāra prakriyā (Ways in Which to Serve the Guru)**

### Text 50

*guru-śayyāsana āra pādukādi, yāna*  
*pāda-pīṭha, snānodaka, chāyāra laṅghana*

**Перевод:**

**“One should not disrespect the guru’s bed, seat, shoes, vehicle, foot-stool, bath-water and shadow.**

### Text 51

*gurura agrete anya pūjā-dvaita-jñāna*  
*dīkṣā, vyākhyā, prabhutvādi karibe varjana*

**Перевод:**

**“One should not worship anyone else before the guru, considering him to be secondary. One should not give dīkṣā or lectures (in front of the guru) nor try to lord over him.**

### Text 52

*yathā yathā gurura pāibe daraśana*  
*daṇḍavat paḍi’ bhūme karibe vandana*

**Перевод:**

**“Whenever one sees the guru, one should offer daṇḍavats, falling to the ground and offering respects.**

### Text 53

*guru-nāma bhaktite karibe uccāraṇa*  
*guru-ājñā helā nā karibe kadācana*

**Перевод:**

**“One should utter his guru’s name with bhakti. One should never, ever, disrespect the guru.**

### Text 54

*gurura prasāda sevā avaśya karibe*  
*gurura apriya vākya kabhu nā kahibe*

**Перевод:**

**“One should honour the prasāda-remnants of the guru. One must never speak unpleasant words to the guru.**

### Text 55

*gurura caraṇe dainye laibe śaraṇa*  
*karibe gurura sadā priya-ācaraṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“One must surrender with humility at the guru’s feet. One must always behave in such a way that one will become dear to the guru.**

### Text 56

*e-rūpa ācāre kṛṣṇa-nāma-saṅkīrtane*  
*sarva-siddhi haya prabho bale śruti-gaṇe*

**Перевод:**

**“Behaving in this way and performing kṛṣṇa–nāma–saṅkīrtana, one attains all perfection. O Lord, this has been declared by the śruti.**

### Text 57

*nāma-guru prati yadi avajñā ghaṭaye*  
*duṣṭa-saṅge duṣṭa-śāstra-mata-samāśraye*

**Перевод:**

**“If one associates with wicked people, or takes refuge in the philosophy found in evil śāstra, then one disobeys the nāma–guru (13).**

### Text 58

*tabe sei saṅga, sei śāstra dūra kari’*  
*vilāpa kariba sei guru-pade dhari’*

**Перевод:**

**“Then one should cast that association or that śāstra far away, and lamenting, one should grasp the guru’s feet.**

### Text 59

*kṛpā kari’ gurudeva haibe sadaya*  
*nāme prema dibe se vaiṣṇava dayāmaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Being kind, the guru will bestow his mercy. Being full of compassion, that Vaiṣṇava will give one nāma–prema.”**

### Text 60

*hari-dāsa pada-reṇu bharasā yāhāra*  
*nāma-cintāmaṇi gāya tṛṇādhika chāra*

**Перевод:**

**He who considers himself lower than a blade of grass, who has faith in the dust of Hari Dāsa’s feet, sings this Hari-nāma-cintāmaṇi.**

## Chapter 7. Śruti-śāstra-nindanam (Criticism of the Śruti-śāstra)

*śruti-śāstra-nindānam*

**“Blaspheming the śruti-śāstra”.**

### Text 1

*jaya jaya gadāi-gaurāṅga nityānanda*  
*jaya sītā-pati jaya gaura-bhakta-vṛnda*

**Перевод:**

**Glories to Gadādhara, Gaurāṅga and Nityānanda. Glories to the husband of Sīta (Advaita) and the devotees.**

### Text 2

*hari-dāsa bale prabhu caturthāparādha*  
*śruti-śāstra-vinindana bhakti-rasa-vādha*

**Перевод:**

**Hari Dāsa said – “Lord, the fourth offence is criticism of the śruti-śāstra which obstructs bhakti–rasa.**

**Āmnāya-i eka-mātra pramāṇa (The Vedas are the only Evidence)**

### Text 3

*śruti-śāstra – veda-upaniṣat-purāṇa*  
*kṛṣṇa-niśvasita haya sarvatra pramāṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“The śruti–śāstra, the Vedas, the Upaniṣads and the Purāṇas – all these pramāṇas (evidences) have issued from the breathing of Kṛṣṇa.**

### Text 4

*viśeṣataḥ aprākṛta-tattve jñāna yata*  
*sakali āmnāya-siddha tāhe ha-i rata*

**Перевод:**

**“They especially contain knowledge of transcendental truths which is beyond the range of the mundane senses (aprākṛta-tattva). The entire Vedas are perfect.**

### Text 5

*jaḍātīta vastu indriyera agocara*  
*kṛṣṇa-kṛpā vinā tāhā nā haya gocara*

**Перевод:**

**“That which transcends dull matter is beyond the senses. Without Kṛṣṇa’s mercy one cannot contact that. (1)**

**Комментарий:**

(1) The mundane senses can only perceive objects comprised of matter. The senses have no power to perceive that which is beyond the mundane. Kṛṣṇa is spiritual by nature and transcends matter. Thus Kṛṣṇa kindly gives the knowledge of āmnāya (the Vedas) so that the jīvas will attain auspiciousness. The word ‘āmnāya’ is used in genuine sampradāyas to refer to the statements of the Vedas.

### Text 6

*karaṇāpaṭava, bhrama, vipralipsā āra*  
*pramāda, sarvatra nara-jñāne ei cāra*

**Перевод:**

**“Throughout human knowledge we find four things – flawed senses (karaṇāpāṭava), the tendency to make mistakes (bhrama), the cheating propensity (vipralipsā), and falling into bewilderment (pramāda).**

### Text 7

*sei saba doṣa-śūnya veda catuṣṭaya*  
*veda vinā paramārthe gati nāhi haya*

**Перевод:**

**“The four Vedas are devoid of all these faults. Without the Vedas there is no other path to the highest goal.**

### Text 8

*māyā-baddha-jīve kṛṣṇa bahu kṛpā kari’*  
*veda-purāṇādi dila ārṣa-jñāne dhari’*

**Перевод:**

**“Being extremely merciful to the jīvas who are bound by Māyā, Kṛṣṇa took the knowledge of the sages in the form of the Vedas, Purāṇas etc. (2)**

**Комментарий:**

(2) Ārṣa-jñāna – that knowledge that the ṛṣis attained while in samādhi. That is ārṣa-jñāna.

**Āmnāya haite daśa-mūla śikṣā prameya nayaṭī (The Ten Fundamental Teachings and Nine Evidences in the Vedas)**

### Text 9

*sei śruti-śāstre jāni – karma-jñāna chāra*  
*nirmala-bhaktite mātra pāi sarva-sāra*

**Перевод:**

**“I know that the śruti-śāstra considers karma and jñāna to be as worthless as ashes, and that only the attainment of pure bhakti is most essential.**

### Text 10

*māyā-mūḍha-jīve karma-jñāne śuddha kari’*  
*śuddha-bhakti adhikāra śikhāile hari*

**Перевод:**

**“The jīvas are fooled by māyā who purifies them through karma and jñāna, and when they are qualified, Hari teaches them about śuddha-bhakti. (3)**

**Комментарий:**

(3) The perfect knowledge of the śruti says that karma and jñāna produce only negligible results, and teaches that the essential truth of pure bhakti is the highest attainment.

### Text 11

*pramāṇa se veda-vākya nayaṭī prameya*  
*śikhāya sambandha, prayojana, abhidheya*

**Перевод:**

**“The pramāṇa of the Vedic statements reveal nine prameyas (objects of knowledge). Their teachings are sambandha, abhidheya and prayojana (the relationship between the jīva and īśvara, the method of attainment, and the goal).**

### Text 12

*ei daśa-mūla-sāra avidyā vināśa*  
*kariyā jīvera kare suvidyā prakāśa*

**Перевод:**

**“The essence of this is the Daśa-mūla (ten fundamental teachings) which destroys the ignorance of the jīva and manifests divine knowledge. (4)**

**Комментарий:**

(4) The Daśa-mūla states that there is one pramāṇa, meaning the Vedic statements; the prameyas are nine – 1. Hari is the Supreme Truth. 2. His form is that of Śyāmasundara, and He is the master of all potencies. 3. The form of Śyāmasundara is replete with supreme rasa; His abode is the spiritual sky. 4. The unlimited jīvas are spiritual atomic particles (cit-paramāṇu) and they are separated parts of Kṛṣna (vibhinnāṁśa). The two types of jīva are eternally bound (nitya-baddha) and eternally liberated (nitya-mukta). 5. The jīvas who are averse to Kṛṣṇa are bound by Māyā. 6. Pure devotees are free from the influence of Māyā. 7. The jīvas and the entire world comprised of gross matter are produced by the potency of inconceivability (acintya-śakti) and are manifestations that are eternally one and different from the Supreme. 8. Abhidheya-tattva refers to the nine processes in kṛṣṇa-bhakti. 9. Kṛṣna-prema is the prayojana-tattva.

**1. hari eka para-tattva, 2. tini sarva-śaktimān, 3. tini rasa-mūrti (Hari is the Supreme Truth, the Possessor of All Potencies, and the Embodiment of Rasa)**

### Text 13

*prathame śikhāya – para-tattva eka hari*  
*śyāma sarva-śaktimān, rasa-mūrti-dhārī*

**Перевод:**

**“The first teaching is that Hari is the Supreme Truth. Śyāma is the possessor of all potencies and the embodiment of rasa.**

### Text 14

*jīvera paramānanda karena vidhāna*  
*saṁvyoma-dhāmete tāṅra nitya adhiṣṭhāna*

**Перевод:**

**“He gives supreme bliss to the jīvas and He eternally resides in His abode in the spiritual sky (saṁvyoma).**

### Text 15

*e-tina prameya haya śrī-kṛṣṇa-viṣaye*  
*veda-śāstra śikṣā dena jīvera hṛdaye*

**Перевод:**

**“The Veda-śāstra places these three prameyas concerning Śrī Kṛṣṇa in the hearts of the jīvas.**

**4. Jīva-tattva (The Jīva Principle)**

### Text 16

*dvitīye śikhāya vibhinnāṁśa jīva-tattva*  
*ananta-saṅkhyaka cit-paramāṇu-sattva*

**Перевод:**

**“The second teaching is in regard to jīva–tattva, who are vibhinnāṁśa (part and parcel of the Supreme). They are infinite in number and atomic units of consciousness.**

**5. Nitya-baddha o 6. nitya mukta bhede jīva dui prakāra (The Two Different Types of Jīvas are eternally bound and eternally liberated)**

### Text 17

*nitya-baddha nitya-mukta bhede jīva dvi-prakāra*  
*saṁvyoma, brahmāṇḍa bhari’ saṁsthiti tāhāra*

**Перевод:**

**“The jīvas are of two types – eternally bound (nitya-baddha) and eternally liberated (nitya-mukta). Respectively, they reside in the spiritual sky and the material universe.**

**Baddha jīva (The Bound Jīva)**

### Text 18

*baddha-jīva māyā bhaji’ kṛṣṇa-bahirmukha*  
*ananta-brahmāṇḍe bhoga kare duḥkha-sukha*

**Перевод:**

**“Being averse to Kṛṣṇa, the bound jīvas worship māyā. They enjoy and suffer in unlimited universes.**

**Mukta jīva (The Liberated Jīvas)**

### Text 19

*nitya-mukta kṛṣṇa bhaji’ kṛṣṇa-pāriṣada*  
*para-vyome bhoga kare premera sampada*

**Перевод:**

**“The eternally liberated jīvas worship Kṛṣṇa, and they are His associates. They enjoy in the spiritual sky and possess the wealth of prema.**

### Text 20

*tinaṭī prameya ei jīvera viṣaye*  
*śruti-śāstra śikṣā dena kṛṣṇa-dāsī haye*

**Перевод:**

**“Theses are the three prameyas in regards to the jīva. The śruti-śāstras, which are Kṛṣṇa’s maidservants, teach this.**

**7. Acintya-bhedābheda sambandha (The Jīva in Relation to Simultaneous Oneness and Difference)**

### Text 21

*cid-vyāpāra āra yata jaḍera vyāpāra*  
*sakali acintya-bhedābheda prakāra*

**Перевод:**

**“Whether they are in the spiritual world or the material world, they are all inconceivably one and different (from the Lord).**

### Text 22

*jīva, jaḍa* ***–*** *sarva-vastu kṛṣṇa-śaktimaya*  
*avicintya-bhedābheda śruti-śāstre kaya*

**Перевод:**

**“All things, including the jīvas and matter are Kṛṣṇa’s potencies and inconceivably one and different from Him. The śruti-śāstras declare this.**

### Text 23

*ei jñāne jīva jāne* ***–*** *āmi kṛṣṇa dāsa*  
*kṛṣṇa mora nitya prabhu cit-sūrya-prakāśa”*

**Перевод:**

**“Through this knowledge the jīva understands ‘I am kṛṣṇa-dāsa. Kṛṣṇa is my eternal Master. He appears like a spiritual sun.’**

### Text 24

*śakti-pariṇāma-mātra veda-śāstre bale*  
*vivartādi-duṣṭa-mate veda-ninde chale*

**Перевод:**

**“The Veda-śāstra speaks about śakti-pariṇāma (the idea that the world is a manifestation of Kṛṣṇa’s energies). Vivarta (the idea that the world is a direct transformation of the Lord) is a corrupt philosophy – a deception created by offenders of the Vedas. (5)**

**Комментарий:**

(5) The Vedas teach that the world is a manifestation of the Supreme’s acintya-śakti. The concept that the world is a direct transformation, or a direct manifestation of the Supreme, is an ideology that is completely opposed to the Vedas.

**Sātaṭī prameya sambandha jñāna (The Seventh Prameya Concerns Sambandha-jñāna)**

### Text 25

*ei-ta’ sambandha-jñāna sātaṭī prameya*  
*śruti-śāstra śikṣā dena ati-upādeya*

**Перевод:**

**“This seventh prameya concerns sambandha-jñāna. The śruti-śāstras give this most excellent teaching.**

### Text 26

*veda punaḥ śikṣā dena abhidheya-sāra*  
*nava-vidha kṛṣṇa-bhakti vidhi, rāga āra*

**Перевод:**

**“The Vedas also give instructions on essential abhidheya, the nine processes of kṛṣṇa-bhakti, as well as vidhi (devotion according to rules) and rāga (spontaneous devotion for Kṛṣṇa).**

**8. abhidheya – nava-vidha bhakti (Abhideya – the Nine Processes of Bhakti)**

### Text 27

*śravaṇa, kīrtana, smṛti, pūjana, vandana*  
*paricaryā, dāsya, sakhya, ātma-nivedana*

**Перевод:**

**“Hearing, chanting, remembering, worshipping, offering prayers, offering service, becoming the Lord’s servant, becoming the Lord’s friend and offering oneself fully.**

### Text 28

*bhaktira prakāra-madhye nāma sarva-sāra*  
*praṇava-māhātmya veda karena pracāra*

**Перевод:**

**“Amongst these various types of bhakti, chanting the Holy Name is the most essential. Thus the Vedas have propagated the glories of praṇava (oṁ).**

**9. Prayojana kṛṣṇa-prema (The Goal is Kṛṣṇa-prema)**

### Text 29

*śuddha-bhakti-samāśraya kariyā mānava*  
*kṛṣṇa-kṛpā-bale pāya premera vaibhava*

**Перевод:**

**“If a human being takes refuge in śuddha–bhakti, then by the power of Kṛṣṇa’s mercy, he attains the wealth of prema. (6)**

**Комментарий:**

(6) Here, śuddha-bhakti refers to the state where the mind is fixed upon that which is favourable for service to Kṛṣṇa with no other desire than to increase one’s bhakti. In this state, one is not covered by karma, jñāna or yoga, but the mind is fixed upon śuddha-bhakti. Bhakti which is mixed with karma or jñāna cannot be said to be śuddha-bhakti. All the Vedas unanimously teach that one should engage in śuddha-bhakti by taking refuge in nāma.

**Ei śruti-śikṣā nindā aparādha (Criticising the Teachings of the Śruti is an Offence)**

### Text 30

*e nava-prameya śruti karena pramāṇa*  
*śruti-tattvābhijña guru balena sandhāna*

**Перевод:**

**“The śruti is the pramāṇa that teaches these nine prameyas. The guru who is learned in these tattvas from the śruti is able to reveal them.**

### Text 31

*e-hena śrutira yei kare vinindana*  
*nāma-aparādhī sei narādhama jana*

**Перевод:**

**“One who criticises the śruti is an offender to nāma. He is the lowest of men.**

**Veda-viruddha vāda-samūha (The Various Philosophies that Oppose the Vedas)**

### Text 32

*jaiminī, kapila, nagna, nāstika, sugata*  
*gautama* ***–*** *e-chaya jana hetuvāde rata*

**Перевод:**

**“Jaiminī, Kapila, Nagna, Nāstika, Sugata and Gautama – these six personalities are devoted to argumentative ideologies.**

### Text 33

*veda māne mukhe, tabu īśa nāhi māne*  
*karma-kāṇḍa śreṣṭha bali’ jaiminī vākhāne*

**Перевод:**

**“Their mouths talk about following the Vedas, but none of them mention a Supreme. Jaiminī declares that karma-kāṇḍa is the highest.**

### Text 34

*īśvara asiddha, kapilera kalpanāya*  
*tabu yoga māne artha bujhā nāhi yāya*

**Перевод:**

**“Kapila imagines that īśvara is imperfect, but he accepts yoga without understanding its meaning.**

### Text 35

*nagna se tāmasa-tantra karaya vistāra*  
*vedera viruddha-dharma karaye pracāra*

**Перевод:**

**“Nagna propagated tāmasika tantras and preached a religion which was opposed to the Vedas.**

**Ei saba matavāda dvārā śruti-nindā haya (One Criticises the Śruti Through All These Philosophies)**

### Text 36

*nāstika cārvāka kabhu veda nāhi māne*  
*sugata, bauddherā eka prakāra vākhāne*

**Перевод:**

**“Nāstika Cārvāka did not accept the Vedas. Sugata described a type of Buddhism.**

### Text 37

*gautama nyāyera kartā īśvara nā bhaje*  
*tāra hetuvāda-mate nara-mātra maje*

**Перевод:**

**“Gautama, the authority on nyāya, did not worship īśvara. These are the six argumentative philosophical schools that humans become fully absorbed in.**

**Ei sab mata-vāda dvārā śruti-nindā haya (All these Doctrines Blaspheme the Śruti)**

### Text 38

*ei saba duṣṭa mate śrutira nindana*  
*kabhu spaṣṭa kabhu gupta, bujhe vijña-jana*

**Перевод:**

**“All these corrupt philosophies criticise the śruti – sometimes openly, sometimes in secret, but those who are wise understand.**

### Text 39

*ei saba mate thāki’ aparādhī haya*  
*ataeva ei sabe tyajibe niścaya*

**Перевод:**

**“All those that accept these philosophies are offenders. Thus, everyone should certainly avoid their association.**

**Māyāvādīra ati duṣṭa mata – veda viruddha (The Philosophy of the Māyāvādīs is the Most Corrupt – it is Opposed to the Vedas)**

### Text 40

*ei-saba ku-mata chāḍi’ āra māyāvāda*  
*śuddha-bhakti anubhavi’ haya nirvivāda*

**Перевод:**

**“One should reject all these evil ideologies including māyāvāda, which are opposed to the realisations of śuddha–bhakti.**

### Text 41

*māyāvāda asat-śāstra gupta-bauddha-mata*  
*vedārtha-vikṛti kali-kālete sammata*

**Перевод:**

**“Māyāvāda is asat-śāstra (opposed to the śāstra) and is concealed Buddhism which mutilates the meaning of the Vedas. It is respected in the age of Kali.**

### Text 42

*umāpati brāhmaṇa-rūpete prakāśila*  
*tomāra ājñāya teṅha ācārya haila*

**Перевод:**

**“The Lord of Umā (Śiva) appeared in the form of a brāhmaṇa, and on Your order became the ācārya (of this philosophy).**

### Text 43

*jaiminī ye-rūpa mukhe veda-mātra māne*  
*vikṛta śrutira artha jagate vākhāne*

**Перевод:**

**“Just as Jaiminī gave lip-service about accepting the Vedas, he (Śiva) gave the world a deformed meaning of the śruti.**

### Text 44

*māyāvādī guru sei-rūpa bauddha-dharma*  
*veda-vākye sthāpi’ ācchādila bhakti-marma*

**Перевод:**

**“In this way, māyāvādī gurus establish Buddhist dharma using Vedic statements, concealing any meaning concerning bhakti. (7)**

**Комментарий:**

(7) Aṣṭāvakra, Dattātreya, Govinda, Gauḍapāda, Śaṅkara, the followers of Śaṅkara, and the jvara-mīmāṁsakas (followers of Jaiminī) are māyāvādī gurus. The attainment of nirvāṇa by the jīva is the central teaching of Buddhist dharma. Although they do not accept the existence of Brahman, they accept that the highest reality is śūnyavāda (the idea of merging into nothingness), which is identical in all respects with the māyāvādīs merging into the qualityless consciousness of Brahman. These philosophies are completely opposed to the eternality of bhakti-tattva.

### Text 45

*ei saba matavāde bhakti dūre yāya*  
*śrī-kṛṣṇa-nāmete jīva aparādha pāya*

**Перевод:**

**“All these philosophies keep bhakti at a distance. By following them, the jīva makes an offence to śrī-kṛṣṇa-nāma. (8)**

**Комментарий:**

(8) Those who accept any of these ideologies, but nevertheless chant kṛṣṇa-nāma—such māyāvādīs are destroyed by committing an offence to the Holy Name.

**Śruti-vicāre śuddha prakriyā (Consulting the Śruti is the Method of Purification)**

### Text 46

*śrutira abhidhā-vṛtti kari’ saṁyojana*  
*śuddha-bhakti labhi’ pāya jīva prema-dhana*

**Перевод:**

**“When the primary meanings (abhidhā-vṛtti) of the śruti are combined, one eventually attains śuddha-bhakti. Then the jīva acquires the wealth of prema. (9)**

**Комментарий:**

(9) When the primary meaning (abhidhā-lakṣaṇa) is applicable, then it is improper to accept secondary meanings (lakṣaṇa) in their place. Keeping this in mind, by accepting the abhidhā-vṛtti of a Vedic statement, one can learn the pure teachings of bhakti-tattva. Please look at the Pramāṇa-mālā for the meaning of abhidhā and lakṣaṇa.

### Text 47

*śrutite lakṣaṇā kare ayathā prakāre*  
*nitya-satya dūre yāya, aparādhe mare*

**Перевод:**

**“One who accepts the unnecessary secondary meanings of the śruti, distances himself from eternal truth and perishes due to his offence.**

### Text 48

*sarva-veda-sammata praṇava kṛṣṇa-nāma*  
*sei nāme jīva saba pāya nitya-dhāma*

**Перевод:**

**“All the Vedas agree that praṇava (oṁ) is the Name of Kṛṣṇa. By chanting that Name, all the jīvas can attain the eternal abode.**

### Text 49

*praṇava se mahā-vākya haya kṛṣṇa-nāma*  
*tāhāte-i śrī-bhaktera satata viśrāma*

**Перевод:**

**“Praṇava, the great statement of the Vedas (mahā-vākya), is a name of Kṛṣṇa. A devotee eternally takes refuge in that.**

### Text 50

*veda bale, nāma cit-svarūpa jagate*  
*nāmera ābhāse siddha haya sarva-mate*

**Перевод:**

**“The Vedas declare to the world the spiritual nature of nāma. Even by a semblance of the Name (nāmābhāsa), all kinds of perfection are achieved.**

**Veda kevala śuddha-nāma-bhajana śikṣā dena (The Vedas Only Give Instructions on Pure Nāma-bhajana)**

### Text 51

*ei saba veda-śikṣā abhāgā nā māne*  
*nāme aparādha kare, vedera nindane*

**Перевод:**

**“Those who are unfortunate do not understand all the teachings found within the Vedas. They commit offences to the Name by criticising the Vedas.**

### Text 52

*śuddha-nāma-parāyaṇa yei mahājana*  
*vedāśraye pāya nāma-rasa prema-dhana*

**Перевод:**

**“Those mahājanas who are engaged in śuddha–nāma take shelter of the Vedas, and attain nāma–rasa and the wealth of prema.**

### Text 53

*sarva-veda bale, gāo hari-nāma-sāra*  
*pāibe paramā prīti ānanda apāra’*

**Перевод:**

**“All the Vedas state, ‘Chant that most essential hari-nāma! By chanting, you will obtain the highest type of love and unlimited bliss.’**

### Text 54

*veda punaḥ bale, yata mukta mahājana*  
*para-vyome sadā kare nāma-saṅkīrtana*

**Перевод:**

**“Again the Vedas say, \`In the spiritual sky, the liberated mahājanas constantly engage in nāma–saṅkīrtana.’**

**Tāmasa-tantra śikṣā veda viruddha (The Teachings of the Tāmasika Tantras are Opposed to the Vedas)**

### Text 55

*kali-yuge bahu-jana māyā-śakti bhaje*  
*cid-ātmā-puruṣa kṛṣṇa-nāma-rasa tyāje*

**Перевод:**

**“In Kali-yuga, many persons worship the māyā–śakti, rejecting the transcendental Personality and the rasa of kṛṣṇa–nāma.**

### Text 56

*tāmasika tantra dhari’ śruti-nindā kare*  
*madya-māṁse prīti kari’ adharmete mare*

**Перевод:**

**“The followers of tantras that are in the mode of ignorance, criticise the Vedas. Finding pleasure in wine and meat, they perish due to their adharma.**

### Text 57

*se-saba ninduka nāhi pāya kṛṣṇa-nāma*  
*kabhu nāhi pāya kṛṣṇera vṛndāvana-dhāma*

**Перевод:**

**“All such critics never accept kṛṣṇa-nāma. They never attain Kṛṣṇa’s abode of Vṛndāvana.**

**Māyā-devīra niṣkapaṭa kṛpā-i prayojana (The Necessity of Māyā Devī’s Mercy)**

### Text 58

*māyā-devī se-saba pāṣaṇḍe adhogati*  
*diyā nāmāmṛta āra nāhi dena mati*

**Перевод:**

**“Māyā Devī forces all such atheists to fall down, and she is disinclined to give them the nectar of the Holy Name.**

### Text 59

*tabe yadi sādhu-sevāya tuṣṭa hana māyā*  
*akapaṭe dena tabe kṛṣṇa-pada-chāyā*

**Перевод:**

**“But if Māyā is satisfied by their service to a sādhu, then she will unreservedly give them the shade of Kṛṣṇa’s feet.**

### Text 60

*māyā kṛṣṇa-dāsī bahirmukha-jīve daṇḍe*  
*māyā pūjile-u śubha nāhi pāya bhaṇḍe*

**Перевод:**

**“Māyā is the maidservant of Kṛṣṇa who punishes those jīvas who are averse to Him. Those hypocrites who worship Māyā never achieve auspiciousness.**

### Text 61

*kṛṣṇa-nāma kare yei, māyā-devī tā’re*  
*niṣkapaṭe kṛpā kari’ laya bhava-pāre*

**Перевод:**

**“For those who chant kṛṣṇa-nāma, Māyā Devī gives her niṣkapaṭa-kṛpā (sincere mercy) and they are able to cross beyond material nature. (10)**

**Комментарий:**

(10) In this world, the māyāvādīs chant the names of Dūrgā and Kālī, and offer worship to her. The cit-śakti is Kṛṣṇa’s original, personal potency. Māyā is the shadow of that. Her aim is to purify the jīvas who are averse to Kṛṣṇa, and gradually bring them back to Him. Māyā has two types of mercy – niṣkapaṭa-kṛpā (sincere mercy) and sakapaṭa-kṛpā (deceptive mercy). Wherever there is niṣkapaṭa-kṛpā, then she bestows her vidyā-vṛtti (the perception of real knowledge) and gives kṛṣṇa-bhakti. Wherever there is sakapaṭa-kṛpā, there she gives temporary material happiness to the jīvas and controls them. When she is very merciful in this particular way, she hurls the jīva into brahma-nirvāṇa. When this occurs, the jīva is completely finished.

### Text 62

*ataeva śruti-nindā-aparādha tyaji’*  
*aharaha nāma-saṅkīrtana-rase maji*

**Перевод:**

**“Thus, one should give up the offence of śruti–nindā (criticism of the śruti) and day after day, absorb oneself in the rasa of nāma–saṅkīrtana.**

**Tad-aparādhera pratikāra (The Remedy for this Offence)**

### Text 63

*pramāde yadyapi haya se-śruti-nindana*  
*anutāpe kari punaḥ se-śruti vandana*

**Перевод:**

**“If I foolishly engage in criticism of the śruti, I should repent and again offer respects to the śruti.**

### Text 64

*kusama-tulasī diyā sei śruti-gaṇe*  
*bhāgavata-saha sadā pūjiba yatane*

**Перевод:**

**“Offering flowers and tulasī to the śrutī, I must constantly worship them along with the Bhāgavata with great care.**

### Text 65

*bhāgavata śruti-sāra kṛṣṇa-avatāra*  
*avaśya karibe more karuṇā apāra*

**Перевод:**

**“The Bhāgavata is the essence of the śruti and is an avatāra of Kṛṣṇa. It will certainly bestow unlimited mercy upon me! (11)**

**Комментарий:**

(11) The Śrīmad Bhāgavata is the essence of all the Vedas. Those persons whose auspicious day has not dawned yet due to some delay, speak harsh words about Śrī Bhāgavata. That is their inherent nature.

### Text 66

*hari-dāsa-pada-rajaḥ bharasā yāhāra*  
*nāma-cintāmaṇi-hāra galāya tāhāra*

**Перевод:**

**“He who has confidence in the dust of Hari Dāsa’s feet, places this jewelled necklace of Holy Names around his neck.**

## Chapter 8. Nāme Arthavāda Aparādha (The Offence of Interpreting the Holy Name)

*tathārtha-vādo hari-nāmni kalpanam*

**Giving an imaginary interpretation to hari-nāma or considering the glories of the Name to be imaginary.**

### Text 1

*jaya gaura-gadādhara śrī-rādhā-mādhava*  
*jaya gaura-līlā-sthalī jāhnavī vaiṣṇava*

**Перевод:**

**Glories to Gaura-Gadādhara and Śrī Rādhā-Mādhava! Glories unto the places of Gaura’s pastimes! Glories to the Gaṅgā and the Vaiṣṇavas!**

### Text 2

*hari-nāme artha-vāda kalpanā cintana*  
*pañcamāparādha prabho śrī-śacīnandana*

**Nāma mahimā (The Glories of Nāma)**

### Text 3

*smṛti kahe helāya śraddhāya nāma laya*  
*kṛṣṇa tāre kṛpā kari hayena sadaya*

**Перевод:**

**“The smṛti says that Kṛṣṇa is merciful to anyone who chants nāma with faith, or even with neglect.**

### Text 4

*nāmera sadṛśa jñāna nāhika nirmala*  
*nāmera sadṛśa vrata nāhika prabala*

**Перевод:**

**“There is no knowledge as pure as the Holy Name. There is no vrata as powerful as the Holy Name.**

### Text 5

*nāmera sadṛśa dhyāna nāhi e jagate*  
*nāmera sadṛśa phala nāhi kona mate*

**Перевод:**

**“There is no meditation in this world like that of the Holy Name. There is no philosophy that gives a result like that of the Holy Name.**

### Text 6

*nāmera sadṛśa tyāga kona rūpe naya*  
*nāmera sadṛśa sama kabhu nāhi haya*

**Перевод:**

**“There is no form of renunciation like that of the Holy Name. There is nothing equal to the Holy Name.**

### Text 7

*nāmera sadṛśa puṇya nāhi e saṁsāre*  
*nāmera sadṛśa gati nā dekhi vicāre*

**Перевод:**

**“There is no piety in this world like that of the Holy Name. I do not consider that there is any destination like that of the Holy Name.**

### Text 8

*nāma-i parama mukti nāma ucca-gati*  
*nāma-i parama śānti nāma ucca-sthiti*

**Перевод:**

**“The Name is the highest type of liberation. The Name is the highest type of destination. The Name is the highest type of peace. The Name is the highest situation.**

### Text 9

*nāma-i parama bhakti nāma śuddhā mati*  
*nāma-i parama prīti nāma parā smṛti*

**Перевод:**

**“The Name is the highest type of bhakti. The Name is the highest type of love. The Name is the highest type of remembrance.**

### Text 10

*nāma-i kāraṇa tattva nāma sarva-prabhu*  
*parama ārādhya nāma guru-rūpe vibhu*

**Перевод:**

**“The Name is the ultimate cause. The Name is the Lord of all. The Name is the most worshippable, and the Name appears as the greatest guru.**

**Kṛṣṇa-nāmera sarvottamatā (Kṛṣṇa’s Name is the Greatest of All)**

**Перевод:**

### Text 11

*sahasra viṣṇu nāmera tulya haya eka rāma nāma*  
*tina rāma nāma tulya eka kṛṣṇa nāma*

**Перевод:**

**“A thousand Names of Viṣṇu equals one Name of Rāma, and three Names of Rāma equals one Name of Kṛṣṇa.**

**Nāmera arthavāda naraka gamana avaśya ghaṭe (Those Who Use Semantics with the Name Certainly Enter a Hellish Condition)**

### Text 12

*śruti gaṇa nāmera māhātmya sadā gāya*  
*nāmera cit-tattva bali jagate jānāya*

**Перевод:**

**“The verses of the śruti eternally sing the glories of the Name. They explain to the world about the transcendental nature of the Name.**

### Text 13

*śruti smṛti pradarśita nāmera ye phala*  
*tāhe arthavāda kare pāṣaṇḍa prabala*

**Перевод:**

**“Those who think the descriptions in the śruti and smṛti concerning the results obtained from the Name are exaggeration are extreme offenders.**

### Text 14

*hari-nāme arthavāda ye adhama kare*  
*se pāpiṣṭha narakete paci’ paci’ mare*

**Перевод:**

**“Those who consider the glories of hari–nāma to be exaggerated are contemptible. Such wicked persons constantly burn and suffer in a hellish condition.**

### Text 15

*ye bale nāmera phala-śruti satya naya*  
*nāme ruci dite mātra tata phala kaya*

**Перевод:**

**“They say, ‘Whatever the śruti says about the results of chanting the Name is not true! It is only there to give nāma–ruci (a taste for the Name) so that people get a result.**

### Text 16

*śāstrera tātparya āra jīva hitāhita*  
*se adhama nāhi jāne bujhe viparīta*

**Перевод:**

**“Such fallen persons have no understanding of the meaning of the śāstra, nor do they know what is favourable or unfavourable. Everything is the opposite for them. (2)**

**Комментарий:**

(2) Someone who has no bhakti-sukṛti never has faith in bhakti-tattva. Within the process of bhakti, chanting the Name is the best thus without sukṛti, a taste for the Name can never appear. Those without sukṛti do not believe in the descriptions of the Name’s infinite results. One who is attached to only one part of the śāstra cannot understand the true meaning of the śāstra.

**Nāmera phala satya. Tāhāte arthavādera prayojana nāi (The Results of the Name are Real. There is no Necessity to Exaggerate Them)**

### Text 17

*karma-kāṇḍa āche ta kaitava svārtha-jñāna*  
*bhakti-tattve nāme tāhā nahe vidyamāna*

**Перевод:**

**“Karma-kāṇḍa contains deceitful knowledge to satisfy one’s own desires. But in bhakti–tattva, and in chanting the Holy Name, these things do not exist. (3)**

**Комментарий:**

(3) The word kaitava means dhūrtatā (deceptive).

### Text 18

*karma-kāṇḍa phala-śruti rocanārtha jāni*  
*bhakti-tattve phala-śruti nitya satya māni*

**Перевод:**

**“I know that the results described in karma–kāṇḍa are meant to charm people. I consider the results found in bhakti–tattva to be eternal and real.**

### Text 19

*nāma-tattve śāṭhya nāhi pāya kabhu sthāna*  
*nijera nāhika svārtha nāma kari dāna*

**Перевод:**

**“There is no such deceit in nāma–tattva. The Name does not fulfil selfish desires. Karma-phalera arthavāda aparityājya (One Should Not Abandon the Idea that the Results of Karma are Exaggerated)**

### Text 20

*nāma dāna śraddāvāne yei jana kare*  
*kṛṣṇa-dāsya kare sei svārtha parihāre*

**Перевод:**

**“One who has faith in the benefits given by nāma engages in service to Kṛṣṇa. He rejects selfish desires.**

### Text 21

*karma karāile yājakera artha lābha*  
*ataeva tāhe kaitavera ta prabhāva*

**Перевод:**

**“Yājakas (Vedic priests) achieve benefits from performing karma, thus they are influenced by deceit.**

### Text 22

*veda-smṛti nāma-phala ananta vākhāne*  
*svārtha-buddhi śūnya se ye tāhā nāhi māne*

**Перевод:**

**“The Vedas and smṛti describe unlimited results from the Holy Name. This does not mean that the Name is devoid of personal benefits. (4)**

**Комментарий:**

(4) Svārtha–buddhi (personal benefits) refers to that mentality by which the jīvas endeavour to advance spiritually.

### Text 23

*karma saba śubhāśubha jaḍera āśraye*  
*jaḍa-maya-phala yāce yajamāna caye*

**Перевод:**

**“All karma produces good and bad results wherein one takes refuge in matter. Thus, a yajamāna (a performer of karmika rituals) requests results that are completely material.**

### Text 24

*karma-phala dūre pheli’ yebā kare karma*  
*hṛdaya viśuddha tāra haya ei marma*

**Перевод:**

**“When someone casts aside the results of karma, but continues to engage in it, this means that their heart is becoming purified. \***

**Перевод:**

**\* Translator’s Note: In this verse, karma refers to one’s varṇāśrama duties. When one engages in such activities out of social obligation but is detached from the results, one is on the path of purification.**

### Text 25

*viśuddha-hṛdaye ātma-rati sunirmala*  
*udaya haiyā haya kramaśaḥ prabala*

**Перевод:**

**“Such a person with a pure heart experiences transcendental pleasure in the ātmā, and gradually that becomes stronger. (5)**

**Комментарий:**

(5) Ātma-rati means taking pleasure in ātmā–tattva (the reality of the self), and thus having aversion to anything unconnected with the ātmā.”

**Nāma cinmaya, tāhāte arthavāda haite pāre nā (The Name is Spiritual, it Does Not Need Any Interpretation)**

### Text 26

*nāma sei ātma-rati nije upasthita*  
*sādhana kālete sādhya vastura vihita*

**Перевод:**

**“That ātma–rati appears from the Name, then during the course of one’s sādhana, one attains the object of attainment.**

### Text 27

*karmera carama phala nāma-rasa haya*  
*sādhu-rūpe anuṣṭhita karmete niścaya*

**Перевод:**

**“The ultimate result of one’s activities should be nāma–rasa. The activities observed by a sādhu should certainly culminate in this.**

### Text 28

*ataeva caudda-loka bhramiyā brāhmaṇa*  
*yei phala nāhi pāna nāma tāhā hana*

**Перевод:**

**“Therefore, although he may roam the fourteen worlds, even a brāhmaṇa will not attain this result of tasting the Holy Name.**

### Text 29

*nāma-phala sarvopari avaśya haibe*  
*karmī jñānī hiṁsā kari nāme ki karibe*

**Перевод:**

**“Indeed, the results from nāma are above all others. How can karmīs or jñānīs harm the Name?**

**Nāmābhāse sarva-karma o brahma-jñānera phala haiyā thāke (By Nāmābhāsa one Attains All the Results from Karma and Brahma-Jñāna)**

### Text 30

*sarva-karma-phala nāmābhāse labdha haya*  
*sarva-jñāna-phala nāmābhāsete milaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Nāmābhāsa awards all the results of karma, and through nāmābhāsa one attains all the results of jñāna.**

### Text 31

*ābhāse milila yadi eta ucca phala*  
*nāma vastu tato’dhika pradāne prabala*

**Перевод:**

**“If one comes in connection with even a semblance of the Name (ābhāsa), this produces a higher result. The Name has the power to award so much more (than karma and jñāna). (6)**

**Комментарий:**

(6) The results from nāmābhāsa are greater than those from karma and jñāna. When the results of nāmābhāsa are such, then the results achieved from the direct (pure) Name must be greater. Is there any doubt in this?

### Text 32

*ataeva śāstre yata nāma phala gāya*  
*śuddha-nāmāśrita jana niścaya tā pāya*

**Перевод:**

**“Therefore, the śāstra sings about the results of the Holy Name. Those persons who take shelter of śuddha-nāma certainly obtain them.**

**Nāma-phale yāhāra sandeha tāhāra maṅgala nāi (Those Who Doubt the Results of Nāma Attain No Auspiciousness)**

### Text 33

*ihāte sandeha yāra se adhama jana*  
*nāma aparādhe tāra avaśya patana*

**Перевод:**

**“Those who doubt this are the most despicable of men. They certainly fall down due to this nāmāparādha.**

### Text 34

*vede rāmāyaṇe āra bhārate purāṇe*  
*ādi antye madhye hari-nāmere vākhāne*

**Перевод:**

**“In the beginning, middle and end of the Vedas, Rāmāyaṇa, Mahābhārata and the Purāṇas, only hari–nāma is spoken of.**

### Text 35

*nāma-phala śruti-vākya anādi niścala*  
*tāhe arthavāda kalpanāra kibā phala*

**Перевод:**

**“The results of nāma are found in the words of the śruti which have no beginning and are unchangable. How can such results be exaggerated or imaginary?**

**Karma-jñānera śakti apekṣā ananta-guṇa-śakti nāme āche (The Potency of the Name has Unlimited Qualities That Are Greater Than the Potencies of Karma and Jñāna)**

### Text 36

*nāma nāmī eka nāme diyā sarva-śakti*  
*sarvopari kariyācha tava nāma-bhakti*

**Перевод:**

**“The Name and the Named are one, and You have invested all Your potencies in the Name. Above all other methods, You have stressed that nāma–bhakti is the highest of all.**

### Text 37

*tumita svatantra-tattva sarva-śaktimān*  
*tomāra icchāya yata vidhira vidhāna*

**Перевод:**

**“You are the independent Reality and the Possessor of all potencies. By Your desire, laws are put in place.**

### Text 38

*karmake karecha jaḍa āra brahma jñāne*  
*diyācha nirvāṇa-śakti svatantra vidhāne*

**Перевод:**

**“You have situated matter within karma and nirvāṇa-śakti (the power of impersonal liberation) within brahma-jñāna, because you are independent.**

### Text 39

*icchāmaya tumi prabhu svīya nāmākṣare*  
*arpiyācha saba śakti āra ke ki kare*

**Перевод:**

**“O Lord, being free to act according to Your desires, You have placed all Your potencies in the syllables of the Holy Name. Having invested all Your powers there, what more can be given? (7)**

**Комментарий:**

(7) “You are the autonomous Supreme Person, possessed of independent desires. You have invested all Your potencies in Your Name – what can be the objection from anyone?

### Text 40

*ataeva tava nāma sarva-śaktimān*  
*nāme arthavāda nāhi karibe vidvān*

**Перевод:**

**“Therefore Your Name possesses all potencies. A wise person will never impose mundane interpretations upon the Name.**

**Tad-aparādhera pratikāra (The Remedy for this Aparādha)**

### Text 41

*nāme arthavāda aparādha ghaṭe yadi*  
*dante tṛṇa dhari yāi vaiṣṇava-saṁsadi*

**Перевод:**

**“If one makes the offence of giving interpretations to the Name, one should place a blade of grass between his teeth and approach an assembly of Vaiṣṇavas. (8)**

**Комментарий:**

(8) Vaiṣṇava saṁsadi means an assembly of Vaiṣṇavas that discusses kṛṣṇa-kathā.

### Text 42

*aparādha jānāiyā vaiṣṇava-caraṇe*  
*kṣamā māgi kākuti kariyā ṛju-mane*

**Перевод:**

**“One should fall at the Vaiṣṇavas’ feet and inform them of his offence. Being straightforward with them, one should shed tears and beg forgiveness.**

### Text 43

*nāmera mahimā jñātā bhāgavata jana*  
*kṣamā kari kṛpā kari dibe āliṅgana*

**Перевод:**

**“The devotees, who are well aware of the glories of the Name, will show forgiveness and compassion, and embrace him.**

### Text 44

*nāme arthavāda āra kalpana-manana*  
*kabhu nāhi habe citte māyā viḍambana*

**Перевод:**

**“One should never give a mundane interpretation to nāma, and think that the glories of the Name are imaginary. This is the deception of Māyā. (9)**

**Комментарий:**

(9) Those who do not believe in nāma attempt to give interpretations to it, yet this is only the deception of Māyā.

### Text 45

*arthavāda-kārī saha haile sambhāṣaṇa*  
*sa-cele jāhnavī-jale kariba majjana*

**Перевод:**

**“If one converses with someone who gives mundane interpretations to the Name, one should jump into the waters of the Gaṅgā with all his clothes in order to become purified. (10)**

**Комментарий:**

(10) One should never look at the faces of all those persons who give mundane interpretations to nāma. If somehow during the course of events, one converses with such a person, one should immediately bathe in the Gaṅgā. When the Gaṅgā is not close by, then one should bathe in some other pure water wearing one’s clothes. Otherwise one should perform mānasa–snāna (bathe mentally) and in this way one will become purified.

### Text 46

*kṛṣṇa priyā vaṁśī kṛpā bharasā yāhāra*  
*hari-nāma cintāmaṇi tāra alaṅkāra*

**Перевод:**

**One who has faith in the mercy of Śrī Vaṁśīvadana Ṭhākura (the avatāra of Kṛṣṇa’s beloved flute) decorates himself with this Hari-nāma Cintāmaṇi.**

## Chapter 9. Nāma-bale pāpa-buddhi (The Mentality of Performing Pāpa on the Strength of the Holy Name)

*nāmno balād yasya hi pāpa-buddhir*  
*na vidyate tasya yamair hi śuddhiḥ*

**The desire to perform pāpa on the strength of the Name, cannot be purified by any type of regulation.**

### Text 1

*gaura-gadādhara jaya jāhnavā-jīvana*  
*jaya jaya sītādvaita jaya bhakta-gaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**Glories to Gaura-Gadādhara and the Life of Jāhnavā (Nityānanda). Glories to Sītā and Advaita and the devotees.**

**Nāma-grahaṇe samasta anartha dūra haya (Taking the Name Removes all Anarthas)**

### Text 2

*hari-dāsa bale nāma śuddha-sattva-maya*  
*bhāgyavān jīva kare nāmera āśraya*

**Перевод:**

**Hari Dāsa said, “The Name is comprised of pure goodness (śuddha-sattva). A fortunate jīva will take shelter of the Name.**

### Text 3

*ati śīghra tāhāra anartha dūre yāya*  
*hṛdaya-daurbalya āra sthāna nāhi pāya*

**Перевод:**

**“Very quickly his anarthas will go far away. His weakness of heart will not be able to remain.**

### Text 4

*nāme dṛḍha haile nāhi haya pāpe mati*  
*pūrva pāpa dagdha haya citta śuddha ati*

**Перевод:**

**“With firm attachment to the Name, he will not be inclined to perform pāpa. His previous pāpā will be burned and his consciousness will become extremely pure.**

### Text 5

*pāpa āra pāpa-bīja pāpera vāsanā*  
*avidyā tāhāra mūla e tina yantranā*

**Перевод:**

**“In relation to pāpa there is pāpa-bīja (the seed of pāpa), pāpa-vāsanā (the tendency to perform pāpa), and avidyā (ignorance), which is at the root of these three and causes misery. (1)**

**Комментарий:**

(1) Pāpa–bīja or pāpa–vāsanā stems from avidyā , and from pāpa–vāsanā comes pāpa. These three things bring suffering to the bound jīvas.

### Text 6

*sarva-jīve dayā āsi haibe udaya*  
*jīvera maṅgala ceṣṭā satata karaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Mercy to all jīvas manifests in his heart and he always makes endeavours for their auspiciousness.**

### Text 7

*jīvera santāpa kabhu sahite nā pāre*  
*yāhe paratāpa yāya tāra ceṣṭā kare*

**Перевод:**

**“He cannot tolerate the suffering of the jīvas. He tries to relieve them of their miseries. (2)**

**Комментарий:**

(2) Paratāpa means miseries created by other jīvas.

### Text 8

*viṣaya-pipāsā ati tuccha mane haya*  
*indriya-lālasā tāra citte nāhi raya*

**Перевод:**

**“The thirst for material pleasure becomes very weak. The desires of the senses cannot remain in his heart.**

### Text 9

*kanaka-kāminī ceṣṭā prati ghṛṇā kare*  
*yathā dharma lābhe tuṣṭa thāki prāṇa-dhare*

**Перевод:**

**“He becomes disgusted with endeavours to achieve wealth and women. He maintains his life by being satisfied by whatever he attains through dharma.**

### Text 10

*bhakti anukūla saba karaye svīkāra*  
*bhakti pratikūla nāhi kare aṅgīkāra*

**Перевод:**

**“He accepts all activities favourable for bhakti. He does not accept those activities unfavorable for bhakti.**

### Text 11

*kṛṣṇa rakṣā-kartā ekamātra bali jāne*  
*jīvane pālana-kartā kṛṣṇa ihā māne*

**Перевод:**

**“He thinks, ‘I know Kṛṣṇa as my only protector. Kṛṣṇa is the protector of my life.’**

### Text 12

*ahaṁ mama buddhy-āsakti nā rākhe hṛdaye*  
*dīna-bhāve nāma laya sakala samaye*

**Перевод:**

**“In his heart he does not retain mental attachments of \`I’ and \`mine’. With a humble mood, he chants the Name at all times (3)**

**Комментарий:**

(3) In this material body, “I” and “mine” are mental attachments.

### Text 13

*svabhāvataḥ yāra ei rūpa nāmāśraya*  
*pāpe mati pāpācāra tāhāra ki haya*

**Перевод:**

**“Naturally, whoever takes shelter of the Name in this way can neither think wicked thoughts nor perform pāpa.**

**Pūrva-pāpa o pāpa-gandha śīghra dūra haya (Previous Pāpa and the Trace of Pāpa Flee Very Quickly)**

### Text 14

*pūrva dṛṣṭa-bhāva tāra krame haya kṣīṇa*  
*pavitra svabhāva śīghra haibe pravīṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“His previous propensities gradually become diminished. His nature quickly becomes purified and he becomes intelligent.**

### Text 15

*ei sandhi-kāle pūrva-pāpera sambandha*  
*thākite-o pāre kichu dina pāpa-gandha*

**Перевод:**

**“During this time there may still be some connection to one’s previous pāpa, and for a while a trace of these misdeeds remain. (4)**

**Комментарий:**

(4) One becomes attracted to the Name. There is a previous state and a present state, and the condition between these two states is known as the sandhi-kāla (the period of time in-between). During that time, one is not attracted to perform any new pāpa. During the course of one’s practice, the trace of pāpa is gradually eliminated.

### Text 16

*nāmera saṁsarge yata sumati udaya*  
*haye sei pāpa-gandha śīghra kare kṣaya*

**Перевод:**

**“As a proper connection with the Name appears, any trace of one’s pāpa is rapidly destroyed.**

### Text 17

*pratijñā karecha nātha arjuna nikaṭe*  
*mora bhakta kabhu nāhi paḍibe saṅkaṭe*

**Перевод:**

**“The Lord promised Arjuna: ‘My devotee will never fall into danger.’**

### Text 18

*saṅkaṭa samaye āmi haiba sahāya*  
*ataeva pāpa yāya tomāra kṛpāya*

**Перевод:**

**“At the time of danger, I will help. Thus, by My mercy, the results of your misdeeds will flee.’**

### Text 19

*jñāna-mārgī kaṣṭe pāpa kariyā damana*  
*tavāśraya chāḍi śīghra hayata patana*

**Перевод:**

**“Those on the path of jñāna struggle to subdue their pāpa, and because they reject Your shelter, they quickly fall down.**

### Text 20

*tava padāśraya yāra sei mahājana*  
*vighna nā pāibe kabhu siddhānta vacana*

**Перевод:**

**“Those great personalities who take shelter of You will never be impeded. That is the conclusive statement (of the śāstra).**

**Pramāde pāpa upasthita haile tāhāra prāyaścittera prayojana nāi (Atonement is not Required for One Who Engages in Pāpa Out of Foolishness)**

### Text 21

*yadi kabhu pramāde ghaṭaya kona pāpa*  
*bhakta tabu nāhi sahe prāyaścitta tāpa*

**Перевод:**

**“If out of foolishness, a devotee engages in some pāpa, he does not need to perform the austerities of prāyaṣcitta (atonement). (5)**

**Комментарий:**

(5) If a devotee commits any pāpa due to delusion, then there is no need for atonement.

### Text 22

*se pāpa kṣaṇika nāhi pāya avasthiti*  
*nāma-rase bhese yāya nā deya durgati*

**Перевод:**

**“Such momentary pāpa cannot remain. Immersed in nāma–rasa, he will not meet any misfortune.**

**Nāmāśrayī nūtana pāpa vicāra kariya karile nāma-bale pāpācaraṇa haya (Considering New Pāpa Committed by One who has Taken Shelter of the Name, and Performing them on the Strength of the Name)**

### Text 23

*kintu yadi kona jana nāme kari bala*  
*ācare nūtana pāpa se jana cañcala*

**Перевод:**

**“But if someone engages in new pāpa on the strength of chanting, such a person is unsteady.**

### Text 24

*se kevala kapaṭatā kariyā āśraya*  
*nāma-aparādha pāya śoka-mṛti-bhaya*

**Перевод:**

**“His taking shelter (of the Name) is only a pretence and due to nāma–aparādha, he only attains misery, death and fear.**

**Pramāda o vicārita karmera bheda (The Difference Between Acts Performed Out of Delusion and Acts performed Deliberately)**

### Text 25

*pramāda ghaṭanā āra vicārita karme*  
*sampūrṇa prabheda āche bhakti-śāstra marme*

**Перевод:**

**“Actions performed out of foolishness and those done deliberately are completely different. This is revealed in the bhakti-śāstra. (6)**

**Комментарий:**

(6) There are two kinds of pāpa, in other words, those that are sudden and caused by foolishness, and vice which is done deliberately. For example, “I will engage in this one misdeed” – before it is actually done, one has thought about it with a steady mind. There are many differences between these two types.

**Nāmāśrayīra pāpa karā dūre thākuka, pāpe mati haile-i nāmāparādha haya (The Pāpa of One who Takes Shelter of Nāma Remains Far Away, One who is Absorbed in Thinking of Pāpa Makes an Offence to the Name)**

### Text 26

*saṁsārī mānava yebā ācaraye pāpa*  
*prāyaścitta āche tāra āra anutāpa*

**Перевод:**

**“While in this material world, a human who engages in pāpa should repent and perform atonement.**

### Text 27

*kintu nāma-bale yadi pāpe kare mati*  
*prāyaścitta nāhi tāra baḍa-i durgati*

**Перевод:**

**“But if someone engages in pāpa on the strength of chanting the Name, he cannot perform any atonement and becomes most unfortunate.**

### Text 28

*bahu yama yātanādi pāile-o tāra*  
*sei aparādha haite nā haya uddhāra*

**Перевод:**

**“Even after experiencing many hellish punishments by Yama, he cannot be delivered from this offence.**

### Text 29

*pāpa mati-mātre haya e-rūpa yantranā*  
*pāpācāre yata doṣe tāra ki gaṇanā*

**Перевод:**

**“Even if he thinks of engaging in pāpa he will be punished in that way, so who can calculate his difficulties if he actually performs pāpa?**

**Pravañcaka śaṭhera nāma-bharasāya pāpa-kriyā markaṭa-vairāgya mātra (A Deceiver who Makes a False Show of Faith in the Name, But Engages in Pāpa is Simply a Monkey-Renunciant)**

### Text 30

*śāstre śuniyāche nāma yata pāpa hare*  
*koṭi janme mahā-pāpī karite nā pāre*

**Перевод:**

**“It is heard in the śāstra that the Holy Name eliminates more pāpa than the greatest reprobate can commit in millions of births.**

### Text 31

*pañca-vidha pāpa mahā-pātaka avadhi*  
*nāmābhāse yāya śāstra gāya niravadhi*

**Перевод:**

**“The five types of pāpa, up to the greatest of pāpa (mahā–pātaka), flee from nāmābhāsa. The śāstra says this continuously.**

### Text 32

*sei ta bharasā kari pravañcaka jana*  
*śaṭhatā kariyā nāma karaye grahaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“A deceiver who believes (in these statements) may deceptively take up the Holy Name.**

### Text 33

*kaṣṭera saṁsāra chāḍi vairāgīra veśe*  
*kanaka kāminī āśe phire deśe deśe*

**Перевод:**

**“Abandoning the taxing life of this world, he may wear the garments of a renunciant. Desiring wealth and women, he wanders from place to place.**

### Text 34

*tumi ta balecha prabhu markaṭa-vairāgī*  
*kāminī sambhāṣi phire dharma gṛha tyāgī*

**Перевод:**

**“O Lord, You have referred to such people as ‘markaṭa-vairāgīs’ (monkey-renunciants). He repeatedly talks with women while pretending to have renounced his home. (7)**

**Комментарий:**

(7) In relation to Choṭa Hari Dāsa, the Lord’s condemnation of “markaṭa-vairāgīs” is described in detail in the Caritāmṛta. A renunciant who chats with women is a markaṭa-vairāgī.

**Niṣkapaṭa nāmāśraya nā karile ei aparādha anirvārya (Those Who Cannot Take Shelter of the Name with Duplicity Will Inevitably Commit this Offence)**

### Text 35

*vairāgyera chale keha gṛhe kāṭe kāla*  
*sambhāṣya nā haya saba viśvera jañjāla*

**Перевод:**

**“Those persons who roam around as renunciants, but simultaneously live in the fashion of a householder are not fit to be spoken to. They ruin the entire world.**

### Text 36

*gṛhe thāku vane yāu tāte nāhi doṣa*  
*niṣpāpe karuk nāma pāiyā santoṣa*

**Перевод:**

**“A person may remain at home, or in the forest—there is no fault in that. He should live a life devoid of any pāpa, chant the Name, and be satisfied. (8)**

**Комментарий:**

(8) A devotee who takes shelter of the Holy Name may remain at home, or in the forest. There is no dispute. If one’s home is favourable for the cultivation of taking shelter of the Name, then it is better than the bhikṣu-āśrama (sannyāsa), but if it is unfavourable for the cultivation of taking shelter of the Name, a Vaiṣṇava should leave home.

### Text 37

*nāma bale pāpa mati mahā-aparādha*  
*tāhāte majile haya bhakti-tattve bādha*

**Перевод:**

**“It is a great offence to commit pāpa on the strength of chanting the Holy Name. Being absorbed in that, such persons are prohibited from entering bhakti–tattva.**

**Nāmābhāsi-vyakti-gaṇa ei kapaṭa lokera saṅge aparādhī hana (Those Persons who Chant Nāmbhāsa that Associate with Such Deceivers will Make this Offence)**

### Text 38

*nāmābhāsī janera ku-saṅga yadi haya*  
*tabe ei aparādha ghaṭibe niścaya*

**Перевод:**

**“If those who chant nāmābhāsa accept their impious association, then they will certainly commit this offence.**

### Text 39

*śuddha nāmodaya yāra hṛdaye haibe*  
*ei nāma aparādha tāra nā ghaṭibe*

**Перевод:**

**“When śuddha-nāma rises in the heart, then they will not make this offence.**

**Śuddha-nāmāśrita vyaktira daśa-vidha aparādha sparśa kare nā (The Ten Kinds of Offences Cannot Touch One who Takes Shelter of Śuddha-Nāma)**

### Text 40

*śuddha-nāmāśrita jane aparādha daśa*  
*kona-rūpe kona-kāle nā kare paraśa*

**Перевод:**

**“For one who takes shelter of śuddha-nāma, the ten offences do not touch them in any way or at any time.**

### Text 41

*nāmāśrita jane nāma sadā rakṣā kare*  
*aparādha kabhu tāra nā haite pāre*

**Перевод:**

**“The Holy Name always protects those who take shelter of Him. Such persons never make offences.**

### Text 42

*yata dina śuddha-nāma nā haya udaya*  
*tata dina aparādha ākramaṇe bhaya*

**Перевод:**

**“During that time while śuddha-nāma has not yet arisen, one may still fear an attack from offences.**

### Text 43

*ataeva nāmābhāsī yadi bhāla cāya*  
*nāma bale pāpa-buddhi haite palāya*

**Перевод:**

**“Therefore, if one is chanting nāmābhāsa and desires to improve, one should flee from the mentality of engaging in pāpa on the strength of chanting the Name.**

**Kata dina sāvadhāne aparādha parityāga karā cāi? (How Long Does it Take to Abandon this Dangerous Offence?)**

### Text 44

*śuddha-nāmāśrita-jana-saṅga-bala dhari*  
*aparādha satarkatā sarvadā ācari*

**Перевод:**

**“Taking strength from associating with those who have take shelter of śuddha-nāma, one should always be cautious to avoid offenses. (9)**

**Комментарий:**

(9) ‘Saṅga-bala’ means strength from associating with pure Vaiṣṇavas.

### Text 45

*śuddha-nāma yāra mukhe tāra dṛḍha mana*  
*kṛṣṇa haite vicalita nahe ekakṣaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“When śuddha-nāma appears in one’s mouth, the mind becomes resolute. It never deviates from Kṛṣṇa, even for a moment.**

### Text 46

*ataeva nāme bala yata-dina naya*  
*tata dina aparādhe karibeka bhaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Thus, on those days when one does not feel strength from the Holy Name, one should fear these offences.**

### Text 47

*viśeṣa yatane pāpa-buddhi dūra kari*  
*aharniśi mukhe balibeka hari hari*

**Перевод:**

**“Especially one should endeavour to keep the offence of harbouring thoughts of engaging in pāpa at a distance. Day and night one’s mouth should chant, ‘Hari! Hari!’**

### Text 48

*śrī-guru-kṛpāya habe susambandha-jñāna*  
*kṛṣṇa-bhakti kṛṣṇa-nāma tāhāte vidhāna*

**Перевод:**

**“By the mercy of Śrī Guru, one will attain true sambandha-jñāna, kṛṣṇa-bhakti, and kṛṣṇa-nāma.**

**Ei aparādha haile tāhāra pratikāra (The Remedy for this Offence)**

### Text 49

*yadyapi pramāde nāma-bale pāpa-buddhi*  
*śuddha vaiṣṇavera saṅge kari tāra śuddhi*

**Перевод:**

**“If one foolishly harbours the mentality of engaging in pāpa on the strength of chanting the Name, one should purify himself through association of pure Vaiṣṇavas.**

### Text 50

*pāpa-spṛhā vāṭapāra pathe āsi’ dhare*  
*viśuddha vaiṣṇava-gaṇa patha rakṣā kare*

**Перевод:**

**“Desires to engage in pāpa are like robbers lying in wait on the road. The pure Vaiṣṇavas protect the road. (10)**

**Комментарий:**

(10) “Vāṭapāra” are those who steal on the road.

### Text 51

*uccaiḥ-svare ḍāki rakṣakera nāma dhari*  
*palāibe bāṭapāḍa āsibe praharī*

**Перевод:**

**“One loudly calls out to these guards by chanting the Name, then the guards come, and the robbers flee.**

### Text 52

*ādare balibe bhāi nāhi kara bhaya*  
*āmi ta rakṣaka tava śuna mahāśaya*

**Перевод:**

**“O brothers! Chant with love and do not fear. Listen, O respected gentlemen, the Lord has said, ‘I am your protector.’**

### Text 53

*kevala vaiṣṇava-pada-dāsya-vrata yāra*  
*hari-nāma-cintāmaṇi pāya sei chāra*

**Перевод:**

**One who vows to exclusively serve the feet of the Vaiṣṇavas, thinking himself as useless as ashes, sings this Hari-nāma Cintāmaṇi.**

## Chapter 10. Śraddhā-hīna Jane Nāmopadeśa (Giving Instructions on the Name to those Without Śraddhā)

*aśraddadhāne vimukhe ‘py aśṛṇvati*  
*yaś copadeśaḥ śiva-nāmāparādhaḥ*

**Instructing those who lack śraddhā, those who are averse, or those who do not have interest to hear, are offences to the auspicious Name.**

### Text 1

*gaura-gadādhara jaya jāhnavā-jīvana*  
*jaya jaya sītādvaita jaya bhakta-gaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**Glories to Gaura-Gadādhara and the Life of Jāhnavā (Nityānanda). Glories to Sītā and Advaita, and the devotees.**

### Text 2

*kara-yuḍi hari-dāsa balena vacana*  
*āra nāma aparādha karaha śravaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**Folding his hands, Hari Dāsa said, “Kindly hear about another offence.**

**Nāme dṛḍha viśvāsake śraddhā bali, tāhā haile-i nāme adhikāra haya (Resolute Trust and Śraddhā in the Name Makes One Qualified)**

### Text 3

*yāhāra hṛdaye śraddhā nā haila udaya*  
*nāma nāhi śune bahir-mukha durāśaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Those who are averse (to the Holy Name), in whose hearts no śraddhā arises, cannot hear the Name.**

### Text 4

*nāhi janme se janāra nāme adhikāra*  
*śraddhā-mātra adhikāra ei tattva-sāra*

**Перевод:**

**“A qualification for the Name does not appear in such a person. Śraddhā is the only qualification for hearing this essential truth.**

### Text 5

*sajjāti sat-kula jñāna bala vidyā dhana*  
*nāme adhikāra dite nā haya kāraṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“High birth, pious family, knowledge, strength, learning and wealth do not give one the eligibility for the Name.**

### Text 6

*nāmera māhātmya yei sudṛḍha viśvāsa*  
*śāstra-mate śraddhā sei sarvatra prakāśa*

**Перевод:**

**“One must have resolute trust in the glories of the Holy Name. That is the opinion of the śāstra which is explained everywhere. (1)**

**Комментарий:**

(1) Kṛṣṇa-nāma is the greatest treasure for the jīva. Someone who takes shelter of kṛṣṇa-nāma performs all auspicious activities, and in this way has trust and śraddhā (in the Name). Those who do not have this kind of śraddhā are not qualified to receive hari-nāma.

**Śraddhā-hīna-janake nāma dile nāmāparādhī haya (One Becomes an Offender to the Name by Giving the Name to a Person who Lacks Śraddhā)**

### Text 7

*śraddhā nāhi janme yāra hari-nāma tāre*  
*sādhu-jana nāhi dena vaiṣṇava ācāre*

**Перевод:**

**“A sādhu will never give hari-nāma to a person in whom śraddhā has not appeared. That is the conduct of a Vaiṣṇava.**

### Text 8

*śraddhā-hīna jana yadi hari-nāma pāya*  
*avajñā karibe mātra sarva-śāstre gāya*

**Перевод:**

**“If a person without śraddhā is given hari–nāma, he will only show disrespect to it. All the śāstra declare this.**

### Text 9

*śūkarake dile ratna se cūrṇa karibe*  
*vānarake dile vastra chiṅḍiyā phelibe*

**Перевод:**

**“If one gives a jewel to a pig, he will tread on it. If one gives garments to a monkey, he will tear them up and throw them away.**

### Text 10

*śraddhā-hīna peye nāma aparādhe mare*  
*saṅge saṅge guruke abhakta śīghra kare*

**Перевод:**

**“That person who has no śraddhā will perish due to his offenses to Nāma, and the guru (who gave him the Name) will immediately become a non-devotee.**

**Śraddhā-hīna nāma pāite prārthanā karile tāhāke kirūpe vyavahāra karā ucita? (If Someone without Śraddhā Prays for the Name, What is the Proper Conduct?)**

### Text 11

*śraddhā virahita jana śaṭhatā kariyā*  
*hari-nāma māge vaiṣṇavera kāche giyā*

**Перевод:**

**“A deceptive person with no śraddhā may approach a Vaiṣṇava and request hari–nāma from him.**

### Text 12

*tāhāra vañcanā vākya bujhi sādhu jana*  
*hari-nāma nāhi dena tāre kadācana*

**Перевод:**

**“Understanding his deceitful words, a sādhu will never give him hari-nāma at any time.**

### Text 13

*sādhu bale ohe bhāi śāṭhya parihara*  
*pratiṣṭhāśā dūre rākhi nāme śraddhā kara*

**Перевод:**

**“The sādhu will say, ‘O brother, give up your deception! Throw your desire for prestige far away and cultivate śraddhā in the Name. (2)**

**Комментарий:**

(2) “The Name is the remover of all pāpa, so if I engage in pāpa, I have nothing to fear. I shall always chant japa of hari-nāma, everyone will call me a Vaiṣṇava and offer their daṇḍavat-praṇāma to me, and I can get a lot of work out of people. My reputation that was ruined due to my pāpa will again be recovered if I take up hari–nāma. The result of hari–nāma will be that I can be extremely happy in this world!” – this sort of intention in taking the Name is deceptive.

### Text 14

*nāme śraddhā haile nāma anāyāse pābe*  
*nāmera prabhāve e saṁsāre tare yābe*

**Перевод:**

**“He who has śraddhā in the Name can attain the Name without effort. By the power of the Name such a person goes beyond this world of birth and death.**

### Text 15

*yata dina nāhi tava nāme śraddhā bhāi*  
*nāma laite tomāra ta adhikāra nāi*

**Перевод:**

**” ‘O brother, as long as you have no śraddhā in the Holy Name, you are not qualified to accept it.**

### Text 16

*śrī-nāma-māhātmya sādhu śāstra mukhe śuna*  
*pratiṣṭhāśā chāḍi dainya karaha grahaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**” ‘Give up the desire for fame. Accept a humble demeanour and hear the glories of Śrī Nāma from the mouths of the sādhu and the śāstra.**

### Text 17

*nāme śraddhā hale tabe guru mahājana*  
*nāma arpibena bhāi nāma mahā-dhana*

**Перевод:**

**” ‘When you have śraddhā in the Name, at that time a guru-mahājana will give the Name to you, O brother, the Name is a great treasure.’**

### Text 18

*śraddhā-hīna jane artha lobhe nāma diyā*  
*narakete yāya nāmāparādha majiyā*

**Перевод:**

**“If, out of greed for wealth, someone gives the Name to a person who lacks śraddhā, they enter a hellish existence due to that nāmāparādha. (3)**

**Комментарий:**

(3) Someone may come with deceptive intentions in order to take the Name, thus they have no śraddhā, and the one who gives hari–nāma out of greed for wealth or desiring fame to someone who has no qualification, commits nāmāparādha. However, at first he may have thought the disciple had śraddhā and so he gave him the Name, but later he understood that the disciple was deceptive and has no śraddhā. Then the guru must follow the following remedy.

**Ei aparādhera pratikāra (The Remedy for this Offence)**

### Text 19

*pramāde yadyapi nāma upadeśa haya*  
*śraddhā-hīne tabe guru pāya mahā-bhaya*

**Перевод:**

**“If one foolishly gives instructions on the Name to a person with no śraddhā, he should be extremely fearful.**

### Text 20

*vaiṣṇava samāje tāhā kari vijñāpana*  
*sei duṣṭa śiṣya-tyāga kare mahājana*

**Перевод:**

**“In an assembly of Vaiṣṇavas, that mahājana should announce that he rejects his corrupt disciple.**

### Text 21

*tāhā nā karile guru aparādha krame*  
*bhakti-hīna durācāra haya māyā-bhrame*

**Перевод:**

**“If he does not do this, then the guru, by the influence of this offence, will become bereft of bhakti, and bewildered by māyā, he will engage in wicked activities.”**

### Text 22

*ataeva prabhu yāre ādeśa karile*  
*nāma-pracārite tāre ei ājñā dile*

**Перевод:**

**“Thus, O Lord, you gave the instruction to propagate the Name along with this order.”**

**E viṣaye prabhura ājñā (The Lord’s Instruction on this Point)**

### Text 23

*śraddhāvān jane kara nāma upadeśa*  
*nāma-mahimāya pūrṇa kara sarva-deśa*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord said, “You should instruct those with śraddhā about the Name. Spread the glories of the Holy Name to every place.**

### Text 24

*ucca saṅkīrtane kara śraddhāra pracāra*  
*śraddhā labhi jīva kare sad-guru vicāra*

**Перевод:**

**“Perform saṅkīrtana loudly! Preach with śraddhā! When the jīvas attain some śraddhā, they will inquire from a genuine guru.**

### Text 25

*sad-guru nikaṭe kare śrī nāma grahaṇa*  
*anāyāse pāya tabe kṛṣṇa-prema-dhana*

**Перевод:**

**“They will accept Śrī–nāma from a genuine guru, then they will easily attain the wealth of kṛṣṇa-prema.**

### Text 26

*cora veśyā śaṭha ādi pāpāsakta jane*  
*chāḍāiyā pāpa mati dibe śraddhā dhane*

**Перевод:**

**“Make those people attached to pāpa such as thieves, prostitutes and charlatans give up even the thought of engaging in pāpa. Give them the wealth of śraddhā.**

### Text 27

*suśraddha haile dibe nāma upadeśa*  
*ei rūpe nāma diyā tāra sarva-deśa*

**Перевод:**

**“When they attain real śraddhā, then teach them about the Name. In this way distribute the Holy Name everywhere.**

**Ei rūpa aparādhera phala (The Result of this Offence)**

### Text 28

*ihā nā kariyā yini dena nāma dhana*  
*sei aparādhe tāṅra narake patana*

**Перевод:**

**“If one does not follow this way of distributing the treasure of the Holy Name, then one will fall into a hellish condition due to this offence.**

### Text 29

*nāma peye śiṣya kare nāma aparādha*  
*tāhāte gurura haya bhakti-rasa bādha*

**Перевод:**
