**“Giving the Name to such a disciple is a nāmāparādha. Due to this, the guru’s access to bhakti-rasa is blocked.**

### Text 30

*ei nāma aparādhe duṅhe śiṣya guru*  
*narakete yāya ei aparādha uru*

**Перевод:**

**“Due to this nāmāparādha, the guru and disciple enter into a hellish situation. This is a great offence.”**

**Agre śraddhā-diyā nāma upadeśa dibe (Initially, One Should give Faith, Then One Can Give Instructions on the Name)**

### Text 31

*jagā mādhā prati tumi mahā-kṛpā kari*  
*nāme śraddhā diyā nāma dile gaura-hari*

**Перевод:**

**“O Gaura-hari, You were extremely merciful to Jagāi and Mādhāi. You gave them śraddhā in the Name and then You granted them the Name itself. (4)**

**Комментарий:**

(4) The ancestors of the two brothers Jagadānanda and Mādhavānanda were the respected brāhmaṇa Rāya Dynasty of Śrīrāmapura. At that time, they lived in Navadvīpa-maṇḍala. Seeing these great debauchees, everyone called them Jagāi and Mādhāi.

### Text 32

*adbhuta caritra tava sarva jaga-gaṇa*  
*śraddhāya karuka anukaraṇa caraṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“Your activities are astounding. Everyone in this world should follow in Your footsteps with śraddhā.**

### Text 33

*bhakta pāda bhaktite vinoda yāhāra*  
*hari-nāma-cintāmaṇi alaṅkāra tāra*

**Перевод:**

**Those who take pleasure in bhakti to the feet of the devotees are adorned with this Hari-nāma Cintāmaṇī.**

## Chapter 11. Anya śubha-karmera sahita nāmake tulya-jñāna (Equating the Holy Name with Other Auspicious Activities)

*dharma-vrata-tyāga-hutādi-sarva-*  
*śubha-kriyā-sāmyam api pramādaḥ*

**Equating pious activities such as vows, penances, renunciation, yajñas and all other auspicious deeds (with the Holy Name), as well as being inattentive (to the Name),**

### Text 1

*jaya jaya gauracandra nāma avatāra*  
*jaya jaya hari-nāma sarva-tattva-sāra*

**Перевод:**

**Glories, glories to Gauracandra, the avatāra of the Name. Glories, glories to hari–nāma, the essence of all truths.**

### Text 2

*hari-dāsa bale prabhu kara avadhāna*  
*anya śubha-karma nahe nāmera samāna*

**Перевод:**

**Hari Dāsa said, “O Lord, kindly be aware that other pious activities are never equal to the Name.**

**Перевод:**

**Nāmera Svarūpa (The Intrinsic Nature of the Name)**

### Text 3

*tumite cinmaya sūrya tomāra svarūpa*  
*sampūṛna cinmaya ei tattva aparūpa*

**Перевод:**

**You are the transcendental sun, for that is Your nature. You are perfectly divine and Your nature is unprecedented.**

### Text 4

*sarvatra cinmaya tava śrī-vigraha haya*  
*nāma dhāma līlā tava sampūrṇa cinmaya*

**Перевод:**

**Your form is fully spiritual in every sense, and Your name, dhāma and līlā are all fully spiritual.**

### Text 5

*tava mukhya-nāma saba tomāte abhinna*  
*jaḍīya vastura nāma-vastu haite bhinna*

**Перевод:**

**Your direct Names are all non-different from You, yet material elements are different to the Name.**

### Text 6

*bhakta-mukhe āile nāma goloka haite*  
*ātmā haite dehe vyāpi nāce jihvā-dite*

**Перевод:**

**The Name descends from Goloka and enters the mouths of the devotees. Manifesting from the ātmā, it pervades the body and dances on the tongue.**

### Text 7

*ei-jñāne nāma laile haya tava nāma*  
*nāme jaḍa-buddhi yāya tāra duḥkhagrāma*

**Перевод:**

**This is the knowledge concerning Your Holy Name. One who thinks the Name is mundane will attain many miseries. (1)**

**Комментарий:**

(1) Anyone who thinks like this—that kṛṣṇa-nāma is comprised of the dull matter of māyā, will experience a hellish existence for a long time. Seeing the face of such a person, one should immediately take bath with one’s clothes and remember Viṣṇu.

**Kṛṣṇa-pāda upeya. Adhikāra bhede upāya bahu-vidha (The Feet of Kṛṣṇa are the Goal. Those with Different Qualifications Attain Many Different Types of Results)**

### Text 8

*tomāre pāite śāstra upāya kahila*  
*adhikāra bhede tāhā nānā-vidha hail****e***

**Перевод:**

**The śāstra has said that attaining You is the goal. Due to different qualifications in people there are various ways (of achieving You). (2)**

**Комментарий:**

(2) Śāstra has designated various ways such as karma, jñāna, bhakti etc. to achieve Kṛṣṇa according to various qualifications. Those who are strongly influenced by matter should purify their consciousness by engaging their intelligence’s tendency to perform karma. Those that are strongly attached to māyā — they follow the path of advaita-jñāna (knowledge of the monistic Advaita philosophy), for all other jīvas—śuddha–bhakti is advised.

**Перевод:**

**Karma-svarūpa: anya śubha-karma jaḍamaya. Upeya vastu cinmaya (The Intrinsic Nature of Karma: All Other Pious Activities are Fully Mundane. That thing which is the Goal is Transcendental)**

### Text 9

*jaḍa-buddhi-jana jaḍa-dravya-kālāśraye*  
*tomāra sādhana kare śamanera bhaye*

**Перевод:**

**Materially-minded people that take refuge in mundane objects and situations, only perform sādhana of You to mitigate fear.**

### Text 10

*tumi ta’ abhaya-pada advitīya hari*  
*tomāra caraṇa-mātra bhavārṇave tarī*

**Перевод:**

**O Hari, You are second to none! Your feet grant fearlessness. Only by Your feet can one cross over the ocean of birth and death.**

### Text 11

*sei pada-lābhe yata upāya sṛjila*  
*jaḍa-bhāvāśraye saba jaḍīya haila*

**Перевод:**

**Materially-minded people have created many ways to attain Your feet, but they are all mundane.**

### Text 12

*iṣṭā pūrta āra yajñādika puṇya-karma*  
*snāna, homa, dāna, yoga, varṇāśrama-dharma*

**Перевод:**

**Domestic fire sacrifices (iṣṭā), public welfare activities (pūrta), long-term fire sacrifices (yajña), pious activities, bathing in sacred rivers, one-day fire sacrifices (homa), giving in charity, yoga, varṇāśrama–dharma…**

### Text 13

*tīrtha-yātrā, vrata, pitṛ-karma, dhyāna, jñāna*  
*daiva-karma, tapaḥ, prāyaścittādi vidhāna*

**Перевод:**

**…going on pilgrimage to holy places, observing vows, worship of the Pitṛs, meditation, gathering knowledge, activities connected to the Devas, performing austerities, atonement etc. are performed by them.**

### Text 14

*sakalai jaḍīya dravya kariyā āśraya*  
*upāya svarūpa sadā śubha-karma haya*

**Перевод:**

**Taking shelter of all these material things, they attempt to attain the nature of their goal by performance of pious activities.**

### Text 15

*upāya dhariyā pāya upeya carame*  
*anitya upāya chāḍe siddhi-samāgame*

**Перевод:**

**Eventually, by following these processes, he achieves the ultimate goal. Rejecting the other methods, he attains perfection.(3)**

**Комментарий:**

(3) At the time of attaining perfection in bhakti, any assistance from mundane karma flees.

### Text 16

*pūrṇānanda lābha haya sarva-siddhi-sāra*  
*jīvera upeya tāhā śuna sārāt-sāra*

**Перевод:**

**Kindly hear about the goal of the jīva, O quintessential Personality, which awards complete bliss and all perfection.**

**Śubha-karma upāya (Attaining the Highest Result Through Pious Deeds)**

### Text 17

*jaḍa-dravya kāla haya nirānandamaya*  
*kauśale jīvera tāhe krame siddhi haya*

**Перевод:**

**Mundane objects and situations are not full of bliss. They are all part of a ruse in order for the jīva to eventually attain perfection. (4)**

**Комментарий:**

(4) The bound jīva cannot exist without material affairs. All his actions and thoughts are mixed with matter. While he is in the midst of matter, the trick of karma is to make him search for śuddha-bhakti, which is beyond matter.

### Text 18

*ataeva śubha-karma sakalai upāya*  
*upeya carama-siddhi prema-rūpe bhāya*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore, all auspicious activities are a way to attain the goal. That goal exists as the highest perfection in the form of prema.**

**Tāhāte upeya prāpti vilamba-siddha (Through these Methods there is a Delay in Attaining Perfection)**

### Text 19

*sarva śubha-karme siddhi vilambe udaya*  
*upeya upāye vyavadhāna-hetu haya*

**Перевод:**

**With all these auspicious activities, achieving perfection is delayed because they will create a distance between the method and the goal. (5)**

**Комментарий:**

(5) The goal is prema, and the method (mentioned previously) is mundane activities, and there is a considerable distance between the two.

**Sādhana-kāle hari-nāma upāya kirūpe haiyācchen (At the Time of Sādhana, Why is Hari-nāma the Method?)**

### Text 20

*hari-nāma e jagate dile kṛpā kari*  
*siddhi-lābhe śiṣṭha jīva laileka bari*

**Перевод:**

**By Your mercy, you have given hari–nāma to the world. Those jīvas who are wise will accept it in order to attain perfection.**

### Text 21

*upāya haila nāma śāstrera sammata*  
*anya śubha-karma-madhye haila ganita*

**Перевод:**

**The śāstra admits that nāma is the method (of attaining perfection). Anything else is counted as being amongst auspicious activities.**

### Text 22

*sarveśvara viṣṇu yena brahmā-śivasane*  
*devatā-lakṣaṇe gaṇya haila tribhūvane*

**Nāma śuddha-sattva. Māyāvādī aparādha-krame anya śubha-karmera sahita nāmake ekai mane karena (The Name is Pure Goodness. Due to their Offences, Māyāvādīs Consider the Name to be Like Any Other Auspicious Activity)**

### Text 23

*nāmera svarūpa haya śuddha-sattvamaya*  
*jaḍa-gandha śuddha nāme kabhu nāhi raya*

**Перевод:**

**The Name is comprised of pure goodness. Not even a trace of matter comes in contact with śuddha-nāma.**

### Text 24

*jaḍī-bhūta-jīva nāme jaḍa-bhāvadāne*  
*anya śubha-karma saha eka kari māne*

**Перевод:**

**Those materially bewildered jīvas, that think of the Name from a mundane perspective, believe that it is just another auspicious activity. (6)**

**Комментарий:**

(6) Jaḍī-bhūta-jīva—the jīva, who is spiritual by nature, is bewildered by avidyā and considers himself to be material.

### Text 25

*māyāvāda haite ei nāma-aparādha*  
*yāhāra daurātmya sadā haya bhakti-vādha*

**Перевод:**

**This nāmāparādha stems from māyāvāda. Those who accept such a depraved philosophy always find impediments to bhakti.**

**Nāmera upāyatva sattveo upeyatva (The Name is Both the Means and the End)**

### Text 26

*kṛṣṇa-nāma haya prabhu pūrṇānanda-tattva*  
*upeya vā siddhi bali yāhāra mahattva*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa-nāma is the Lord Himself and the principle of complete bliss. Those who understand its glories attain the highest goal, or the greatest perfection.**

### Text 27

*upāya haiyā āvirbhūta dharātale*  
*upeya upāya aikya sarva śāstre bale*

**Перевод:**

**The method (of chanting the Holy Name) has appeared upon the earth. The means and the end are one and the same. This is stated by all the śāstra.**

### Text 28

*adhikāra-bhede yini upāya svarūpa*  
*tini-i upeya anye baḍa aparūpa*

**Перевод:**

**According to their different qualifications, some consider the Name to be the method, and others consider it to be the most comparable goal. (7)**

**Комментарий:**

(7) ‘Adhikāra-bhede’ means that as long as the jīva does not have ātmā–rati (attraction to the ātmā), then he will consider nāma to be the means to a goal. Therefore, he will continue to engage in sādhana as the means to attain the goal of ātmā–rati.

**Śubha-karma gauṇopāya nāma mukhyopāya (Auspicious Activities are the Indirect Means, and Nāma is the Direct Means)**

### Text 29

*ataeva upāya dvi-vidha guṇa dhāma*  
*gauṇopāya śubha-karma mukhyopāya nāma*

**Перевод:**

**Thus, O Abode of all good qualities, the method is of two types. The performance of auspicious activities is the indirect means, and the Holy Name is the direct method. (8)**

**Комментарий:**

8\) Although nāma is counted as the means, it is the direct means. All other types of auspicious activities are counted as indirect methods. One must understand this reality, that the Holy Name is distinct from other types of auspicious deeds.

**Перевод:**

**8) Although nāma is counted as the means, it is the direct means. All other types of auspicious activities are counted as indirect methods. One must understand this reality, that the Holy Name is distinct from other types of auspicious deeds.**

**Nāmera atīndriyatva (The Name is Beyond the Senses)**

### Text 30

*ataeva śāstre yata anya śubha-karma*  
*nāma saha nahe ei sarva-śāstra marma*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore, the śāstra says that other auspicious deeds are never equal to the Name. This is the meaning of all the śāstra.**

### Text 31

*sarala hṛdaye yabe kṛṣṇa-nāma gāya*  
*atīndriya-sukha āsi cittake nācāya*

**Перевод:**

**One who chants kṛṣṇa–nāma with a sincere heart experiences how happiness beyond the material senses dances within his heart.**

### Text 32

*sei sukha kṛṣṇa-nāma-svabhāva tatpara*  
*ātma-rati ātma-krīḍā nāhi yāra para*

**Перевод:**

**That happiness is connected to the intrinsic nature of kṛṣṇa-nāma. Even attachment or bliss in connection with the ātmā cannot compare.**

**Sāyujya kaivalya sukha ānanda sukhera chāyā mātra (The Happiness of Absorption and Identification with Brahman is But a Shadow of that Bliss)**

### Text 33

*brahma-jñāne yoge ye ānanda vaibhava*  
*jaḍera viccheda sukha chāyā anubhava*

**Перевод:**

**The great bliss experienced through knowledge of Brahman or yoga is merely the cessation of gross materialism, and is thus a shadow of the happiness (found in kṛṣṇa–nāma).**

### Text 34

*abhedya kaivalya sukha svalpa bali jāni*  
*kṛṣṇa-nāmānanda-sukha bhūmā bali māni*

**Перевод:**

**I know that happiness in the indivisible Brahman is meagre. but I consider the bliss of chanting kṛṣṇa–nāma to be the greatest.**

**Anya śubha-karma haite nāmera vailakṣaṇya (Other Auspicious Activities are Different to the Holy Name)**

### Text 35

*sādhana-kālete nāma upāya svarūpa*  
*siddhi-kāle upeya se ei aparūpa*

**Перевод:**

**At the time of sādhana, the Name is the means. At the time of attaining perfection, it is the incomparable goal.**

### Text 36

*upāya svarūpa nāme upeyatva siddha*  
*anya śubha-karme aiche nahe ta prasiddha*

**Перевод:**

**The Name is the means and the goal of all perfection. Other auspicious activities are not important.**

### Text 37

*anya śubha-karma yata saba jaḍāśrita*  
*nāma ta cinmaya sadā svataḥ siddhodita*

**Перевод:**

**All other auspicious activities take refuge of matter. The Name is spiritual and eternally manifests as self-perfect.**

### Text 38

*sādhana kāleo nāma śuddha su-nirmala*  
*sādhakera anarthete dekhāya sa-mala*

**Перевод:**

**Even at the time of sādhana, the Name remains pure and completely free from any defect, but the anarthas of the sādhaka make it look as if it is impure.**

### Text 39

*sādhu-saṅge nāma laite jaḍa-buddhi yāya*  
*anartha niḥśeṣa haile śuddha nāma bhāya*

**Перевод:**

**Material conceptions leave when one takes the Name in the association of sādhus. When anarthas are finished, then śuddha–nāma shines forth.**

### Text 40

*anya śubha-karmī kare tyajiyā upāya*  
*upeya parama-bhāva carame āśraya*

**Перевод:**

**Rejecting all other auspicious activities as the means, one should take shelter of the topmost ecstasy (parama–bhāva).**

### Text 41

*kintu nāmāśrayī jana nāma nāhi tyaje*  
*nāmera śuddhatā mātra siddhi-kāle bhaje*

**Перевод:**

**But one who takes shelter of the Name never abandons it. They simply worship śuddha–nāma even at the time of perfection.**

### Text 42

*anya śubha-karma haite ati vilakṣaṇa*  
*nāmera svarūpa haya apūrva lakṣaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**It is completely different from other auspicious activities. Intrinsically, the Name has unparalleled qualities.**

### Text 43

*sādhana daśāya ei vilakṣaṇa jñāna*  
*guru kṛpā haite haya vedera pramāṇa*

**Перевод:**

**At the stage of sādhana, by the guru’s mercy and through the evidence of the Vedas, one should understand these differences (between the Name and auspicious deeds). (9)**

**Комментарий:**

(9) From śraddhā comes sādhu-saṅga. From sādhu-saṅga comes bhajana-kriyā, and by constantly engaging in bhajana, one’s anarthas are removed. To the extent that anartha–nivṛtti occurs, the purity of the Name will arise, and as niṣṭḥā progresses, ātmā–rati emerges. At the time of sādhana, these truths must be understood with faith by the saāhaka by the mercy of the guru. Otherwise one will make nāmāparādha and anarthas will increase.

### Text 44

*sādhana daśāya yini ei jñāna hīna*  
*nāma aparādhī tiṁha ati arvācīna*

**Перевод:**

**If one is bereft of this knowledge at the stage of sādhana, one is an offender to the Name and totally backwards.**

### Text 45

*nāma sarvopari nāma-tulya kichu naya*  
*e dṛḍha viśvāsa kari yei nāma laya*

**Перевод:**

**The Name is the highest of all and has no equal – one who has firm faith in this may properly chant the Name.**

### Text 46

*acire tāṅhāte haya śuddha-nāmodaya*  
*pūrṇānanda nāma-rasa karena āśraya*

**Перевод:**

**Very soon śuddha–nāma arises and he takes shelter in the completely blissful nāma–rasa.**

**Ei aparādhera pratikāra (The Remedy for this Offence)**

### Text 47

*kāhāro yadyapi anya śubha-karma sane*  
*nāme sama buddhi haya duṣkṛti bandhane*

**Перевод:**

**One who considers other auspicious deeds to be equal to the Holy Name is bound by impiety.(10)**

**Комментарий:**

(10) ‘Duṣkṛti’ is offences to Vaiṣṇavas. As a result of this, a taste appears in the jīva towards the misconceptions of the māyāvādīs in regards to the Name. If this impiety is destroyed through sādhu–saṅga, then one will attain nāma–śraddhā.

### Text 48

*se duṣkṛti kṣaya lāgi karibe yatana*  
*nāme śuddha buddhi pābe prema-dhana*

**Перевод:**

**One should endeavour to destroy such impiety. Then through the Name, one’s intelligence will be purified and one will achieve the great wealth of prema.**

### Text 49

*antyaja gṛhastha śuddha-nāma parāyaṇa*  
*tāṅra pada dhūli dehe karibe mṛkṣaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**One should smear one’s body with dust from the feet of an outcaste gṛhastha who is dedicated to śuddha-nāma. (11)**

**Комментарий:**

(11) Rejecting the notion of vaiṣṇave jāti buddhi (considering a Vaiṣnava to be of a particular caste or community), one should smear on one’s body the foot-dust of a sādhu who is devoted to śuddha-nāma.

### Text 50

*khāibe adharāmṛta pibe pada-jala*  
*tabe śuddha nāme mati haibe nirmala*

**Перевод:**

**One should eat his nectarean food-remnants and drink the water that has washed his feet. Then one’s consciousness will be purified and one will attain śuddha-nāma.**

### Text 51

*kāli-dāse ei rūpe duṣkṛti khaṇḍana*  
*punaḥ tava kṛpā-prāpti gāya jagaj-jana*

**Перевод:**

**Everyone in the world sings about how Kālī Dāsa destroyed such impiety by Your mercy.**

### Text 52

*āmi jaḍa-buddhi nātha nāma mātra gāi*  
*nāma-cintāmaṇi-tattva kabhu nāhi pāi*

**Перевод:**

**O Lord, I possess dull intelligence – I just chant the Name, but I have never attained the cintāmaṇi gem of the Holy Name.**

**Hari dāsa ṭhākurera nāma-viṣaye niṣṭhā (Hari Dāsa Ṭhākura’s Attachment to the Name)**

### Text 53

*kṛpā kari’ nāma-rūpe āmāra jihvāya*  
*nirantara nāca prabhu dhari tava pāya*

**Перевод:**

**Be merciful unto me. Assuming the form of the Name, constantly dance upon my tongue. O Lord, I hold Your feet.**

### Text 54

*rākha iṅhā lao tāṅhā tava icchā mata*  
*yāṅhā rākha deha more kṛṣṇa-nāmāmṛta*

**Перевод:**

**Keep me here, or take me there – whatever is Your desire. Wherever this body remains, kindly give me the nectar of kṛṣṇa-nāma. (12)**

**Комментарий:**

(12) Iṅhā (here) – the material world. Tāṅhā (there) – the spiritual world.

### Text 55

*jagaj-jane nāma dite tava avatāra*  
*jagaj-jana-mājhe more kara aṅgīkāra*

**Перевод:**

**Your avatāra has descended to distribute the Name to the people of this world. Kindly accept me as one of those people.**

### Text 56

*āmi ta adhama tumi adhama tāraṇa*  
*ubhaye sambandha ei patita pāvana*

**Перевод:**

**I am fallen and You are the deliverer of the fallen. O purifier of the fallen, that is our relationship.**

### Text 57

*acchedya sambandha ei tomāya āmāya*  
*yāra bale nāmāmṛta e adhama cāya*

**Перевод:**

**The relationship between You and I can never be broken. This fallen person desires the nectar of the Name.**

**Kali-yuge nāme kena yuga-dharma hailena (Why the Name is the Yuga-dharma in Kali-yuga)**

### Text 58

*kali-yuge su-duḥsādhya anya śubha-karma*  
*ataeva nāma āsi haila yuga-dharma*

**Перевод:**

**In Kali-yuga other auspicious activities produce no results. Therefore the Name is the only yuga–dharma. (13)**

**Комментарий:**

(13) The Name is the highest dharma at all times, but in the age of Kali there is no faith in other types of dharma, so the Name has descended in the form of the yuga-dharma to deliver the jīvas of this world from their suffering.

### Text 59

*hari-dāsa-dāsa bhaktivinoda se jana*  
*hari-nāma-cintāmaṇi gāya akiñcana*

**Перевод:**

**Bhaktivinoda, who is bereft of everything, a servant of the servant of Hari Dāsa, sings this Hari-nāma-cintāmaṇi.**

## Chapter 12. Nāmāparādha—pramāda (Inattention as an Offence to the Name)

### Text 1

*jaya jaya mahāprabhu jaya bhakta-gaṇa*  
*yādera prasāde kari nāma-saṅkīrtana*

**Перевод:**

**Glories to Mahāprabhu, glories to the devotees. It is by their mercy that I engage in nāma–saṅkīrtana.**

**Pramāda-nāmaka aparādha (Inattention as an Offence to the Name)**

### Text 2-3

*hari-dāsa bale prabhu hethā sanātane*  
*āra ta gopāla bhaṭṭa dakṣiṇa bhramaṇe*

*śikhāile apramāde śrī-kṛṣṇa-bhajana*  
*pramādake aparādhe karile gaṇana*

**Перевод:**

**Hari Dāsa said, “O Lord, You taught to Sanātana here, and to Gopāla Bhaṭṭa while travelling in the south, that one should not be inattentive during śrī-kṛṣṇa-bhajana. Thus, pramāda (inattention) is counted as an offence. (1)**

**Комментарий:**

(1) He told to Śrī Sanātana:

*eka aṅga sādhe keha sādhe bahu aṅga*  
*niṣṭhā haile upajaya premera taraṅga*

**(“Whether one performs one or many divisions of bhakti, when niṣṭhā appears, the waves of prema will awaken.” (Śrī Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya-līlā 22.134)**

By abandoning pramāda, or inattention, then niṣṭhā at the time of sādhana is born.

**Перевод:**

**By abandoning pramāda, or inattention, then niṣṭhā at the time of sādhana is born.**

### Text 4

*anya aparādha tyaji sadā nāma laya*  
*tabu nāme prema nāhi haya ta udaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Some give up all the other offences and constantly chant the Name, but still prema for the Name does not arise in them.**

### Text 5

*tabe jāni pramāda nāmete aparādha*  
*prema-bhakti sādhanete kariteche bādha*

**Перевод:**

**“Then I know that the nāmāparādha of pramāda is impeding their sādhana and their attainment of prema-bhakti.**

**Anavadhānakei pramāda bale (Inattention is Known as Pramāda)**

### Text 6

*pramāda anavadhāna ei mūla artha*  
*ihā haite ghaṭe prabhu sakala anartha*

**Перевод:**

**“The original meaning of pramāda is ‘inattention.’ O Lord, from this, all other anarthas arise.**

**Tina prakāra anavadhāna (The Three Types of Inattention)**

### Text 7

*audāsīnya jāḍya āra vikṣepa e tina*  
*prakāra anavadhāna bujhibe pravīṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“Those who are wise understand three types of inattention—audāsīnya (indifference), jāḍya (laziness) and vikṣepa (distraction). (2)**

**Комментарий:**

(2) In respect to sādhana, audāsīnya means a lack of niṣṭhā, jāḍya means laziness, and vikṣepa means that the mind is invested in other things.

**Anurāga nā haoyā paryanta nāma-grahaṇe yatnera āvaśyakatā (If One has not Achieved Anurāga, then it is Required that One Should Chant the Name with Great Care)**

### Text 8

*kona bhāgye kona jīvera śraddhā yadi haya*  
*tabe tiṅha hari-nāma grahaṇa karaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Due to some good fortune, if a jīva attains śraddhā, then he can chant hari–nāma. (3)**

**Комментарий:**

(3) One should keep count on tulasī beads, and gradually a desire will arise to increase the number.

### Text 9

*yatna kari smare nāma saṅkhyāra sahita*  
*tabe nāme anurāga haya ta udita*

**Перевод:**

**“By carefully remembering the Name and keeping count, anurāga (attraction) for the Name will develop.**

### Text 10

*ye paryanta anurāga nā haya udaya*  
*se paryanta yatna kari nāma sadā laya*

**Перевод:**

**“As long as anurāga has not appeared, one should endeavour to constantly chant the Name.**

**Yatnābhāve sādhakera citta sthira haya nā (The Sādhaka who is Careless Cannot Steady the Mind)**

### Text 11

*nisargataḥ loka saba viṣaye āsakta*  
*smṛti-kāle viṣaya-antare anurakta*

**Перевод:**

**“People are naturally attracted to sense objects. At the time when one remembers them, the internal senses become attached to them. (4)**

**Комментарий:**

(4) Viṣaya-antare (sense objects in the mind) – other things (unrelated to Kṛṣṇa).

### Text 12

*ruci yāya anya sthāne nāme udāsīna*  
*nāme citta magna nahe jape prati-dina*

**Перевод:**

**“By having a taste for other things, one becomes indifferent to the Name. Even by chanting japa every day, still one’s mind is not absorbed in the Holy Name.**

### Text 13

*citta eka dike āra anya dike nāma*  
*tāhāra maṅgala kise haya guṇa dhāma*

**Перевод:**

**“The mind will be in one direction, while the Name will be in another direction. O Abode of all good qualities, how can such a person attain any auspiciousness?**

### Text 14

*lakṣa nāma haile pūrṇa saṅkhyā mālā gaṇi*  
*hṛdaye nahila rasa bindu guṇa maṇi*

**Перевод:**

**“O Jewel of all qualities, one may complete chanting a lakh of Names (100,000 Names, or 64 rounds) on the beads, but not even a drop of rasa appears in the heart.**

### Text 15

*ei ta anavadhāna doṣera prakāra*  
*viṣayī hṛdaye prabhu baḍa durnivāra*

**Перевод:**

**“This is the fault with this kind of inattentiveness – it is extremely difficult to remove from the heart of those who are attached to sense objects.**

**Yatna karibāra vidhi (The Rules for Being Careful)**

### Text 16

*sādhu saṅge svalpa kāla chāḍiyā viṣaya*  
*nirjane laile nāma ei doṣa kṣaya*

**Перевод:**

**“In a short time, one can abandon sense objects through sādhu–saṅga. This fault is destroyed by chanting the Name in a secluded place. (5)**

**Комментарий:**

(5) Initially, one may regularly follow in this way for a half hour, starting with chanting the Name in a secluded place in the association of sādhus. Eventually, by observing the nature of those sādhus, one will emulate them, abandon his indifference and a desire (for service) will arise.

### Text 17

*krame krame kṛṣṇa-nāme citta haya sthira*  
*nirantara nāma-rase haya ta adhīra*

**Перевод:**

**“Gradually, the mind will become fixed upon kṛṣṇa-nāma, then one loses all composure by perpetually tasting nāma-rasa.**

### Text 18

*tulasīra sannikaṭe kṛṣṇa-līlā sthāne*  
*sādhu sannidhāne basi sātvata-vidhāne*

**Перевод:**

**“The sātvata–vidhāna (method prescribed by the sādhus) states that one should reside near tulasī, a place of Kṛṣṇa’s pastimes and in the company of sādhus. (6)**

**Комментарий:**

(6) Sātvata-vidhāna (the method prescribed by the sādhus) – the method of the previous sādhus absorbed in the bliss of bhajana. First one chants for half an hour, then an hour, then one gradually increases his chanting to two hours, then gradually his chanting is increased to one lakṣa (64 rounds), and then to three lakṣa (192 rounds). Such an increase develops naturally.

### Text 19

*krame kāla vṛddhi kari sei nāma smare*  
*ati śīghra viṣayera chanda haite tare*

**Перевод:**

**“Gradually, one increases his time meditating on the Name, and very quickly transcends the desire for sense objects.**

**Anya prakriyā. ei rūpa karile audāsīnya rūpa anavadhāna haya nā (Another Method, which Does Not Lead to Indifference)**

### Text 20

*athavā nirjane basi smari sādhu-rīti*  
*indriya pidhāna kari nāme kare mati*

**Перевод:**

**“Otherwise, one may sit in a secluded place, remembering the method of the sādhus. By covering the senses, one may fix the mind on the Name. (7)**

**Комментарий:**

(7) Indriya pidhāna kari (covering the senses) – sitting alone in a room with the door closed, or covering the eyes, nose etc. with a cloth during sādhana.

### Text 21

*satvare nāmete niṣṭhā ruci krame haya*  
*audāsīnya doṣe tāra krame haya kṣaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Swiftly one attains niṣṭhā and then gradually ruci (a taste) for the Name. Then the fault of indifference is eventually destroyed.**

**Jāḍya-janita anavadhāna lakṣaṇa (The Symptoms of Inattention Arising from Laziness)**

### Text 22

*jāḍye ye anavadhāna alasera mane*  
*tāhe ruci nāhi haya śrī-nāma-grahaṇe*

**Перевод:**

**“Inattention from jāḍya stems from an idle mind. Thus, a taste will not arise when chanting śrī–nāma.**

### Text 23

*smṛti-kāle punaḥ śīghra virāme prayāsa*  
*ei doṣe nāma-rasa nā haya prakāśa*

**Перевод:**

**“At the time of remembering (the Holy Name) one will endeavour to finish one’s chanting quickly. Due to this fault, nāma–rasa will not manifest.**

### Text 24

*anya kārye vṛthā kāla nā haya yāpana*  
*sādhu-gaṇa ihā cinti smare anukṣaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“Sādhus never waste time in other useless activities, but remember the Name at every moment.(8)**

**Комментарий:**

(8) The symptom of a sādhu’s character is avyartha-kālatva-dharma (‘his nature is that he does not waste his time engaging in unnecessary activities’), and one should emulate this.

### Text 25

*nāma smare rase maje anya nāhi cāya*  
*sei rūpa sādhu-saṅge ei doṣa yāya*

**Перевод:**

**“Meditating upon nāma and absorbed in rasa, they desire nothing else. This fault (of laziness) vanishes in the association of sādhus.**

### Text 26

*anveṣiyā sei rūpa sādhu-saṅga kare*  
*tad-anukaraṇe citta jāḍya parihare*

**Перевод:**

**“In this way, one must search for sādhu–saṅga, and by emulating them, one will avoid mental laziness.(9)**

**Комментарий:**

(9) A viśuddha-sādhu-bhakta (pure devotee sādhu) is rare. Searching from place to place, one will eventually find sādhu–saṅga.

### Text 27

*avyartha kālatva dharma sādhura carita*  
*dekhile tāhāte ruci haibe niścita*

**Перевод:**

**“It is the nature of a sādhu’s character not to waste time in useless things. Observing such actions, an aspiring devotee will certainly attain ruci.**

### Text 28

*mane habe āhā kabe ihāra samāna*  
*smariba gāiba nāma haye bhāgyavān*

**Перевод:**

**“He will think, ‘Oh when will I be the same as this? When will I become fortunate enough to remember and chant the Name like him?’”**

### Text 29

*sei ta utsāha āsi alasera mane*  
*jāḍya dūra kare kṛṣṇa-nāmera smaraṇe*

**Перевод:**

**“In this way, he will become enthusiastic. Lazy thoughts stemming from jāḍya will go far away and he will be able to remember kṛṣṇa–nāma.**

### Text 30

*mane habe āja lakṣa nāma ye kariba*  
*krame krame tina lakṣa nāma ye smariba*

**Перевод:**

**“He will think, ‘Today I will chant one lakṣa of Holy Names and gradually I will meditate upon three lakṣa of Names.’**

### Text 31

*mahāgraha habe citte nāmera saṅkhyāya*  
*acire yāibe jāḍya sādhura kṛpāya*

**Перевод:**

**“In his mind he has great eagerness and he chants a fixed number of Names. Very soon, jāḍya goes away, by the mercy of the sādhu.**

**Vikṣepa janita anavadhāna lakṣaṇa (Understanding the Characteristics of Distraction)**

### Text 32

*vikṣepa haite yei pramāda udaya*  
*bahu yatne sei aparādha haya kṣaya*

**Перевод:**

**“The inattention that comes from vikṣepa is an aparādha which can only be destroyed with great effort.**

### Text 33

*kanaka kāminī āra jaya parājaya*  
*pratiṣṭhāśā śāṭhya-vṛtti tāhāra nilaya*

**Перевод:**

**“It resides in the desire for gold, women, the victory and defeat of others, hankering for prestige, and the tendency to deceive. (10)**

**Комментарий:**

(10) Tāhāra nilaya (‘they reside there’) – these are the residences for jāḍya.

### Text 34

*e saba ākṛṣṭi hṛde haile udaya*  
*nāmete anavadhāna svabhāvataḥ haya*

**Перевод:**

**“When attraction for all these arises in the heart, one naturally becomes inattentive to the Name. (11)**

**Комментарий:**

(11) Ākṛṣṭa – “pulled or drawn towards.”

**Vikṣepa-tyāgera upāya (The Method of Abandoning Distraction)**

### Text 35

*krame krame sei saba cintā parihāre*  
*yatibe saubhāgyavān vaiṣṇava ācāre*

**Перевод:**

**“Gradually, one gives up thinking about all these things. One who is most fortunate endeavours to follow the conduct of a Vaiṣṇava.(12)**

**Комментарий:**

(12) Yatibe – struggles.

### Text 36

*prathamete hari-dine bhoga-cintā tyaji*  
*sādhu-saṅge rātra-dina hari-nāma bhaji*

**Перевод:**

**“Firstly, on days that are sacred to Hari, one should renounce even the thought of food, and in the association of sādhus, worship hari-nāma day and night. (13)**

**Комментарий:**

(13) Hari–dina—Hari-vasara, Ekādaśī, appearance days of the Lord etc.

### Text 37

*hari-kṣetre hari-dāsa hari-śāstre laye*  
*utsave majibe sukhe parama nirbhaye*

**Перевод:**

**“In a place sacred to Hari, with Hari’s servants, and reading śāstra about Hari, one should absorb oneself in such supremely blissful festivals without any fear. (14)**

**Комментарий:**

(14) Hari-kṣetra – Śrī Navadvīpa, Śrī Vṛndāvana, Śrī Puruṣottama etc. Hari-dāsa – those pure Vaiṣṇavas who are rūpānugas. Hari-śāstra – the śruti, the Gītā, Śrī Bhāgavata, and all texts that follow Vaiṣṇava siddhānta.

### Text 38

*krame bhakti-kāla mana karibe vardhana*  
*hari-kathā mahotsave majāiyā mana*

**Перевод:**

**“Gradually, one spends more and more time rapt in activities connected to bhakti. The mind becomes immersed in a festival of bliss when engaged in hari–kathā.**

### Text 39

*śreṣṭha rasa krame citte haibe udaya*  
*jaḍera nikṛṣṭa rasa chāḍibe niścaya*

**Перевод:**

**“Eventually, the greatest rasa dawns within the heart. Then one will definitely abandon the mediocre rasa of mundane things.**

### Text 40

*mahājana mukhe hari-saṅgīta śravaṇe*  
*mugdha habe manaḥ karṇa rasa āsvādane*

**Перевод:**

**“Hearing songs about Hari from the mouth of a mahājana, one’s mind and ears become charmed and relish such rasa.**

### Text 41

*nikṛṣṭa viṣaya-spṛhā haibe vigata*  
*nāma gāne citta sthira habe avirata*

**Перевод:**

**“When the desire for inferior sense objects disappears, the mind becomes steady in chanting the Holy Name.**

### Text 42

*ataeva bahu yatne e pramāda tyaje*  
*sthira citte nāma-rase cira dina maje*

**Перевод:**

**“Thus, one should endeavour to reject this type of pramāda, then with a steady mind one will always be immersed in nāma–rasa.**

**Āgraha (Eagerness)**

### Text 43

*saṅkalpita nāma saṅkhyā pūrṇa karibāre*  
*nā haya ayatna nāme dekhi bāre bāre*

**Перевод:**

**“One must be determined to complete one’s prescribed number of Names. Again and again, one should not be careless in observing the Name. (15)**

**Комментарий:**

(15) Those who are addicted to inattention in the form of vikṣepa, try to finish their prescribed number of Names as quickly as possible. One should not be careless and, again and again, maintain caution in observing one’s nāma-sādhana.

### Text 44

*satarka haiyā kari nāma saṅkīrtana*  
*pramāda chāḍiyā kari nāmera bhajana*

**Перевод:**

**“Perform nāma–saṅkīrtana with care. Reject pramāda and worship the Name.**

### Text 45

*saṅkhyādhika spṛhā chāḍi ekāgra-mānase*  
*nirantara kari nāma tava kṛpā-bale*

**Перевод:**

**“One must reject the desire to simply increase the number (of rounds), and with one-pointed concentration one will incessantly chant the Holy Names, by Your mercy. (16)**

**Комментарий:**

(16) “Increasing the number of names” – one should try to chant the syllables of the Name clearly and with feeling. It is proper to make an endeavour in this regard.

### Text 46

*ei kṛpā kara prabhu nāmete pramāda*  
*nā bādhe āmāra citte nāma rasāsvāda*

**Перевод:**

**“O Lord, please give Your mercy, so that inattention to the Name never becomes an obstacle to my mind, and I can relish nāma-rasa.**

**Prakriyā (The Method)**

### Text 47

*ekāgra mānase nirjanete svalpa kṣaṇa*  
*nāma smṛti abhyāsa karibe bhakta jana*

**Перевод:**

**“One should spend a little time alone, and practice meditating upon the Name with single-minded attention.**

### Text 48

*ataeva spaṣṭa nāma bhāva lagna mane*  
*sadā haya e prārthanā tomāra caraṇe*

**Перевод:**

**“Thus, one will be able to chant the Name clearly and with feeling within one’s mind. This is my constant prayer at Your feet.**

### Text 49

*āpana yatnete keha nāhi pāre*  
*tomāra prasāda vinā e bhava-saṁsāre*

**Перевод:**

**“By one’s own endeavours, no one in this world of birth and death can overcome distraction without Your mercy. (17)**

**Комментарий:**

(17) “No jīva can overcome pramāda by his own effort. However, by Your mercy, it becomes easy. Therefore, in all endeavours it is necessary to pray fervently for Your mercy.”

**Yatnāgrahera āvaśyakatā. Niṣkapaṭa nāma grahaṇe tāhā avaśya thāke, natuvā aparādha (It is Necessary to Make a Great Endeavour. One Must Chant the Name Sincerely, without Duplicitous Pretense, Otherwise There Will be an Offence)**

### Text 50

*yatna kari kṛpā māgi vyākula antare*  
*tumi kṛpāmaya kṛpā kara ataḥpare*

**Перевод:**

**“I shall try to beg for Your mercy with a desperate heart. You are the repository of compassion, so kindly be merciful.**

### Text 51

*tava kṛpā lābhe yadi nā kari yatana*  
*tabe āmi bhāgya hīna he śacī-nandana*

**Перевод:**

**“O Śacī-nandana! If I do not try to attain Your mercy, then I will be bereft of any good fortune.” (18)**

**Комментарий:**

(18) Those who engage in bhajana on the strength of their own intelligence or wealth can never achieve success. Kṛṣṇa’s mercy is at the root of all activities. Therefore, one who does not endeavour to attain Kṛṣṇa’s mercy is deprived of good fortune. At the end of this chapter, it is said that one should practice meditating on the Name with one-pointed concentration. In this regard, Śrī Mahāprabhu gave the following advice to all jīvas (Caitanya-bhāgavata, Madhya-khāṇḍa 23.75-78):

*āpane sabāre prabhu kare upadeśa*  
*kṛṣṇa-nāma mahā-mantra śunaha hariṣe*  
*hare kṛṣṇa hare kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa hare hare*  
*hare rāma hare rāma rāma rāma hare hare*  
*prabhu bale hari-nāma ei mahā-mantra*  
*ihā japa giyā sabe kariyā nirbandha*  
*ihā haite sarva-siddhi haibe sabāra*  
*sarva-kṣaṇa bala ithe vidhi nāhi āra*

**(“The Lord gave everyone this instruction: ‘Be happy by hearing the mahā–mantra comprised of Kṛṣṇa’s names – Hare kṛṣṇa hare kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa hare hare, hare rāma hare rāma rāma rāma hare hare.’ The Lord said – ‘Chant this mahā–mantra. All of you chant it a prescribed number of times. By doing so, you will attain all perfection. Chant at every moment. There is no other rule to follow.”)**

Here, the word nirbandha means that the devotee will chant these sixteen names and thirty-two syllables, one-hundred and eight times on tulasī beads. Four rounds on the beads is a grantha. One should regularly chant one grantha, and then gradually increase till one is chanting sixteen granthas, or one lakṣa (64 rounds). When one gradually increases to chanting three lakṣas (192 rounds), then all his time will be spent with the Name. All the previous mahājanas obeyed the Lord’s instruction and attained all perfection. It is still possible for everyone to attain all perfection through nāma–japa. Those who are already liberated (mukta), those who desire liberation (mumukṣu) and materialists (viṣayī) are all eligible to chant the Name. When chanting the Name, dIfferent emotions are seen in the mukta etc. In both the state of viraha (separation) and sambhoga (union), the Holy Name is eternally relished.

### Text 52

*hari-nāma-cintāmaṇi alaṅkāra yāra*  
*hari-dāsa-pada-yuga bharasā tāhāra*

**Перевод:**

**This person, who decorates himself with this Hari-nāma Cintāmaṇi, has faith in the feet of Hari Dāsa.**

## Chapter 13. Ahaṁ-mama-bhāvāparādha (The Offence of Considering ‘I’ and “Mine’)

*śrute’pi nāma‑māhātmye yaḥ prīti‑rahito naraḥ*  
*ahaṁ‑mamādi‑paramo nāmni so’py aparādha‑kṛt*

**That person who is so fallen that, even after hearing the glories of the Name, is still devoid of love for the Name, but maintains the idea of ‘I’ and ‘mine’ makes an offence.**

### Text 1

*gadāi-gaurāṅga jaya jāhnavā jīvana*  
*sītādvaita jaya jaya gaura-bhakta-jana*

**Перевод:**

**Glories to Gadāi-Gaurāṅga and the Life of Jāhnavā (Nityānanda). Glories to Sītā and Advaita and the devotees of Gaura.**

### Text 2

*preme gada gada hari-dāsa mahāśaya*  
*śeṣa nāma aparādha prabhu-pade kaya*

**Перевод:**

**With his voice choked with prema, Hari Dāsa Mahāśaya presented the final nāmāparādha at the feet of the Lord.**

### Text 3

*śuna prabhu ei aparādha sarvādhama*  
*ei doṣe nāma prema nā haya udgama*

**Перевод:**

**“O Lord, please hear about the lowest offence of all. Nāma–prema will never appear while this fault is present. (1)**

**Комментарий:**

(1) Even after accepting dīkṣā, many people who are materially attached to the physical body, maintain the mentality of ‘I’ and ‘mine’ and fall from the path of bhakti. “I am a brāhmaṇa, I am a Vaiṣṇava, I am a king, this is my body, my house, my children, my grandchildren, my wealth, my followers” – such an arrogant, false conception (abhimāna) does not lead to a desire to cultivate nāma-bhajana. This is a heinous offence. One who is surrendered to the Name will never commit such an offence.

**Nāme śaraṇāpattira prayojanīyatā (The Necessity of Surrendering to the Name)**

### Text 4

*anya naya aparādha kariyā varjjana*  
*nāmete śaraṇāpanna haibe sajjana*

**Перевод:**

**“Rejecting the nine offences, a true devotee (sajjana) will surrender to the Holy Name.**

### Text 5

*ṣaḍ-vidha śaraṇāgati sarva śāstre kaya*  
*vistārita balite āmāra sādhya naya*

**Перевод:**

**“All the śāstra describes the six types of surrender. It is not feasible for me to explain them in detail now.**

**Śaraṇāpattira prakāra (The Various Kinds of Surrender)**

### Text 6

*saṁkṣepe caraṇe tava kari nivedana*  
*ānukūlye saṅkalpa prātikūlya visarjana*

**Перевод:**

**“I will offer this summary at Your feet – making a vow to accept that which is favourable, and rejecting that which is unfavourable. (2)**

**Комментарий:**

(2) Anukūlye saṅkalpa – “I will only accept those things in life that are favourable to bhakti” – with this vow, one accepts those things that are favourable. “I will completely reject those things that are unfavourable to bhakti” – With this vow, one rejects those things that are unfavourable.

### Text 7

*kṛṣṇe rakṣā-kārī buddhi pālaka bhāvana*  
*nije dīna buddhi āra ātma-nivedana*

**Перевод:**

**“Having the understanding that Kṛṣṇa will give protection and maintain you, considering oneself to be insignificant, and surrendering oneself completely.**

### Text 8

*e jīvana nā rahile nā haya bhajana*  
*jīvana rakṣāya mātra viṣaya grahaṇa*

**Перевод:**

**“If one cannot preserve his life, he cannot engage in bhajana, so only to maintain life should one accept material things.**

### Text 9

*bhakti anukūla ye viṣaya yatakṣaṇa*  
*tāhe rocamāna-vṛttye jīvana yāpana*

**Перевод:**

**“At every moment, one should continue one’s life by developing an interest in those things that are favourable to bhakti. (3)**

**Комментарий:**

(3) Rocamāna-vṛtti (developing an interest) – having a taste which is conducive to establishing a relationship with Kṛṣṇa.

### Text 10

*bhakti pratikūla ye viṣaye yabe haya*  
*tāhāte aruci tāhā varjibe niścaya*

**Перевод:**

**“When something is unfavourable for bhakti, one has no taste for it, and totally rejects it.**

### Text 11

*kṛṣṇa vinā rakṣā-kartā nāhi keha āra*  
*kṛṣṇa se pālaka mātra jānibe āmāra*

**Перевод:**

**“One will think, ‘Apart from Kṛṣṇa, no one else is my protector. I know that Kṛṣṇa is my only maintainer.**

### Text 12

*āmi dīna akiñcana sakalera chāra*  
*adhama durgata kichu nāhika āmāra*

**Перевод:**

**” ‘I am insignificant and poverty-stricken. I am the most useless person of all. No one is as fallen or as miserable as me.**

### Text 13

*kṛṣṇera saṁsāre āmi āchi cira dāsa*  
*kṛṣṇa icchā mata kriyā āmāra prayāsa*

**Перевод:**

**“‘I am an eternal servant in the family of Kṛṣṇa, I only endeavour to act according to the desire of Kṛṣṇa.**

### Text 14-16

*āmi kartā āmi dātā āmi pālayitā*  
*āmāra e deha geha santāna vanitā*

*āmi vipra āmi śūdra āmi pitā pati*  
*āmi rājā āmi prajā santānera gati*

*ei saba buddhi chāḍi kṛṣṇe kari mati*  
*kṛṣṇa kartā kṛṣṇa icchā mātra balavatī*

**Перевод:**

**“‘I am the doer. I am the benefactor. I am the maintainer. This body and house are mine. These are my children, this is my wife. I am a brāhmaṇa. I am a śūdra. I am a father. I am a husband. I am a king. I am a subject. I am the shelter of my children.’ – I will reject all these ideas and only think of Kṛṣṇa. He is the doer, and His desire alone is the greatest.**

### Text 17

*kṛṣṇera ye haya icchā tāhāi kariba*  
*nija icchā anusāre kichu nā cintiba*

**Перевод:**

**“‘I will do whatever Kṛṣṇa desires. I will not even think about anything according to my own desires.**

### Text 18

*kṛṣṇa icchā mate haya āmāra saṁsāra*  
*kṛṣṇa icchā mate āmi hai bhava-pāra*

**Перевод:**

**“‘By the desire of Kṛṣṇa I have been placed in saṁsāra, and by Kṛṣṇa’s desire I will be delivered from this material world.**

### Text 19

*duḥkhe thāki sukhe thāki āmi kṛṣṇa dāsa*  
*kṛṣṇecchāya sarva jīve dayāra prakāśa*

**Перевод:**

**“‘Whether in distress or in happiness, I am a servant of Kṛṣna. It is by Kṛṣṇa’s desire that I show compassion to all jīvas.**

### Text 20

*mama bhoga karma-bhoga kṛṣṇa icchā mata*  
*āmāra vairāgya kṛṣṇa icchā anugata*

**Перевод:**

**“‘My experiences and the experience of my karmika reactions are dependent upon Kṛṣṇa’s desire. My renunciation is also dependent upon Kṛṣna’s desire. (4)**

**Комментарий:**

(4) My experience of the results of karma within this world, and my renunciation of that are both dependent upon Kṛṣṇa.

**Śaraṇāpatti haile ātma-nivedana haya (When One Surrenders, One Dedicates Oneself Completely)**

### Text 21

*sarala bhāvete yabe ei bhāva haya*  
*ātma nivedana tāre bali mahāśaya*

**Перевод:**

**“O Mahāśaya, when a mood of sincerity arises, this is known as ātma–nivedana (full dedication of the self).**

**Śaraṇāpatti vyatīta nāmāśraye yāhā haya (Taking Refuge in the Name without Surrender)**

### Text 22

*ṣaḍ-vidha śaraṇāgati nāhika yāhāra*  
*se adhama ahaṁ mama buddhi doṣe chāra*

**Перевод:**

**“Whoever does not follow the six divisions of śaraṇāgati (surrender) becomes most fallen and useless, due to the defect of thinking in terms of ‘I’ and ‘mine.’**

### Text 23

*se bale āmi ta kartā saṁsāra āmāra*  
*nija karma phala bhoga sukha duḥkha āra*

**Перевод:**

**“They declare, ‘I am the doer! This is my clan! I only experience happiness and distress because of my own actions.**

### Text 24

*āmāra rakṣaka āmi, āmi ta pālaka*  
*āmāra vanitā bhrātā bālikā bālaka*

**Перевод:**

**“‘I am my own protector, and I am the support for my wife, brothers, daughters, and sons.**

### Text 25

*āmi ta arjana kari āmāra ceṣṭāya*  
*sarva kārya siddha haya sarva śobhā pāya*

**Перевод:**

**“‘My achievements are due to my hard work, and because of that, everything is perfect and beautiful.’**

### Text 26

*ahaṁ mama buddhi krame bahirmukha jana*  
*nija jñāna bale bahu karaye mānana*

**Перевод:**

**“Owing to their mentality of ‘I’ and ‘mine,’ such persons are actually averse to the Supreme because they stress great importance on their own knowledge.**

### Text 27

*sei jñāna bale śilpa vijñāna vistāre*  
*īśvarera īśitā nā māne duṣṭācāre*

**Перевод:**

**“They develop knowledge about certain crafts and sciences, but, as a result of their bad conduct, they cannot recognise the hand of the Supreme. (5)**

**Комментарий:**

(5) Those who are averse think, “We are very intelligent in improving various arts, sciences etc. and this has increased our happiness.” The truth is that everything happens by Kṛṣṇa’s desire, yet such persons never remember this.

### Text 28

*śrī-nāma-māhātmya śuni viśvāsa nā kare*  
*loka vyavahāre kabhu kṛṣṇa-nāmoccāre*

**Перевод:**

**“They may hear the glories of śrī–nāma but they have no faith in them. However, due to social obligations they may sometimes chant kṛṣṇa-nāma.**

### Text 29

*kṛṣṇa-nāma kare tabu nāhi pāya prīti*  
*dharma-dhvajī śaṭha jana jīvane e rīti*

**Перевод:**

**“They chant kṛṣṇa-nāma, but still do not attain prīti. This is the behaviour of those who live the deceitful life of a dharma–dhvajī (a hypocrite wrapped in the flag of religion).**

### Text 30

*helāya uccāre nāma kichu puṇya haya*  
*prīti phala nāhi phale sarva-śāstre kaya*

**Перевод:**

**“By chanting the Name neglectfully one acquires some puṇya, but not the actual result of prīti. This is declared by all the śāstra.**

**Ihāra mūla ki? (What is the Basis of This?)**

### Text 31

*māyā baddha haite ei aparādha haya*  
*ihāte niṣkṛti lābha kaṭhina niścaya*

**Перевод:**

**“This offence is due to being conditioned by māyā. To achieve freedom from it is certainly very difficult.**

### Text 32

*śuddha-bhakti phale yāṅra virakti haila*  
*saṁsāra chāḍiyā sei nāmāśraya nila*

**Перевод:**

**“Those who have attained the result of śuddha–bhakti and are detached, have given up the material world and taken shelter of the Name.**

**Ei doṣa tyāgera upāya (The Method of Giving Up this Fault)**

### Text 33

*niṣkiñcana bhāve bhaje śrī kṛṣṇa caraṇa*  
*viṣaya chāḍiyā kare nāma saṅkīrtana*

**Перевод:**

**“Accepting the mood of a niṣkiñcana (one who has no desire for material wealth), they worship the feet of Śrī Kṛṣṇa. They abandon mundane sense objects and perform nāma-saṅkīrtana.**

### Text 34

*sei sādhu jane anveṣiyā tāṅra saṅga*  
*karibe sevibe chāḍi viṣaya taraṅga*

**Перевод:**

**“Such persons seek out the association of sādhus and serve them, rejecting the waves of mundanity.**

### Text 35

*krame krame nāme mati haibe sañcāra*  
*ahaṁtā mamatā yābe māyā habe pāra*

**Перевод:**

**“By doing this, eventually, the Name will manifest in one’s mind and the concept of ‘I’ and ‘mine’ will disappear, and you will transcend māyā.**

### Text 36

*nāmera māhātmya śuni ahaṁ mama bhāva*  
*chāḍiyā śaraṇātai bhaktera svabhāva*

**Перевод:**

**“It is natural for a devotee to surrender when he hears the glories of the Name and he will give up the mood of ‘I’ and ‘mine’.**

### Text 37

*nāmera śaraṇāgata yei mahājana*  
*kṛṣṇa nāma kare pāya prema mahā-dhana*

**Перевод:**

**“That mahājana who is surrendered to the Name chants kṛṣṇa-nāma and attains the great wealth of prema.**

**Daśāparādha śūnya vyaktira lakṣaṇa (The Symptoms of One who is Free from the Ten Offences)**

### Text 38

*ataeva sādhu-nindā yatane chāḍiyā*  
*para-tattva viṣṇu śuddha manete jāniyā*

**Перевод:**

**“Therefore, carefully giving up any criticism of sādhus, knowing within his purified mind that Viṣṇu is the Supreme Truth (para–tattva)… (6)**

**Комментарий:**

(6) One does not achieve everything just by giving up the ten offences. Apart from the ten offences there are also ten activities that one must perform. These are located in the instructions to give up the offences.

### Text 39

*nāma-guru nāma-śāstra sarvottama jāni*  
*viśuddha cinmaya nāma hṛdayete māni*

**Перевод:**

**…knowing that the nāma–guru (the guru gives the Holy Name) and the nāma–śāstra (those texts that glorify the Name) are supreme, considering within the heart that the Name is transcendentally pure and spiritual…**

### Text 40

*pāpa-spṛhā pāpa-bīja tyajiyā yatane*  
*pracāriyā śuddha-nāma śraddhānvita jane*

**Перевод:**

**…endeavouring to reject the desire to engage in pāpa, which is the seed of pāpa, teaching those persons who have śraddhā about śuddha–nāma…**

### Text 41

*anya śubha-karma haite laiyā virāma*  
*smare ye śaraṇāgata apramāde nāma*

**Перевод:**

**…and ceasing all other auspicious activities, one who is surrendered remembers the Name, free from inattention.**

**Niraparādhe nāme laile alpa dine bhāvodaya haya (After a Few Days Chanting Without Offence, Bhāva Arises)**

### Text 42

*sei dhanya tri-jagate sei bhāgyavān*  
*kṛṣṇa kṛpā yogya sei guṇera nidāna*

**Перевод:**

**“Such a person is blessed within the three worlds and most fortunate. He is eligible to receive Kṛṣṇa’s mercy. He is an abode of good qualities.**

### Text 43

*ati alpa dine tāṅra śrī-nāma-granaṇe*  
*bhāvodaya haya āra pāya prema-dhane*

**Перевод:**

**“In a few days, by chanting śrī–nāma, bhāva will manifest and they will attain the wealth of prema.**

**Unnati krama (The Stages of Advancement)**

### Text 44-45

*evambhūta janera sādhana-daśā prāya*  
*ati svalpa dine yāya kṛṣṇera icchāya*

*bhāva-daśā haite haite prema daśā haya*  
*prema daśā sarva-siddhi sarva-śāstra kaya*

**Перевод:**

**“After a few days, by the will of Kṛṣṇa, a person at the stage of sādhana (sādhana–daśā) comes to the stages of bhāva (bhāva-daśā) and then the stage of prema (prema–daśā). All the śāstra declares that this is the highest perfection. (7)**

**Комментарий:**

(7) If one takes refuge in the Name according to the teachings of Śrī Hari Dāsa Ṭhākura, one will cross the stage of sādhana–daśā in a few days.

### Text 46

*tumi baliyācha nāma yei mahājana*  
*laibe niraparādhe pābe prema-dhana*

**Перевод:**

**“You have stated that a mahājana who chants the Name without any offence will attain the wealth of prema.**

**Vyatireka bhāve ihāra cintā (Considering the Opposite of This)**

### Text 47

*aparādha nāhi chāḍi’ nāma yadi laya*  
*sahasra sādhane tāra bhakti nāhi haya*

**Перевод:**

**“If one chants the Holy Name but does not give up these offences, then even by engaging in thousands of sādhanas, one will never achieve bhakti.**

### Text 48

*jñāne mukti karme bhukti jñānī karmī jane*  
*sudurlabhā kṛṣṇa bhakti nirmala sādhane*

**Перевод:**

**“The jñānī and karmī know that through jñāna one attains mukti, and through karma one attains bhukti (material enjoyment), but it is extremely rare for them to attain kṛṣṇa–bhakti even by performing pure sādhana.**

### Text 49

*bhukti mukti śukti-sama bhakti muktā-phala*  
*jīvera mahimā bhakti-prāpti su-nirmala*

**Перевод:**

**“Bhukti and mukti are like oysters, whereas the result of bhakti is like a pearl. The jīva achieves glory when he attains pure unalloyed bhakti.**

### Text 50

*sādhane naipuṇya-yoge atyalpa-sādhane*  
*bhakti-latā prema-phala dena bhakta-jane*

**Перевод:**

**“When devotees are expert in the state of sādhana, the bhakti–latā (creeper of bhakti) awards them the fruit of prema, even after engaging in a little sādhana. (8)**

**Комментарий:**

(8) By engaging in sādhana expertly, devotees will attain prema, the fruit of the bhakti-latā, even after a little sādhana.

**Bhajana naipuṇya (Expertise in Bhajana)**

### Text 51

*daśa aparādha chāḍi nāmera grahaṇa*  
*ihāi naipuṇya haya sādhana bhajana*

**Перевод:**

**“Avoiding the ten offences while chanting the Holy Name is considered to be expertise in sādhana and bhajana.**

**Nāmāparādhera gurutā (The Severity of Nāmāparādha)**

### Text 52

*ataeva bhakti lābhe yadi lobha haya*  
*daśa aparādha chāḍi kari nāmāśraya*

**Перевод:**

**“Therefore, if one has greed to attain bhakti, one should avoid the ten offences, and take shelter of the Name.**

### Text 53

*eka eka aparādha satarka haiyā*  
*yatanete chāḍi citte vilāpa kariyā*

**Перевод:**

**“One must be careful about each of the offences, and lament in his heart for not giving them up.**

### Text 54

*nāmera caraṇe kari dṛḍha nivedana*  
*nāma kṛpā hale aparādha vidhvaṁsana*

**Перевод:**

**“By firmly dedicating oneself at the feet of the Holy Name, the Name will be merciful and one’s offences will be eradicated.**

### Text 55

*anya śubha-karme nāma aparādha kṣaya*  
*kona prāyaścitta yoge kabhu nāhi haya*

**Перевод:**

**“Other auspicious activities or various types of atonement can never destroy these offences.**

**Nāmāparādha parityāgera upāya (The Method to Give Up Nāmāparādha)**

### Text 56

*aviśrānta nāme nāma aparādha yāya*  
*tāhe aparādha kabhu sthāna nāhi pāya*

**Перевод:**

**“By continuous chanting of the Name, nāmāparādha disappears. Then those offences never find any place (in the sādhaka). (9)**

**Комментарий:**

(9) Aviśrānta-nāma (continuous chanting of the Name) – nāmāparādha will be destroyed if one chants the Name at all times, apart from when one needs to sleep in order to perform one’s daily functions. Nāmāparādha is not destroyed by any other auspicious deeds or atonement.

### Text 57

*divārātra nāma laya anutāpa kare*  
*tabe aparādha yāya nāma phala dhare*

**Перевод:**

**“Day and night, one should chant the Name and feel remorse. Then the offences will disappear, and one will achieve the result of nāma.**

### Text 58

*aparādha gate śuddha-nāmera udaya*  
*śuddha-nāma bhāvamaya āra premamaya*

**Перевод:**

**“When the offences have gone, śuddha-nāma appears. Śuddha–nāma overflows with bhāva and prema.**

### Text 59

*daśa aparādha yena hṛdaye nā paśe*  
*kṛpā kara mahāprabhu maji nāma rase*

**Перевод:**

**“O Mahāprabhu, be merciful and immerse me in nāma-rasa, so that these ten offences never enter my heart, ”**

### Text 60

*e bhaktivinoda hari-dāsa kṛpā bale*  
*hari-nāma-cintāmaṇi gāya kutūhale*

**Перевод:**

**By the mercy of Hari Dāsa, this Bhaktivinoda chants Hari-nāma Cintāmaṇi with astonishment.**

**Перевод:**

## Chapter 14. Sevāparādha (Offences During Service)

### Text 1

*jaya gaura-gadādhara jāhnavā jīvana*  
*jaya sītāpati śrīvāsādi-bhakta-jana*

**Перевод:**

**Glories to Gaura-Gadādhara and to the life of Jāhnavā (Nityānanda). Glories to the Lord of Sītā (Advaita), Śrīvāsa and the devotees.**

**Nāma-tattve śrī-haridāsa ṭhākurake ācārya baliyā ukti kariyāchen (Śrī Hari Dasa Ṭhākura is Declared the Ācārya of Nāma-Tattva)**

### Text 2

*mahāprabhu bale śuna bhakta hari-dāsa*  
*nāma aparādha tattva karile prakāśa*

**Перевод:**

**Mahāprabhu said, “Listen, Bhakta Hari Dāsa, you have revealed the truth about nāmāparādha.**

### Text 3

*ihāte kalira jīva labhibe maṅgala*  
*nāma-tattve tumi hao ācārya prabala*

**Перевод:**

**“Due to this, the jīvas of the age of Kali will attain auspiciousness. You are the great ācārya of nāma–tattva. (1)**

**Комментарий:**

(1) At the time of the avatāra of Śrī Caitanya, Śrī Hari Dāsa Ṭhākura was the ācārya of nāma–tattva. Just as the Thākura taught the jīvas about nāma, nāmābhāsa, the glories of the Name, and how to avoid nāmāparādha, he also taught them by his own example.

### Text 4

*tava mukhe nāma-tattva karite śravaṇa*  
*āmāra ullāsa baḍa śuna mahājana*

**Перевод:**

**“O mahājana, please listen—I am most pleased to hear nāma-tattva from your mouth.**

### Text 5

*ācāre ācārya tumi pracāre paṇḍita*  
*tomāra carita nāma-ratne vibhūṣita*

**Перевод:**

**“You are the ācārya of proper conduct, and most learned in teaching. Your character is adorned with the gem of the Holy Name.**

### Text 6

*rāmānanda śikhāila more rasa-tattva*  
*tumi śikhāile more nāmera mahattva*

**Перевод:**

**“Rāmānanda taught Me rasa–tattva and you have taught Me the glories of the Name.**

### Text 7

*ebe bala sevā aparādha ki prakāra*  
*śuniyā ghucibe jīvera citta andhakāra*

**Перевод:**

**“Now tell Me, what are the other types of offences when engaged in sevā? When the jīvas listen to this, the darkness in their hearts will vanish.”**

### Text 8

*hari-dāsa bale se sevaka-jana jāne*  
*āmi nāmāśraye thāki jāniba kemane*

**Перевод:**

**Hari Dāsa said, “Those who are engaged in sevā know about these. I have taken refuge in the Name. How would I know about these?**

### Text 9

*tabu tava ājñā āmi laṅghibāre nāri*  
*yāhā balāibe tāhā baliba vistāri*

**Перевод:**

**“Yet I cannot violate Your instruction. Whatever You make me say, I will explain in detail.**

**Sevāparādha saṅkhyā (The Number of Offences in Service)**

### Text 10

*sevā aparādha haya ananta prakāra*  
*śrī-mūrti sambandhe saba śāstrera vicāra*

**Перевод:**

**“There are unlimited offences found during sevā. All the śāstras describe them in relation to the Deity (śrī–mūrti).**

### Text 11

*kona śāstre dvātriṁśat aparādha gaṇi*  
*kona śāstre pañcāśat gane guṇa-maṇi*

**Перевод:**

**“Some śāstras enumerate thirty-two offences, and other śāstras say fifty, O jewel of all divine qualities.**

**Catur-vidha (The Four Types)**

### Text 12

*sei aparādha catur-vidhādi prakāre*  
*vibhāga karena budha gaṇa śāstre dvāre*

**Перевод:**

**“According to the śāstra, the wise divide these offences into four categories.**

### Text 13-14

*śrī-mūrti-sevaka-niṣṭha kataguli tāra*  
*śrī-mūrti-sthāpaka-niṣṭha aparādha āra*

*śrī-mūrti darśaka niṣṭha āra katipaya*  
*sarva-niṣṭha aparādha kati-vidha haya*

**Перевод:**

**“These offences relate to those serving the Deity, offences in installing the Deity, offences in looking at the Deity, and other types of general offences. (2)**

**Комментарий:**

(2) Sevāparādhas (offences in sevā) are in relation to service to the Deity. Some offences relate to those engaged in service to the Deity, there are offences related to those who install Deities, there are offences by those who see the Deity, and some offences are general. These can be easily understood.

**Sevāparādha dvātriṁśa prakāra (The Thirty-two Offences)**

### Text 15

*pādukā sahita yāya īśvara mandire*  
*yāne caḍi’ yāya tathā svacchanda-śarīre*

**Перевод:**

**“One should not enter the temple of the Lord wearing shoes, or in a vehicle for the comfort of one’s body…**

### Text 16

*utsave nā seve āra pragati nā kare*  
*ucchiṣṭha aśauca dehe vandana ācare*

**Перевод:**

**…One should not avoid engaging in service during a festival, nor should one avoid observing it; One should not enter the temple after eating or in an unclean state; one should not avoid offering prayers…**

### Text 17

*eka haste praṇāma sammukhe pradakṣiṇa*  
*devāgre prasāre pada haya vīrāsīna*

**Перевод:**

**…one should not offer praṇāma with one hand; one should not circumambulate (pradakṣiṇa) in front of the Deity; one should not spread one’s legs in front of the Deity; one should not sit in front of the Deity holding one’s knees or ankles…**

### Text 18

*devāgre śayana āra bhakṣaṇa karaya*  
*mithyā kathā ucca bhāṣā jalpanā nicaya*

**Перевод:**

**…one should not lie down in front of the Deity, nor eat in His presence; one should not speak lies nor speak loudly in front of the Deity. nor talk about mundane topics with others..**

### Text 19

*nigrahānugraha yuddha abhakti rodana*  
*krūra bhāṣā para nindā kambalāvaraṇa*

**Перевод:**

**…one should refrain from chastising or showing mercy to others in front of the Deity; one should not argue and fight; one should not cry for some mundane reason; one should not use harsh words, criticise others; one should not wear a blanket in front of the Deity…**

### Text 20

*para stuti, aślīlatā, vāyu-vimokṣaṇa*  
*śakti sattve gauṇa upacārera yojana*

**Перевод:**

**…one should not praise others, nor swear or speak about indecent things; one should not pass air in front of the Deity; one should not use inferior paraphernalia for the Deity if one has the capacity to offer something better…**

### Text 21

*devānivedita dravya bhakṣaṇe svīkāra*  
*kālodita phalādira anarpaṇa āra*

**Перевод:**

**…one should not eat anything that is not offered to the Deity; one must offer seasonal fruits etc. to the Deity…(3)**

**Комментарий:**

(3) Eating or drinking food or drink that has not been offered to the Deity is a sevāparādha for everyone.

### Text 22

*anya bhukta avaśiṣṭa khādya nivedana*  
*deva prati pṛṣṭha kari sammukhe āsana*

**Перевод:**

**…one should not offer the Deity someone else’s food remnants; one should not sit with one’s back to the Deity…(4)**

**Комментарий:**

(4) Offering a portion of food to the Deity that someone else has already partaken is an offence.

### Text 23

*devāgre anyera abhivādana pūjana*  
*guru prati mauna nija stotra ālocana*

**Перевод:**

**…one should not offer respects and worship to someone else in front of the Deity; one should not remain silent and not offer some praise of the guru, and one should not praise oneself…(5)**

**Комментарий:**

(5) One should not offer praise to anyone in the temple in front of the Deity, except to one’s guru.

### Text 24

*devatā nindana ei dvātriṁśa prakāra*  
*sevā aparādha mahā purāṇe pracāra*

**Перевод:**

**..one should also not slander the Devas. These are the thirty-two kinds of sevāparādha described in the mahā–purāṇas.**

**Anya śāstra mate prakāra varṇana (A Description in Another Śāstra)**

### Text 25

*anyatra āchaye aparādha anyatama*  
*saṁkṣepe baliba prabhu tava icchā mata*

**Перевод:**

**“O Lord, according to Your wish, I will briefly describe another list of offences from another source.**

### Text 26

*rājānna bhojana āra andhakāra ghare*  
*praveśiyā deva mūrti saṁsparśana kare*

**Перевод:**

**“One should not eat opulently, or enter a dark room and touch the Deity…**

### Text 27

*avidhi pūrvaka hari mūrtyapasarpaṇa*  
*vinā vādye mandirera dvāra udghāṭana*

**Перевод:**

**…one should not touch the Deity of Hari without the proper rituals; one should not open the doors of the temple without any sound…**

### Text 28

*sārameya dṛṣṭa khādya deve samarpaṇa*  
*arcana samaye mauna bhaṅga* ***akāraṇe***

**Перевод:**

**…One should not offer food to the Deity which has been seen by dogs etc; one should not break silence at the time of worship…**

### Text 29

*bahirdeśe gamanādi pūjāra samaye*  
*gandha mālya nāhi diyā dhūpana karaye*

**Перевод:**

**…during pūjā one should not go outside; one should not avoid offering fragrant garlands and incense…**

### Text 30

*anarha-puṣpete kṛṣṇa pūjādi karaṇa*  
*adhauta vadane kṛṣṇa pūjā ārambhana*

**Перевод:**

**…one should not offer scentless flowers during pūjā to Kṛṣṇa; one should clean one’s teeth before starting the worship of Kṛṣṇa…**

### Text 31

*strī saṅga kariyā kimbā rajaḥsvalā nārī*  
*dīpa, śava sparśiyā, ayogya vastra pari*

**Перевод:**

**…One should not enter the temple immediately after sexual union with one’s wife, nor after touching a woman during her menstrual cycle, a lamp or a dead body. One should not wear unclean cloth…**

### Text 32

*śava heri adhovāyu kariyā mokṣaṇa*  
*krodha kari śmaśānete kariyā gamana*

**Перевод:**

**…one should not enter the temple after seeing a dead body, nor pass gas in the temple. One should not be angry in front of the Deity, nor enter the temple after visiting a crematorium…**

### Text 33

*ajīrṇa udare āra kusumbha paināka*  
*sevana kariyā āra tāmbula guvāka*

**Перевод:**

**…one should not enter the temple until one’s food has been digested; one should not worship the Deity after eating safflower, asafoetida, tāmbula or betel-nut…**

### Text 34

*taila mākhi kari hari-śrī-mūrti-sparśana*  
*eraṇḍa-patrastha puṣpe karaya arcana*

**Перевод:**

**…one should not touch the Deity of Hari after taking an oil-bath; one should not perform worship with flowers that have been placed on the leaves of the castor oil plant…**

### Text 35

*āsurika kāle pūje pīṭhe bhūme basi*  
*snapana samaye mūrti vāma haste sparśi*

**Перевод:**

**…one should not perform pūjā during an inauspicious period (asura–kāla); one should not perform worship while sitting directly on the floor (without a seat); one should not touch the Deity with the left hand at the time of bathing Him…**

### Text 36

*vāsi vā yācita phule devatā arcana*  
*pūjā kāle garva ukti ayathā niṣṭhīvana*

**Перевод:**

**…one should not worship the Deity with old flowers or flowers gained by begging; one should not show arrogance at the time of pūja, nor spit…**

### Text 37

*tiryak puṇḍra dhare āra adhauta caraṇe*  
*mandire praveśa kare pūjāra kāraṇe*

**Перевод:**

**…one should not wear tripuṇḍra (three horizontal lines on the forehead), nor enter the temple to do pūjā with unwashed feet…**

### Text 38

*avaiṣṇava pakva kare deve nivedana*  
*avaiṣṇave dekhāiyā karaye pūjana*

**Перевод:**

**…one should not offer foodstuff cooked by non-Vaiṣṇavas to the Deity, nor perform pūjā in front of non-Vaiṣṇavas… (6)**

**Комментарий:**

(6) Cooked grains prepared by a pure Vaiṣṇava can be offered to Kṛṣṇa. Non-Vaiṣṇavas should not be present at the time of kṛṣṇa-pūjā.

### Text 39

*viśvaksene nā pūjiyā kāpāli dekhiyā*  
*hari pūje nakha jale śrī mūrti smariyā*

**Перевод:**

**…one should not offer pūjā without first worshipping Viśvaksena; one should not offer worship after seeing a Kāpāli (a tantrika mendicant); one should not perform pūjā of Hari by bathing the Deity with water touched with one’s fingernails…**

### Text 40

*gharmāmbu-saṁspṛṣṭa jale karaye arcana*  
*kṛṣṇera śapatha kare nirmālya laṅghana*

**Перевод:**

**…one should not engage in worship while profusely sweating; one should not make an oath in the name of Kṛṣṇa, nor step over nirmalya (flower remnants of the Deity).**

### Text 41

*ei saba kāryye haya sevā aparādha*  
*sevākārī janera yāhāte bhakti bādha*

**Перевод:**

**…all these are sevāparādhas which obstruct bhakti for those persons engaged in service.**

**Sevāparādha yāhāra pakṣe yāhā, tāhā tini varjjana karibena (One Should Give Up these Offences in Sevā)**

### Text 42

*śrī-mūrti sambandhe yāra bhajana pūjana*  
*sevā aparādha teṅha karuna varjjana*

**Перевод:**

**“In relation to bhajana and worship of the Deity, one should give up these offences in sevā.**

### Text 43

*vaiṣṇava sarvadā nāma sevā aparādha*  
*varjiyā śrī-kṛṣṇa-sevā karuna āsvāda*

**Перевод:**

**“By giving up all offences to the Name and sevāparādha, a Vaiṣṇava relishes serving Śrī Kṛṣṇa.**

### Text 44

*ei saba aparādha madhye yāṅra yāhā*  
*sambandhe paḍibe tāṅra varjjanīya tāhā*

**Перевод:**

**“If one commits any amongst these offences, one should give them up.**

**Nāmāparādha sakala vaiṣṇava-mātrerai varjjanīya (All Vaiṣṇavas Reject Nāmāparādha)**

### Text 45

*kintu nāma aparādha sakala vaiṣṇava*  
*sarva kāla tyaji labhe bhaktira vaibhava*

**Перевод:**

**“However, in order to attain the opulence of bhakti, all Vaiṣṇavas avoid nāmāparādha at all times. (7)**

**Комментарий:**

(7) Vaiṣṇavas must give up the ten offences to the Name. Whenever there is a chance for a sevāparādha to be committed, it must be avoided. It is essential to understand that the avoidance of such offences is the main duty of a Vaiṣṇava.

**Bhāva-sevāya sevāparādha vicāra svalpa (At the Stage of Bhāva-sevā, Sevāparādha is Negligable)**

### Text 46

*śrī-mūrti virahe yini nirjjanete basi*  
*bhajana-kāraṇe bhāva mārge aharniśi*

**Перевод:**

**“One who is not engaged in the worship of the Deity, who resides in a secluded place, performing bhajana, is situated on the path of bhāva, day and night.**

### Text 47

*nāma aparādha sadā varjjanīya tāṅra*  
*nāma aparādha daśa sarva kleśādhāra*

**Перевод:**

**“He constantly avoids nāmāparādha because those ten offences to the Name are the repository of all miseries.**

### Text 48

*nāma aparādha gate bhāva-sevā haya*  
*ataeva aparādha tāhe nāhi raya*

**Перевод:**

**“When nāmāparādha has vanished, bhāva–sevā emerges. Thus, offences do not occur at that point on. (8)**

**Комментарий:**

(8) On the path of bhāva, mānasa–sevā (service in a transcendentally purified mind) is prevalent. In this state there is no sevapārādha. In relation to the service of govardhana–śilā, Mahāprabhu told Śrī Raghunātha Dāsa Gosvāmī:

*prabhu kahe, — “ei śilā kṛṣṇera vigraha*  
*iṅhāra sevā kara tumi kariyā āgraha*

*ei śilāra kara tumi sāttvika pūjana*  
*acirāt pābe tumi kṛṣṇa-prema-dhana*

*eka kuṅjā jala āra tulasī-mañjarī*  
*sāttvika-sevā ei — śuddha-bhāve kari*

*dui-dike dui-patra madhye komala mañjarī*  
*ei-mata aṣṭa-mañjarī dibe śraddhā kari’*

*śrī-haste śilā diyā ei ājñā dilā*  
*ānande raghunātha sevā karite lāgilā*

*eka-vitasti dui-vastra, piṅḍā eka-khāni*  
*svarūpa dilena kuṅjā ānibāre pāni*

*ei-mata raghunātha karena pūjana*  
*pūjā-kāle dekhe śilāya ‘vrajendra-nandana’*

**(“The Lord said – ‘This stone is the form of Kṛṣṇa. You should render service to Him with enthusiasm. You should perform sāttvika–pūjā (worship in the mode of goodness) to this stone and very soon you will attain the wealth of kṛṣṇa–prema. In order to offer sattvika–sevā in a mood of pure bhāva, you only need one jug of water and some tulasī-mañjarīs. With śraddhā, you should offer eight soft tulasī–mañjarīs, each with two leaves on either side.’ Upon giving these instructions, the Lord gave the stone with His divine hand and Raghunātha began to offer service to the stone in a blissful mood. Svarūpa Dāmodara gave him two cloths of six inches, a wooden platform and a water jug. In this way, Raghunātha offered pūjā, and while worshipping, he saw the stone as Vrajendra-nandana.” – Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Antya–līlā 6.294-300)**

*jala-tulasīra sevāya tāṅra yata sukhodaya*  
*ṣoḍaśopacāra-pūjāya tata sukha naya*  
*tabe svarūpa-gosāñi tāṅre kahilā vacana*  
*aṣṭa-kauḍira khājā-sandeśa kara samarpaṇa*

**(“The happiness that he experienced in offering service with water and tulasī was beyond comparison, more than one could attain by offering pūjā with ṣoḍaśopacāra \[sixteen articles of worship\]. After he had offered worship for some days, Svarūpa Gosvāmī spoke to him as follows – ‘You should offer eight kauḍis worth of khājā and sandeśa.’ – Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Antya–līlā 6.302-304)**

**Nāma smaraṇa kārīdera bhāva-sevāi kartavya (One Who is Engaged in Meditation on the Name Should Perform Bhāva-sevā)**

### Text 49

*śrī nāma smaraṇe bhāva sevāra udaya*  
*tomāra kṛpāya prabhu jīve bhāgyodaya*

**Перевод:**

**“When one meditates on the Name (nāma–smaraṇa), bhāva–sevā manifests. O Lord, Your mercy makes this good fortune arise within the jīvas.**

### Text 50

*bhaktira sādhana yata āchaya prakāra*  
*se saba carame deya nāme prema sāra*

**Перевод:**

**“The various aspects of sādhana–bhakti all essentially culminate in nāma-prema.**

### Text 51

*ataeva nāma laya nāma rase maje*  
*anya ye prakāra saba tāhā nāhi bhaje*

**Перевод:**

**“Therefore, one should chant the Name and immerse oneself in nāma–rasa. One should not take up other types of bhajana.”**

### Text 52

*hari-dāsa ājñā bale akiñcana jana*  
*hari-nāma-cintāmaṇi karilā kīrtana*

**Перевод:**

**By the power of Hari Dāsa’s teachings, this beggar performs kīrtana of Hari-nāma Cintāmaṇi.**

## Chapter 15. Bhajana-praṇālī (The Method of Bhajana)

### Text 1

*gadāi gaurāṅga jaya jaya nityānanda*  
*jaya sītānātha jaya gaura-bhakta-vṛnda*

**Перевод:**

**Glories to Gadāi-Gaurāṅga! Glories to Nityānanda! Glories to the Lord of Sītā (Advaita) and the devotees of Gaura.**

### Text 2

*saba chāḍi hari-nāma je kare bhajana*  
*jaya jaya bhāgyavān sei mahājana*

**Перевод:**

**Glories, glories, to those most fortunate mahājanas that give everything up to perform bhajana of hari-nāma.**

### Text 3

*prabhu bale hari-dāsa tumi bhakti-bale*  
*peyecha sakala jñāna e jagatī-tale*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord said, ‘O Hari Dāsa, by the power of your bhakti you have attained all the knowledge of the world.**

### Text 4

*sarva-veda nāce dekhi tomāra jihvāya*  
*sakala siddhānta dekhi tomāra kathāya*

**Перевод:**

**“I see all the Vedas dancing upon your tongue, and I see how your words are all full of siddhānta. (1)**

**Комментарий:**

(1) “The correct Vedic siddhānta concerning bhagavat-tattva, jīva-tattva, māyā-tattva, nāma-tattva, nāmābhāsa-tattva, nāmaparādha-tattva etc. are found in your words. Therefore, all the Vedas blissfully dance upon your tongue.” Through Hari Dāsa, Mahāprabhu revealed nāma-rasa-tattva and all these other topics. Rasa is the highest attainment of nāma–tattva.

**Nāma-rasa-jijñāsā (An Inquiry about Nāma-rasa)**

### Text 5

*ebe spaṣṭa bala nāma-rasa ki prakāra*  
*ki rūpe labhibe jīva tāhe adhikāra*

**Перевод:**

**“Please clearly describe nāma–rasa and how the jīva can become eligible to experience it.”**

### Text 6

*hari-dāsa mahā-preme kare nivedana*  
*tomāra preraṇā-bale kariba varṇana*

**Перевод:**

**Feeling great prema, Hari Dāsa made this submission, “On the strength of Your order, I will describe it.**

**Rasa-tattva (The Science of Rasa)**

### Text 7

*śuddha-tattva para-tattva yei vastu siddha*  
*rasa nāme sarva-vede tāhāi prasiddha*

**Перевод:**

**“All the Vedas glorify that which is called ‘rasa’ to be of the same spiritual substance as the para-tattva (Supreme Reality), who is the purest truth. (2)**

**Комментарий:**

(2) The rasa described in ordinary books of rhetoric (alaṅkāra) is mundane. In reality, it is not rasa, but a distortion of rasa. That which exists beyond the twenty-four elements of prakṛti is śuddha–sattva–tattva (transcendental reality), and that is true rasa. Those who are ātmārāma (who find satisfaction in the self) transcend the limits of prakṛti, yet they cannot see the wonderful variety that exists within śuddha–sattva–tattva, therefore they are nirasa (bereft of rasa). Spiritual variety is found within śuddha–sattva—that is eternal rasa.

### Text 8

*sei se akhaṇḍa rasa para-brahma tattva*  
*ananta ānanda-dhāma carama mahattva*

**Перевод:**

**“Parabrahma is undivided rasa. He is the abode of unlimited bliss and His glories are the greatest.**

### Text 9

*śakti śaktimān rūpe viśeṣa tāhāya*  
*bheda nāi bheda-sama darśanete bhāya*

**Перевод:**

**“He manifests as the śakti (potency) and the śaktimān (the possessor of potencies). Although these are non-different, they appear to be different due to viśeṣa (3)**

**Комментарий:**

(3) He (Hari Dāsa) explains the process of rasa thus—Parabrahma (the Supreme) is situated in śuddha-sattva, and that indivisible substance naturally appears in the specific forms of śakti and śaktimān. Śaktimān–tattva is difficult to comprehend. In truth, there is no substantial difference between śakti and śaktimān. Various distinctions are perceived due to viśeṣa (an apparent difference between objects). Śaktimān eternally appears as the independent Puruṣa, and His śakti manifests His powers. This śakti reveals itself as the threefold phenomena of cit (spiritual potency), jīva (the living beings) and māyā (the illusory potency).

### Text 10

*śaktimān sudurlakṣya śakti prakāśinī*  
*trividha śaktira kriyā viśva-vikāśinī*

**Перевод:**

**“That incomprehensible śaktimān is revealed by the śakti, and using the threefold śaktis, He creates the universe.**

**Cic-chakti-dvārā vastu-prakāśa (The Cit-śakti Reveals the Supreme Substance)**

### Text 11

*cic-chakti-svarūpe prakāśaye vastu rūpa*  
*vastu nāma vastu dhāma tat-kriyā svarūpa*

**Перевод:**

**“The cit–śakti reveals the Name, dhāma, activities and form of the Supreme substance.**

### Text 12

*kṛṣṇa se parama vastu śyāma tāra rūpa*  
*kṛṣṇa-dhāma golokādi līlāra svarūpa*

**Перевод:**

**“Kṛṣṇa is the parama–vastu (Supreme substance) and His form is dark. Goloka and other abodes are Kṛṣṇa’s dhāmas where He exhibits His līlā.**

### Text 13

*nāma dhāma rūpa guṇa līlā ādi yata*  
*sakalai akhaṇḍādvaya-jñāna antargata*

**Перевод:**

**“His Names, dhāma, forms, qualities, līlā etc. all belong to the category of akhaṇḍādvāya–jñāna (indivisible non-dual knowledge).**

### Text 14

*vicitratā yata saba parā-śakti karma*  
*kṛṣṇa dharmī parā-śakti kṛṣṇa-nitya-karma*

**Перевод:**

**“All varieties are due to the parā-śakti. Kṛṣṇa is the natural proprietor (dharmī) of the parā–śakti and it is Kṛṣṇa’s eternal activity.**

### Text 15

*dharma-dharmī bheda nāi akhaṇḍa advaye*  
*vicitra viśeṣa mātra sac-cin-nilaye*

**Перевод:**

**“The dharma (property) and the dharmī (proprietor) are non-different, indivisible and non-dual. In the abode of eternity and cognisance, variety is only due to viśeṣa. (4)**

**Комментарий:**
